63-amuttimaraṇaṃjhāṇe [amuktimaraṇadhyāne]
bhuva0 mocanaṃ muktiḥ saṃsārācchivagatirūpā na muktiramuktistasya maraṇamamuktimaraṇaṃ tasya dhyānaṃ tasmin| yathā muktivighnakarametannidānaṃ mā kuru | puna: punaścitreṇa nivāryamāṇasyāpi etāṃ vibhūtimananubhūya muktimapi na spṛhayeyamityādyaśubhabhāvena nidānaṃ kurvataḥ saṃbhūtasyeva yathā vā loke vaṃśārjunasyoktiḥ |
vaṃśalakṣmīmanuddhṛtya samucchedena vidviṣām |
nirvāṇamapi manyehamantarāyaṃ jayaśriyaḥ || iti [kirāta-11-69] ||63|| kvacitpāṭhe rūvaṃjhāṇe ityadhikamapi padaṃ dṛśyate | tatra rupyate nirīkṣyate ādarśādiṣviti rūpaṃ tasya dhyānaṃ tasmin tacca dvidhā svarūpadhyānaṃ pararūpadhyānaṃ vā | tatra madīyarūpaṃ śobhanamiti devābhyāṃ praśaṃsitasya sanatkumārasyeva | pararūpadhyānaṃ tu gopadārakāṇāmivābhīrakanyārūpātiśayātkṣiptacetasāṃ tacchakaṭanikaṭautpathena śakaṭakheṭanena bhagnanijaśakaṭānām | caṇḍapradyotasya vā aṅgāravatyādirūpāṇi citraphalakalikhitāni vīkṣamāṇasya pararūpadhyānaṃ bhavati | tadetāni triṣaṣṭhānyapi dhyānānyātaraudrasvabhāvānīti mithyāduṣkṛtaviṣayāṇi |
soma0 amuttimaraṇaṃ jhāṇe - muktirmokṣagatirna muktiramuktiḥ saṃsārasukhābhilāṣa ityarthaḥ, tena maraṇaṃ tasya dhyānam, “muktivighnakaram etannidānaṃ mā kuru" iti punaḥ punaścitreṇa bhrātṛsādhunā nivāryamāṇasyāpi “etāṃ cakrivibhūtimananubhūya muktimapi na spṛhayeyam,” ityādyaśubhabhāvena nidānaṃ kurvataḥ saṃbhūtasyeva tasmin ||63|| ___ etāni triṣaṣṭirapi dhyānāni ārttaraudrasvabhāvāni mithyāduṣkṛtaviṣayāṇi | anusvārāśca sarvadhyānapadeṣu alākṣaṇikā jñeyāstallopaśca kāryaḥ |
guṇa. muktiḥ saṃsārācchivagatirūpā | na muktiramuktistasyā maraṇamamuktimaraṇaṃ tasya dhyānaṃ yathā muktivighnakarametannidānaṃ mā kuru [iti] punaḥ punarnivāryamāṇasyāpi etāṃ vibhūtima[na]nubhūya muktimapi na spṛhayeyamityādyaśubhabhāvena nidānaṃ kurvataḥ sambhūtasyeva ||63||
pasuttassa vā paḍibuddhassa vā jo me koī devasio rāio uttamaḍhe aikkamo vaikkamo aiyāro aṇāyāro tassa micchā mi dukkaḍaṃ ||11||
prasuptasya vā pratibuddhasya vā yo mayā kaścidaivasiko rātrika uttamārthe'tikramo vyatikramo'ticāro'nācārastasya mithyā me duṣkṛtam ||11||

bhuva0 atha kasyāmavasthāyāṃ jātānītyāha - pasuttassa vā tti prasuptasya nidrāṃ gatasya paḍibuddhassa vā pratibuddhasya jāgrataḥ | jo me kao devasio rāio yo me mayā ko'pi bahurvālpo vā divase jāto daivasiko rātrau bhavo rātrikaḥ | uttamaḍhe tti uttamārthe jñānādau aikkamo tti atikramaṇamatikrama ādhākarmādyāmantraṇādau pratiśravaṇarūpaḥ | vaikkamo tti viśeṣeṇātikramaṇaṃ vyatikrama: tasyaiva grahaṇāya padabhedarūpaḥ | aiyāro tti atīcaraṇamatīcāra ādhākarmādigrahaṇarūpaḥ | aṇāyāro tti ācaraṇamācārastatpariharaṇapariṣṭhāpanarūpo na ācāro'nācārastadbhakṣaṇamityarthaḥ | jāta iti padādhyāhāraḥ | tassa micchāmi dukkaḍaṃ ti tasya sarvasyā'nācārasya mithyā duṣkṛtaṃ bhavatu yanmayā duṣkṛtaṃ tanmithyā bhavatu | ayaṃ ca mithyāduṣkṛtavidhirbālapaṇḍitamaraṇe, paṇḍitamaraṇe, paṇḍitapaṇḍitamaraṇe ca sādhāraṇa eva|
soma0 atha kasyāmavasthāyāmetāni dhyānāni jātānītyāha- prasuptasya nidrāṃ gatasya, 'paḍibuddhassa vā' pratibuddhasya jāgrato vā yo me mama ko'pi 'baharvā'lpo vā' divase jāto daivasiko, rātrau bhavo rātrikaḥ 'uttamaṭṭe 'tti uttamārthe jñānādau | 'aikkamo' atikramaṇamatikrama ādhākarmādyāmantraṇādau pratiśravaṇarūpaḥ, 'vaikkamo' viśeṣeṇātikramaṇaṃ vyatikrama ādhākarmādargrahaṇāya padabhedarūpaḥ, 'aiyāro' aticāra ādhākarmādyāhāragrahaṇarūpaḥ, ācārastatpariharaṇa-pariṣṭhāpanarūpaḥ, 'aṇāyāro' anācārastadbhakṣaṇamityarthaḥ, jāta iti padādhyāhārastasya sarvasya prāguktasyātikramādemithyā duṣkṛtaṃ bhavatu | yanmayā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtaṃ tanmithyā bhavatvityarthaḥ | ayaṃ ca mithyāduṣkṛtavidhirbālapaṇḍitamaraṇe, paṇḍitamaraṇe, paṇḍitapaṇḍitamaraṇe ca sādhāraṇa eva ||11||
guṇa. kasyāmavasthāyāṃ jātānītyāha - prasuptasya nidrāṃ gatasya, pratibuddhasya jāgrato yo me mayā ko'pi bahurvālpo vā daivasiko rātrikaḥ | uttamārthe jñānādau atikrama ādhākarmādyāmantraṇādau pratiśravaṇarūpaḥ, vyatikramastasyaiva grahaṇāya padabhedarūpaḥ, aticāra ādhākarmādigrahaṇarūpaḥ, anācārastadbhakṣaṇamityarthaḥ | jāta iti padādhyāhāraḥ tasya sarvasyānācārasya mithyāduṣkṛtaṃ bhavatu ||11||
esa karemi paṇāmaṃ jiṇavaravasahassa vaddhamāṇassa | sesāṇaṃ ca jiṇāṇaṃ sagaṇaharāṇaṃ ca savvesi ||12|| eṣa karomi praṇāmaṃ jinavaravṛṣabhasya varddhamānasya |

jaṃ kiṃci vi duccariyaṃ taṃ savvaṃ vosirāmi tiviheṇaṃ | sāmāiyaṃ ca tivihaṃ karemi savvaṃ nirāgāraṃ ||19|| yat kiñcidapi duścaritaṃ tatsarvaṃ vyutsṛjāmi trividhena |

khaṇḍaḥ- 1 gā. 21, 22
soma0 punaryat karoti tadāha - bahirbhavaṃ bāhyaṃ pātrādyupakaraṇarūpam, abhyantaraṃ kaṣāyādilakṣaṇaṃ tathā śarīrādi ca saha bhojanena varttata iti sabhojanam | athavā upadhiṃ kimbhūtam ? śarīrādi, upadhīyate jīvo durgatau sthāpyate'nenā'yatnavyāpāritena ityupadhiḥ taṃ sarvamupadhiṃ manovākkāyairbhāvena bhāvato vyutsṛjāmi tyajāmi | sarvaśabdād vibhaktilopaḥ prākṛtatvāt ||20||
guṇa bāhyaṃ pātrādilakṣaṇam, abhyantaraṃ kaṣāyādilakṣaṇam | upadhiḥ parigrahaṃ upadhīyate poṣyate jīvo'nenetyupadhiḥ śarīrādi ca sabhojanaṃ manovākkāyaiḥ sarvaṃ bhāvato vyutsṛjāmi || 20 ||
savvaṃ pāṇāraṃbhaṃ paccakkhāmi tti aliyavayaṇaṃ ca | savvamadinnādāṇaṃ mehuṇṇa pariggahaṃ ceva || 21 || sammaṃ me savvabhūesa veraṃ majjha na keṇaī | āsāo vosirittāṇaṃ samāhimaṇupālae || 22 || sarvaṃ prāṇārambhaṃ pratyākhyāmyalīkavacanaṃ ca | sarvamadattādānaṃ maithunaṃ parigraha caiva || 21 | |
sāmyaṃ me sarvabhūteṣu vairaṃ mama na kenacit | āśā vyutsṛjya samādhimanupālaye || 22 ||
bhuva0 ito'pi kiṃ karotītyāha - nanvidaṃ gāthādvayamagre'pyuktaṃ kimiti punaruktamuktam ? ucyate pūrvaṃ sākārapratyākhyānakarttāramāśrityoktamiha tu saṃstārakapratipattāramā - śrityoktamiti na paunaruktyam | tarhi " esa karemi " iti gāthā kimiti noktā ? ucyateetasyā artho namotthu dhuyapāvāṇamityanayokta iti noktā | gāthādvayavyākhyānaṃ pūrvavadeva | samādhiṃ prapadye || 21-22 ||

-
-
soma0 punaḥ kiṃ karotītyāha - idaṃ gāthādvayaṃ prāg vyākhyātaṃ tathaiva jñeyam | nanu punaretat kasmāduktam ? ucyate - pūrvaṃ sākārapratyākhyānakarttāramāśrityoktam, iha tu saṃstārakānaśanapratipattāramāśrityoktamiti na paunarūktyam | tarhi “esa karemi paṇāma" iti gāthā'pi kimiti noktā ? ucyate etasyā artho “namutthu dhuapāvāṇaṃ" itigāthayā ukta eveti noktā || 21 || 22 || guṇa gāthādvayavyākhyānaṃ tu pūrvavadeva | samādhiṃ prapadye || 21-22 ||

rāgaṃ baṃdhaṃ paosaṃ ca harisaṃ dīṇabhāvayaṃ | ussugattaṃ bhayaṃ sogaṃ raiṃ. araiṃ ca vosire ||23|| rāgaṃ bandhaṃ pradveṣaṃ ca harṣa dīnabhāvatām |
utsukatvaṃ bhayaṃ śokaṃ ratimaratiṃ ca vyutsṛjāmi ||23|| bhaka pratipannānaśanaścānyadapi kimabhidhatta ityāha - rāgamabhiṣvaṅgaṃ, bandhahetutvādvandhaṃ rāgalakṣaṇaṃ bandhamityarthaḥ | bandhaśabdaḥ pratipadaṃ yojya: | pradveṣaṇaṃ pradveṣastaṃ ca bandhaṃ matsaramityarthaḥ | harṣaṇaṃ harṣaḥ svajanamelāpakādau saṅghamelāpakādau vā mama mahatī pūjā bhaviṣyatīti | dīnabhāvasya bhāvo dīnabhāvatā, tāṃ pratyanīkāpamānādau | utsukasya bhāvaḥ utsukatvaṃ pīḍotpattau jhaṭityeva yadi mriye'hamityevaṃrūpam | bhayaṃ devādyupasarge sati, śokamiṣṭaviyogādau, ratiṃ tathāvidhānandarūpāṃ manojñaśabdādau, aratiṃ ca nokaṣāyamohanīyodayajacittavikārodvegalakṣaṇaṃ parīṣahopasargādau vyutsṛjāmītyarthaḥ ||23||
soma0 atha pratipannānaśanaḥ kimabhidhatta ityāha - rāgaṃ abhiṣvaṅgaṃ kimbhūtam ? bandhahetutvād bandhaṃ tathā prakarṣeṇa dveṣaḥ pradveṣo matsara ityarthaḥ taṃ ca kimbhūtam ? bandhaṃ bandhahetuṃ, bandhaśabdaḥ padadvaye'pi yojyate rāga-dveṣayordvayorapi bandhanatvāt, tathā praharṣaṇaṃ praharṣaḥ svajanamelāpakādau saṅghamelāpakādau vā mama mahatī pūjā bhaviṣyatīti pramodaḥ, dīno bhāvo mānasādhyavasāyo yasyāsau dīnabhāvo dīnabhāvasya bhāvo dīnabhāvatā tāṃ pratyanīkāpamānādau dainyamityarthaḥ | utsukasya bhāva utsukatvam, pīḍotpattau śīghrameva yadi mriye'haṃ tadā varamityevaṃrūpaṃ, bhayaṃ devādyupasarge sati, śokamiṣṭaviyogādau, ratiṃ mano'bhimatakṣetrasthānādiviṣayām, aratiṃ cānabhimatakṣetrādiviṣayāṃ cittodvegalakṣaṇāṃ vyutsṛjāmi tyajāmītyarthaḥ ||23||
guṇa rāgamabhiṣvaṅgaṃ bandhahetutvād bandhaṃ, rāgalakṣaṇaṃ bandhamityarthaḥ | bandhaśabdaḥ pratipadaṃ yojyaḥ | pradveṣaṃ matsaraṃ, harṣaḥ svajanamelāpakādau me mahatī pūjā bhāvinī, dīnabhāvatā tāṃ pratyanīkāpamānādau | utsukatvaṃ pīḍotpattau jhaṭityeva yadi mriye'hamityevaṃrūpaṃ, bhayaṃ devādhupasarge sati, śokamiṣṭaviyogādau, ratiṃ manojñarājyādau, aratiṃ ca cittodvegalakṣaṇāṃ vyutsṛjāmi ||23||

khaṇḍa:-1 gā. 24, 25
mamattaṃ parivajjāmi nimmamattaṃ uvaṭṭhio | ālaṃbaṇaṃ ca me āyā, avasesaṃ ca vosire ||24 || mamatvaṃ parivarjayāmi nirmamatvamupasthitaḥ |
ālambanaṃ ca me ātmā avaśeṣaṃ ca vyutsṛjāmi ||24|| bhuva tathā - mamaitadityevaṃrūpo bhāvo mamattvaṃ pratibandhaṃ sarvasāravastuṣviti | jñaparijñayā parijānāmi, pratyākhyānaparijñayā pariharāmi | kimbhūtaḥ san nirmamatvamupasthita āśritaḥ | tahi kimālambanatayā cintayatītyāha - ālambanaṃ ca āśrayo me mama ātmaivārādhanāheturavaśeṣaṃ ca śarīropadhyādi vyutsṛjāmītyarthaḥ ||24||
soma0 tathā mamaitadityevaṃrūpo bhāvo mamatvaṃ pratibandhaṃ sarvamano'bhimatavastuṣviti śeṣaḥ parijānāmi jñaparijñayā jñātvā pratyākhyānaparijñayā pariharāmītyarthaḥ | ahaṃ kimbhūtassan ? nirmamatvaṃ nissaṅgatvamupasthita āśrita ityarthaḥ | tarhi kimālambanatayā cintayatītyāha-ālambanaṃ cāśrayo'vaṣṭambha ādhāra ityarthaḥ, me mamātmaivārāghanā-heturayamavaśeṣamaparaṃ śarīropadhyādi sarvaṃ vyutsṛjāmi ||24||
gaṇa. mamatvaṃ pratibandhaṃ sāravastuṣviti jñaparijñayā parijānāmi pratyākhyānaparijñayā pariharāmi | nirmamatvamupasthita āśritaḥ | ālambanaṃ ca āśrayo mama ātmaivārādhanāheturavaśeṣa ca śarīropadhyādi vyutsṛjāmi ||24||
āyā hu mahaṃ nāṇe, āyā me daṃsaṇe caritte ya | āyā paccakkhāṇe, āyā me saṃjame joge ||25|| ātmaiva mama jñāne ātmā me darśane cāritre ca |

soma0 atha keṣu kāryeṣvayamātmā''lambanaṃ vidheya ityāha - atati satataṃ gacchati tāsu tāsu yoniṣu iti ātmā, sa mama jñāne jñānaviṣaye ālambanaṃ sahāya ityarthaḥ, 'hu' sphuṭaṃ bhavatu iti śeṣaḥ, ātmā me darśane samyaktve cāritre'pi cālambanaṃ tathā pratyākhyāne bhaktaparijñārūpe saṃyame ca sarvaviratyaṅgīkārarūpe yoge ca praśastamanovākkāyavyāpārarūpe mama ātmaivālambanam | yata etat sāhāyyasādhyāni jñānadarśanacāritrāṇi tāni cātmanaḥ kadācidapi na bhinnāni | ataḥ prāga gāthāyāṃ 'ālaṃbaṇaṃ ca me āyā' ityatra ātmopādānena tānyapyupāttāni dṛṣṭavyāni ||25 ||
guṇa ātmā mama jñānaviṣaye ālaṃbanaṃ 'hu' sphuṭaṃ bhavatu | darśane cāritre'pi cātmaiva tathā pratyākhyāne'pi bhaktaparijñārūpe saṃyame sarvaviratirūpe praśastayogatrayarūpe mamātmaivālambanam | yata etatsādhyāni jñānadarśanacāritrāṇi tāni cātmanaḥ kadācinna bhinnāni ātmopādāne tānyapyupāttāni ||25||
ego vaccai jīvo, ego cevuvavajjae | egassa ceva maraṇaṃ, ego sijjhai nīrao ||26|| eko vrajati jīvaḥ ekaścaivotpadyate |
ekasya caiva maraṇam ekaḥ sidhyati nīrajaskaḥ ||26|| bhava evaṃ ca parityaktasamastabāhyābhyantaropadhika ekatvabhāvanāṃ bhāvayannidamāha - ekaḥ svajanadhanādivirahito vrajati jīvo bhavāntaram | eka eva ca utpadyate janmatayā | ekasyaiva maraṇaṃ bhavati | eka eva ca niṣkarmā karmarajorahita: siddhyati jīvaḥ ||26||
soma0 atha nirmamatvāya ekatvabhāvanāṃ bhāvayati - ekaḥ svajanadhanādirahito vrajati jīvo bhavāntaramiti śeṣaḥ, eka eva copapadyate manuṣyatvādirūpatayā; ekasyaiva maraṇaṃ bhavati, eka eva ca karmarajorahitaḥ san sidhyati jīvaḥ | bhavāntaragamanasya maraṇasya caikārthatve'pi pṛthagupādānamekatvabhāvanotkarṣapoṣārthaṃ nānādeśajavineyānāṃ vyaktyārthapratipādanārthaṃ vā ||26 | |

āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakam gā. 41
saṃyatabhave tad yogād-devā apyabhiyogā mahardhikasurāṇāṃ preṣyakarmakāriṇa evaṃvidhā devadurgatiḥ | 4āsuriyatti asurāṇāṃ karmaṇā caṇḍakopena carantīti āsurikāḥ saṃyatabhave kṛtacaṇḍakopā asuratvenotpannāstathāvidhā devā api āsurikā evaṃrūpā devadurgatiḥ | 5-saṃmoha tti sammohayantyunmārgadarśanādinā mokṣamārgād bhraṃśayanti jīvān ye te sammohāḥ saṃyatā apyevaṃvidhā devatvenotpannāḥ sammohā evaṃrūpā devadurgatiḥ | tā etā devadurgatayo maraṇe'padhyānādinā virādhite sati bhavanti, tataścyutāścānantaṃ saṃsāraṃ paribhramanti ||40|||
guṇaḥ atha praśnasya nirvacanamāha - kandarpo'ṭṭahāsahasanaṃ kandarpakaraṇaśīlāḥ kandarpāśca te devāśca kandarpadevāḥ | kilbiṣaṃ pāpaṃ jñānādyā''śātanādikaṃ, tadyogāddevā api kilbiṣikāḥ | abhiyojanamabhiyogaḥ, tadyogāddevā api abhiyogāḥ | abhiyogarūpā durgatirityarthaḥ | asurāṇāṃ bhāvaḥ karma vā'sureṇa caṇḍakopena carantyāsurikā devā api | sammohayantyunmārgadarśanādinā mārgānmokṣamārgād vidhvaṃsayanti ye te sammohāḥ tā etā devadurgatayo maraṇe virādhite'padhyānādinā bhavanti ||40||
micchādasaṇarattā saniyāṇā kiṇhalesamogāḍhā | iha je maraṃti jīvā tesiṃ dulahā bhave bohī ||41|| mithyādarśanaraktāḥ sanidānāḥ kṛṣṇaleśyāvagāḍhāḥ |
iha ye mriyante jīvāsteṣāṃ durlabho bhavedbodhiḥ ||41|| bhuva0 kā devaduggai ityasya praśnasya nirvacanamuktamatha kā abohītyasya savipakṣasya nirvacanamāha-mithyādarśanaṃ viparyastadarśanaṃ mithyātvaṃ tatra ratāḥ | yata ārṣam - "kahannaṃ bhaṃte samaṇā niggaṃthā mohaṇijjā kammaṃ veyaṃti ? goyamā ! tehiṃ tehiṃ nāṇaṃtarehiṃ daṃsaṇaṃtarehiṃ carittaṃtarehiṃ kappaṃtarehiṃ maggaṃtarehiṃ nayaṃtarehiṃ bhaṃgaṃtarehiṃ pāvayaṇaṃtarehiṃ samaṇā niggaṃthā kaṃṣā mohaṇijjaṃ kammaṃ veyaṃti |" tathā saha nidānena devatvādiprārthanārūpeṇa varttanta iti sanidānāḥ | tathā kṛṣṇāṃ sarvanikṛṣṭāṃ leśyāṃ jīvapariṇāmarūpāmavagāḍhāḥ prāptāḥ ihāsmin [jagati] ye mriyante jīvāsteṣāṃ durlabhā bodhirityarthaḥ || kiñca -

khaṇḍa:-1 gā. 42
67
jiṇasāsaṇaṃ pi pāviya saṃpāviya sādhusāvagattevi |
kugurukuvaeseṇa hao aṇaṃtakhutto duhaṃ patto ||1||41|| soma0 atha kā abodhiriti dvitīyapraśnasyottaramāha - mithyādarśanaṃ viparyastadarśanaṃ mithyātvaṃ mithyākriyādyabhilāṣarūpaṃ, tatra ratāḥ, tathā saha nidānena devatvādiprārthanārūpeṇa vartanta iti sanidānāḥ tathā kṛṣṇāṃ sarvādhamāṃ leśyāṃ jīvapariṇāmarūpāmavagāḍhāḥ prāptāḥ | ihāsmin jagati evaṃvidhā ye jīvā mriyante, teṣāṃ durlabhā bodhirbhavet ||41 ||
guṇa0 [kā] devadurgatiḥ iti asya praśnasya nirvacanamuktamatha kā abohī ? tasya nirvacanamāhaviparyastadarśanaṃ mithyātvaṃ tatra ratāḥ | tathā saha nidānena devatvādiprārthanārūpeṇa varttanta iti sanidānāḥ | tathā kṛṣṇāṃ sarvanikṛṣṭāṃ leśyāṃ jīvapariṇāmarūpāmavagāḍhāḥ prāptāḥ ||41 |
sammaiṃsaṇarattā aniyāṇā sukkalesamogāḍhā | iha je maraṃti jīvā tesiṃ sulahā bhave bohī ||42|| samyagdarśanaraktā anidānāḥ śukkaleśyāmavagāḍhāḥ |
iha ye mriyante jīvāsteṣāṃ sulabho bhavedvodhiḥ ||42| bhuva0 kā abohītyasyottaramuktamatha tadvipakṣabhūtaṃ prastāvādvodhisulabhatvamāha - samyagdarśane raktā bhāvitāntaḥkaraṇāstadvāsanayā saptānāmapi dhātūnāṃ rañjanāt | anidānā nidānavarjitāḥ | tathā śodhayatyaṣṭaprakāraṃ karmamalamiti śuklā | śucaṃ vā klamayatīti śuklā | sā cāsau leśyā ca śuklaleśyā, tāmavagāḍhāḥ prāptāḥ | iha pravacane ye mriyante jīvāsteṣāṃ sulabhabodhirityarthaḥ ||42||
soma0 atha prastāvād bodhidurlabhatvavipakṣabhūtaṃ bodhisulabhatvamapyāha - samyagdarśanena raktā bhāvitāntaḥkaraṇāḥ samyaktvavāsitasaptadhātava ityarthaḥ | anidānā nidānavarjitāḥ, tathā śodhayatyaṣṭaprakāraṃ karmamalamiti śuklā śucaṃ klamayatīti vā, śuklā cāsau leśyā ca śuklaleśyā tāmavagāḍhāḥ prāptāḥ, evaṃvidhā iha pravacane ye jīvā mriyante teṣāṃ sulabho bhaved bodhiḥ ||42||
guṇa kā abohītyasyottaramuktamatha tadvipakṣabhūtaṃ bodhisulabhatvamāha - sammamityādi ||42||

āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakam gā. 43
je puṇa gurupaḍiṇīyā bahumohā sasabalā kusīlā ya | asamāhiṇā maraṃti u te haṃti aṇaṃtasaṃsārī ||43|| ye punargurupratyanīkā bahumohāḥ saśabalāḥ kuśīlāśca |
asamādhinā niyante te bhavantyanantasaṃsāriṇaḥ ||43|| bhuvaḥ atha keṇa aṇaṃtaṃ pāramiti praśnasya nirvacanamāha - gṛṇātyabhidhatte tattvamiti gurustaṃ pratītyāśritya pratyanīkā gurupratyanīkā gurvāśātakā gośālakavat| tathāhi -
jaccāīhiṃ avannaṃ bhāsai vaṭṭai nayā vi uvavāe |
ahio chiddappehī pagāsavāī aṇaṇukūlā khisakā || [paṃca va0 1639] ityarthaḥ|| tathā bahumohā tti triṃśanmohanīyasthānavartinaḥ bahumohāḥ, tathā saha sabalairekaviṃśatyā śabalasthānairvartanta iti saśabalāḥ kutsitaṃ śīlaṃ samācāraṃ yeṣāṃ te kuśīlāḥ | caḥ samuccaye | asamādhinā ārttaraudratayā ye mriyante te'nantasaṃsāriṇo bhavantīti | keṇa aṇaṃtaṃpāramityasya nirvacanamuktam | nanu kimiti tṛtīyapraśnaṃ vimucya caturthasya prathamaṃ nirṇaya uktaḥ ? ucyate'maraṇavirāhie' iti gāthāyāmayameva kramastasyaiva nirvacanamabhāṇi | tarhi praśnasaṃgrahagāthāyāṃ kimiti vyatikramagrahaṇam ? ucyate-śikṣābhyūhya śaktivarddhanārthaṃ vicitrā sūtraracaneti nyāyapradarśanārthaṃ vā iti paramārthaḥ ||43||
soma0 atha kenānantaṃ pāramiti caturthasya praśnasya nirvacanamāha - ye punarjīvā gṛṇāti abhidhatte tattvamiti gurustaṃ prati jātyādyavarṇavādabhāṣaṇādinā pratyanīkāḥ pratikūlā: tathā bahumohāstriṃśanmohanīyasthānavartinaḥ, saha śabalairekaviṃśatyā śabalasthānairvartante ye te saśabalāḥ, kutsitaṃ śīlamācāro yeṣāṃ te kuśīlāḥ, caḥ samuccaye | evaṃvidhā ye'samādhinā''taraudrabhāve vartamānā niyante te'nantasaṃsāriṇo bhavantīti | nanu 'keṇeva vujjhaī maraṇaṃ' ? iti tṛtīyaṃ praśnaṃ vimucya caturthapraśnasya prathamaṃ nirṇayaḥ kasmāduktaḥ ? ucyate-vicitrā siddhāntasūtraracanā bhavatīti nyāyadarśanārthaṃ śiṣyāṇām ||43|||
guṇa atha keṇa aṇaṃtaṃ pāramiti praśnasya nirvacanamāha - gṛṇāti tattvamiti gurustaṃ pratītyāśritya pratyanīkā gurvāśātanāḥ tathā bahu0 triṃśanmohanīyasthānavartinaḥ bahumohāḥ, saha śabalairekaviṃśatyā śabalasthānairvartanta iti saśabalāḥ | kuśīlāḥ | caḥ samuccaye ||43||

khaṇḍa:-1 gā. 44, 45
jiṇavayaṇe aṇurattā guruvayaṇaṃ je karaṃti bhāveṇaṃ | asabala asaṃkilaṭṭhā te haṃti parittasaṃsārī ||44|| jinavacane'nuraktā guruvacanaṃ ye kurvanti bhāvena |
aśabalā asaṃkliṣṭāste bhavanti parīttasaṃsāriṇaḥ ||44 || bhuva0 athānantasaṃsārivipakṣabhūtāḥ parīttasaṃsāriṇaḥ tatsvarūpamityāha - jinavacanasyānuraktāstadvāsitamanasaḥ guruvacanaṃ dharmācāryopadeśaṃ bhāvato hitamiti kṛtvā ye kurvanti | aśabalāḥ śabalasthānadūravartinaḥ | asaṃkliṣṭā viśuddhādhyavasāyāste parīttasaṃsāriṇaḥ stokasaṃsāriṇo bhavantītyarthaḥ ||44||
soma0 atha anantasaṃsārivipakṣabhūtatvāt parīttasaṃsārisvarūpamāha - jinavacane śrījinendravacane śrījinapravacane'nuraktāstadvāsitamanaso guruvacanaṃ dharmācāryopadeśaṃ bhāvato hitamiti kṛtvā ye kurvanti, aśabalāḥ śabalasthānadūravartinaḥ, asaṃkliṣṭā viśuddhādhyavasāyā evaṃvidhā ye mriyanta iti gamyaṃ te parīttasaṃsāriṇaḥ stokasaṃsāriṇo bhavantītyarthaḥ ||44||
guṇa jinavacane anuraktāstadvāsitamanaso guruvacanaṃ dharmācāryopadeśam | aśabalāḥ śabalasthānadūravartinaḥ, asaṃkliṣṭāste parīttasaṃsāriṇaḥ stokasaṃsāriṇo bhavantītyarthaḥ ||44||
bālamaraṇāṇi bahuso bahuyāṇi akāmagāṇi maraṇāṇi | marihaṃti te varāyā je jiṇavayaṇaṃ na yāṇaṃti ||45|| bālamaraṇāni bahuśo bahukāni ca akāmāni maraṇāni |
niyante te varākā ye jinavacanaṃ na jānanti ||45|| bhuva. atha keṇeva vujhaī maraṇamityasya nirvacanaṃ sārddhagāthādvayenāha - ye varākā jinavacanaṃ na jānanti te bālamaraṇāni śastragrahaṇādīni bahuśo'nekaśo marihaṃti mariṣyanti | tathā bahūni bahukāni ca akāmukāni nirabhilāṣāṇi maraṇāni kecidvarākā mahārogādiduḥkhapīḍitā anicchanto'pi mariṣyanti | jinavacanabāhyāḥ santaḥ ||45||

āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakam gā. 46
ye varākā jinavacanaṃ na jānanti te bālamaraṇāni śastragrahaṇa-viṣabhakṣaṇādīni bahuśo'nekaśo 'marihaṃti' mariṣyantīti sambandhastathā bahukāni bahūnyevākāmukāni nirabhilāṣāṇi maraṇāni darśayati kecid varākā mahārogādigrastā mohānmartumanicchanto mariṣyanti jinavacanabāhyāḥ santa ityarthaḥ ||45||
gaṇa ye varākā jinavacanaṃ na jānanti te bālamaraṇāni śastragrahaṇādīni bahaśo mariṣyanti tathā bahūni bahukāni ca akāmukāni nirabhilāṣāni maraṇāni kecit varākā mahārogādiduḥkhapīḍitā anicchanto'pi mariṣyanti jinavacanabāhyāḥ santaḥ ||45||
[gā. 46-47 bālamaraṇam] satthaggahaṇaṃ visabhakkhaṇaṃ ca jalaṇaṃ ca jalapaveso ya | aṇayārabhaṃḍasevī jaṃmaṇa-maraṇāṇubaṃdhīṇi ||46|| śastragrahaṇaṃ viṣabhakṣaṇaṃ ca jvalanaṃ ca jalapraveśaśca |
anācārabhāṇḍasevī janmamaraṇānubandhīni ||46|| bhuva0 atha bālamaraṇānyeva vyaktīkriyante- tatra śastragrahaṇaṃ tīrthādau mastake krakacadāpanaṃ śiraḥśāraṇādi visabhakkhaṇaṃceti viṣabhakṣaṇaṃ tālupuṭādi vairāgyāt, jvalanapraveśaḥ kāṣṭhabhakṣaṇaṃ pañcāgnitapaśca jalapraveśaśca tīrthe payasi nimajjanaṃ kaṇṭhasnānaṃ vā māghamāse | caśabdādanyadapyevaṃvidhamudbandhanādi bālamaraṇaṃ jñātavyam | etāni bālamaraṇāni ke mariṣyantītyāha - aṇayārabhaṃḍasevitti - ācāraḥ śāstravihito vyavahārastena bhāṇḍamupakaraṇamācārabhāṇḍopakaraṇaṃ, na ācārabhāṇḍopakaraṇamanācārabhāṇḍopakaraṇam | tatsevituṃ śīlaṃ yeṣāṃ te anācārabhāṇḍasevinaste ca parivrājakādayo mithyādṛṣṭayaḥ svayūthyā vā ye hāsamohādibhistatsevanti | tāni ca parivrājām |

khaṇḍaḥ-1 gā. 47
kalācikābharaṇaviśeṣaḥ | pavitrī - aṅgulyābharaṇaviśeṣaḥ | tāpasānāṃ katchoṭakapuṭikātāmrabhājanādi, brāhmaṇānāṃ yajñopavītādi | evamanyeṣāmapi | te caivamanācārabhāṇḍasevina: santo bālamaraṇāni mariṣyanti | kathaṃ janmāni ca maraṇāni tānyanubadhnantītyevaṃśīlāni janmamaraṇānubandhīni bahūni mariṣyantītyarthaḥ ||46||
soma0 bālamaraṇāni darśayati - śastragrahaṇaṃ tīrthādau mastake krakacadāpanaṃ - śiraḥśāraṇādi, viṣabhakṣaṇaṃ tālupuṭādi, duḥkhagarbhavairāgyatvāt jvalanamagnau kāṣṭhabhakṣaṇapañcāgnitapaḥkaraṇādirūpama, jalapraveśaśca tīrthajale nimajjanaṃ snānaṃ vā māghamāsādau caśabdādanyadapyevaṃvidhamudbandhanādi bālamaraṇaṃ jñātavyam | etāni bālamaraṇāni ke kurvantītyāha - aṇāyāra0 ācāraḥ śāstravihito vyāpārastena bhāṇḍamupakaraṇam ācārabhāṇḍam, na ācārabhāṇḍam anācārabhāṇḍam, svabuddhikalpitanānāvidhaveṣādi-rūpam, tat sevituṃ śīlaṃ yeṣāṃ te'nācārabhāṇḍasevinaste ca tridaṇḍakuṇḍakādidharāścakraparivrājakādayo mithyādṛṣṭayo hāsa-mohādinā saṃyataveśadhāriṇo'pyanācārabhāṇḍasevinaḥ, te caivaṃvidhāssanto bālamaraṇāni mariṣyanti bahūni | kathambhūtāni ? janmāni ca maraṇāni ca paramparābhāvīnyanekāni tānyanubadhnanti ityevaṃśīlāni janmamaraṇānubandhīni ityarthaḥ ||46||
guṇa0 tīrthādau mastake kakracadāpana, visa0 tālupuṭādi[bhakṣaṇaṃ], vairāgyāt jvalanapraveśaḥ, pañcāgnitapaśca jalapraveśaḥ payasi nimajjanaṃ māghamāse | caśabdādanyadapi udvandhanādi bālamaraṇaṃ jñātavyam | ācāraḥ śāstravihito vyavahārastena bhāṇḍamupakaraṇamityācārabhāṇḍaṃ, na ācārabhāṇḍamityanācārabhāṇḍaṃ tatsavituṃ śīlaṃ yeṣāṃ te'nācārabhāṇḍasevina:, te ca parivrājakādayo mithyādṛṣṭayaḥ janmamaraṇāni tānyanubandhīni bahūni mariṣyantītyarthaḥ ||46||
uḍḍamahe tiriyammi vi mayāṇi jīveṇa bālamaraṇāṇi | daṃsaṇa-nāṇasahagao paṃḍiyamaraṇaṃ aṇumarissaṃ ||47|| ūrdhvamadhastiraścayapi mṛtāni jīvena bālamaraṇāni |

guṇa atha guruṇā śrāvitavratavirādhane kṣapakacintā - yadi utpadyate jāyate duḥkhaṃ kaṣṭaṃ tato draṣṭavyo jñeyaḥ svabhāvatastat prādurbhāvaḥ na ko'pyātmavyatirikto'nyo hetuḥ, ātmaiva heturiti vicintayati | athavā navaraṃ punaḥ kiyaduḥkhametat | purāpi kā duḥkhaparaṃparā me mayā | mae tti made jātyādau āsaktacetasā saṃsāre saṃsaratā bhramatā satā na prāptā nānubhūtāḥ ? paraṃ tā akāmanirjarayā soḍhā alpastatsahane phalaviśeṣaḥ | idānīṃ tvanantaguṇanirjarālābhahetutvāt samyak soḍhavyā ityarthaḥ| athavā yadi utpadyate duḥkhaṃ tataḥ kiṃ kartavyamityāha- to tadā draṣṭavyo'sau sakhā mitraṃ rogādiḥ puruṣādirvā | kathaṃbhūtaḥ sakhā ? āpadaṃ dadātīti āpaddaḥ | sa sakhā draṣṭavyaḥ navaramayaṃ viśeṣaḥ | kā ca āpatkī me iti māṃ karmatāpannaṃ na prāptā | atha kā āpat karmatāpannā mayā ka; na prāptā'nupalabdhā, kiṃ viśiṣṭena ? saṃsaratā paribhramatā saṃsāre ||49||

khaṇḍa:-1 gā. 54
dvādaśamāsadvādaśapakṣasaṃlekhanaḥ | anidāno nidānarahitaḥ | īhiūṇa vitaḻa vicārya mati tatkālotpannāṃ, buddhimuttarakālabhāvinīṃ sūkṣmārthaparyālocikā paścādvicāraṇānantaram | malitakaṣāyo marditasamparāya: san sadyo jhaṭiti maraṇaṃ paṇḍitamaraṇaṃ pratīcchāmyajhīkaromītyarthaḥ ||53||
soma0 atha bhāvitagurūpadeśo vijñātaviṣayavipākaśca kṣapakaścintayati pūrva prathamaṃ “cattāri vicittāī" ityādikrameṇa kṛtaparikarmā kṛtadvādaśavarṣa-dvādaśamāsa-dvādaśapakṣarūpotkṛṣṭa-madhyamajaghanyasaṃlekhano'nidāno nidānarahita 'ūhiūṇa' vicārya svayogyatāviṣayāṃ matiṃ tatkālotpannāṃ buddhiṃ ca tadviṣayāmevottarakālabhāvinīṃ sūkṣmārthapalocananipuṇām, paścāt tadvicāraṇānantaraṃ malita-kaṣāyo marditasaṃparāyaḥ san sadyaḥ śīghraṃ maraṇaṃ tadhetutvenopacārādanaśanaṃ pratīcchāmyaṅgīkaromītyarthaḥ ||53||
guṇa iti vijñātaviṣayavipāko bhāvitagurūpadeśaśca kṣapako yadbhaṇati tadāha - pūrva prathama "cattāri vicittā" ityādikrameṇa kṛtaparikarmā tti kṛtokṛṣṭamadhyamajaghanyadvādaśavarṣadvādaśamāsadvādaśapakṣasaṃlekhanaḥ | anidāno nidānarahitaḥ | īhiūṇa vitaḻa vicārya matiṃ tatkālotpannāṃ buddhimuttarakālabhāvinī paścādvicāraṇānantaraṃ malitakaṣāyo marditasamparāyaḥ san sadyo jhaṭiti pratīcchāmyaṅgīkaromi ||53||
akkaṃḍe'cirabhāviya te purisā maraṇadesakālammi | puvakayakammaparibhāvaṇāe pacchā parivaḍaṃti ||54|| akāṇḍe'cirabhāvitāste puruṣāḥ maraṇadeśakāle |
pūrvakṛtakarmaparibhāvanātaḥ paścāt pratipatanti ||54|| bhuvaḥ atha ye'prastāve'parikarmitā vā'naśanaṃ pratipadyante teṣāmapāyamāha - akāṇḍe'prastāve'navasare ye ārādhanāṃ kurvanti | tathā na ciraṃ prabhūtaṃ kālaṃ paribhāvitaḥ parikarmita ātmā yaiste acirabhāvitāḥ | vibhaktilopaḥ prākṛtatvāt | te'kṛtā'navasarārādhanāḥ akṛtārādhanāḥ puruṣā maraṇadeśakāle maraṇasamaye pūrvakṛtakarmaparibhāvanātaśca prāktanakarmavipākodayata: pratipatanti [paścāt] nidānādi kurvantīti | nandiṣeṇavat mithyātvamapi durgatiṃ ca yānti | kuruḍotkuruḍavadityarthaḥ ||54||

soma0 atha ye'prastāve'parikarmitā vā saṃlekhanayā'naśanaṃ pratipadyante teṣāmapāyamāha - akāṇḍe'prastāve'navasare ye ārādhanāmanaśagrahaṇarūpāṃ kurvanti, tathā ciraṃ prabhūtaṃ kālaṃ bhāvitaḥ saṃlekhanayā parikarmita ātmā yaiste cirabhāvitāḥ, tathā ye na evaṃvidhāste [acirabhāvitāḥ] puruṣā maraṇadeśakāle maraṇasamaye ityarthaḥ, deśakālaśabdena prastāva ucyate | pūrvaṃ kṛtamarjitaṃ yat karma tasya parisamantād bhāvanā tataḥ, svaprākṛtakarmavipākodayata ityarthaḥ | paścāt pratipatanti nidānādikaraṇena nandiṣeṇavat mithyātvaṃ durgatiṃ ca yānti kuruḍotkuruḍavat ||54 ||
gaṇa0 atha ye'prastāve'parikarmitā vā'naśanaṃ pratipadyante teṣāmapāyamāha-aprastāve'navasare ārādhanāṃ kurvanti. | ciraṃ prabhūtaṃ kālaṃ bhāvitaḥ parikarmita ātmā yaiste [cirabhāvitāḥ, tathā ye na evaṃvidhāste acirabhāvitā: |] acirabhāviyā iti atra vibhaktilopaḥ prākṛtatvāt te kṛtānavasarārādhanāḥ puruṣā maraṇadeśakāle maraṇasamaye pūrvakṛtakarmaparibhāvanātaśca prāktanakarmavipākodayataḥ | pratipatanti nidānāni kurvanti nandiṣeṇavat | durgatiṃ ca yānti kuruḍotkuruḍavat ||54||

kālaṃ kṛtvā suvihitaḥ susādhuḥ samyak utkṛṣṭato'tiśayena samyagārādhanāṃ kṛtvā trīn bhavān gatvā labhate nirvāṇaṃ mokṣamityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati - yadi paramasamādhānena kālaṃ karoti samyak tatastṛtīye bhave'vaśyaṃ siddhyati | atrāha paraḥ utkṛṣṭato'ṣṭabhavābhyantare sāmāyikaṃ prāpya siddhyati ityetannāpyutkṛṣṭaṃ nā'pi jaghanyaṃ tataśca kathaṃ na virodha: ? iti ucyate - anavagāhitasiddhāntasadbhāvasyaitadvacaḥ | yatpunarekainaiva bhavena jaghanyataḥ siddhyatītyuktaṃ tadvaṛṣabhanārācasaṃhananamāśritya etaccacchedapṛṣṭasaṃhananamaṅgīkṛtyocyate | chedapṛṣṭasaṃhanano hi yadyutkṛṣṭārādhanāṃ karoti tatastṛtīye bhave mokṣaṃ prāpnoti | utkṛṣṭaśabdaścātrātiśayārthe dṛṣṭaḥ| na tu bhavānaṅgīkṛtya, bhavāṅgīkaraṇe punaraṣṭabhirevotkṛṣṭato bhavaiśchedapṛṣṭasaṃhananaḥ siddhyatīti na virodha iti ||2||
soma0 athārādhakasya kiṃ phalamityāha - ārāhaṇā0 uttamārthapratipattyā''rādhanāyāṃ vopayukta udyataḥ sāvadhāna ityarthaḥ kālaṃ maraṇaṃ kṛtvā suvihitaḥ susādhuḥ samyak śuddhabhāvanotkṛṣṭata utkṛṣṭārādhanābalāt trīn bhavān gatvā labhate nirvāṇaṃ mokṣamityarthaḥ, yadi paramasamādhinā kālaṃ maraṇaṃ nirantaramaṣṭabhavārādhanayā jaghanyatastvekabhavārādhanayā'pi sidhyatītyuktam | atra tu tṛtīyabhave sidhyatīti tadetannāpyutkṛṣṭaṃ nāpi jaghanyaṃ tataśca kathaṃ na virodhaḥ ? ucyate - yāvadekaineva bhavena sidhyatītyuktaṃ tad vajraṛṣabhanārācasaṃhananamāśritya etacca sevārttasaṃhananamaṅgīkṛtyocyate sevārttasaṃhanano hi yadyutkṛṣṭārādhanāṃ karoti tatastṛtīye bhave sidhyatīti utkṛṣṭaśabdaścātiśayārthaḥ, ārādhanāviśeṣaṇaṃ ca dṛṣṭavyaḥ na tu bhavānaṅgīkṛtya, bhavāṅgīkāre punarukṛṣṭato'ṣṭabhireva bhavaiḥ sevārttasaṃhananaḥ sidhyatīti na virodhaḥ ||62|||
guṇa ārādhakasya kiṃ phalaṃ - ārādhanayā uttamārthapratipattyā upayukta udyataḥ kālaṃ kṛtvā suvihitaḥ susādhuḥ samyak utkṛṣṭato'tiśayena samyagārādhanāṃ kṛtvā trīn bhavān gatvā labhate nirvāṇaṃ mokṣamityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati - yadi paramasamādhānena kālaṃ karoti samyak tatastṛtīye bhave'vaśyaṃ sidhyati ||62||
samaṇo tti ahaṃ paḍhama, bīyaṃ savvattha saṃjao mi tti | savvaṃ ca vosirāmi, eyaṃ bhaṇiyaṃ samāseṇaṃ ||3|| śramaṇa iti ahaṃ prathamaṃ dvitīyaṃ sarvatra saṃyato'smīti |

āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakam gā. 68, 69
cirausiyabaṃbhayārī papphoḍeūṇa sesayaṃ kammaṃ | aṇupubbīi visuddho gacchai siddhiṃ dhuyakileso ||68|| ciroṣitabrahmacārī prasphoṭya śeṣakaṃ karma |
ānupūrvyā viśuddho gacchati siddhiṃ dhūtakleśaḥ ||68|| bhuka cirausiyatti prākṛtatvādvisaṃdhiḥ | ciramuṣito brahmaṇaḥ caraṇaṃ brahmacāraḥ | ciroṣito brahmacāro yena sa ciroṣitabrahmacāraḥ | ciroṣitaḥ brahmacāro vidyate yasyā'sau ciroṣitabrahmacārī tvam | prasphoṭya vidhūya śeṣakaṃ karma kṣiptoddhāritaṃ karma jñānāvaraṇādi | athavā cirao si baṃbhayārī iti vā pāṭhaḥ| tadāyamarthaḥ - yata eva virato'si brahmacāryasi ata eva prasphoṭya śeṣakaṃ karmeti sambandhaḥ | ānupūrvyā krameṇottarottaraguṇasthānādhirohaṇata: viśuddhaḥ karmamalamuktaḥ san ||68||
soma0 tathā ciram uṣitaḥ sevitaḥ prākṛtatvādvisandhirasvāralopaśca ciroṣito yo brahmaṇaścaraṇaṃ brahmacāraḥ sa vidyate yasyā'sau ciroṣitabrahmacārī | 'cira usibaṃbhayārī' itipāṭhe tvayamoṃ yata eva virato'si brahmacāryasi tvam, ata eva prasphoṭya vidhūya śeṣakaṃ pūrvakṣiptakarmabhya uddharitaṃ karma jñānāvaraṇādi ānupūrvyā krameṇottarottaraguṇasthānādhirohalakṣaṇena viśuddhaḥ san karmamalakṣālanataḥ prathamapāṭhe sāmānyato jīvaḥ kartā gacchati yāti siddhiṃ śivaṃ dhūtakleśaḥ kṣiptasaptabhayakleśaḥ sannityarthaḥ | dvitīyapāṭhe prākṛtatvād vibhaktivyatyayaṃ kṛtvā gacchasi yāsyasi siddhiṃ śivamiti vyākhyeyam ||68|||
guṇa ciramuṣito brahmacāro yena sa ciroṣitabrahmacārī prasphoṭya vidhūya śeṣakaṃ pūrvakṣiptoddharitaṃ karma jñānāvaraṇādi ānupūrvyā krameṇa viśuddho karmamalakṣālanāt kṣiptabhayakleśaḥ ||68 ||
nikkasāyassa daṃtassa sarassa vavasāiṇo | saṃsāraparibhīyassa paccakkhāṇaṃ suhaṃ bhave ||69|| niṣkaṣāyasya dāntasya śūrasya vyavasāyinaḥ |

khaṇḍa:-1 gā. 70, 71
soma0 tathā niṣkaṣāyasya kaṣāyarahitasya tathā, dāntasyendriya-noindriyadamena, tathā śūrasya mohamallajaye, vyavasāyina udyamavata ārādhanāpatākālābhāya, tathā saṃsārāt paribhītasya bhavabhīroḥ pratyākhyānabhaṅgadoṣadarśina iti hṛdayam, evaṃvidhasya sādhoḥ pratyākhyānamanaśanapratipatirūpaṃ śubhaṃ śubhāyatiphalaṃ bhavet syādityarthaḥ ||69||
guṇa niṣkaṣāyasya kaṣāyarahitasya dāntasya indriyanoindriyadamena śūrasya mohamallajaye, vyavasāyina ārādhanāpatākālābhāya saṃsāraparibhītasya bhavabhīroḥ, pratyākhyānamanaśanapratipattirūpaṃ śubhaṃ bhavatu ||69||
eyaṃ paccakkhāṇaṃ jo kāhī maraṇadesakālammi | dhīro amūḍhasano so gacchai uttamaṃ ṭhāṇaṃ ||70|| etatpratyākhyānaṃ yaḥ kariṣyati maraṇadeśakāle |
dhīro'mūḍhasaṃjñaḥ sa gacchatyuttamaṃ sthānam ||70|| bhuva0 eyaṃ paccakkhāṇaṃ ti etadanaśanapratipattirūpaṃ pratyākhyānaṃ yaḥ ko'pyanyo'pi kariṣyati | maraṇadeśakāle dhīro vidvān na mūḍhā mūrchitā saṃjñā jñānaṃ yasya so'mūḍhasaṃjñaḥ saṃpūrṇajñāna ityarthaḥ | sa gacchati yātyuttamaṃ sthānaṃ mokṣasthānamityarthaḥ ||70||
soma0 tathā etadanaśanapratipattirūpaṃ pratyākhyānaṃ yaḥ ko'pyanyo'trāpi kariṣyati maraṇadeśakāle prāntasamaye, dhīro vidvān na mūḍhā mūrchitā saṃjñā jñānaṃ yasya so'mūḍhasaṃjñaḥ spaṣṭajñānaḥ sampūrṇajñāna ityarthaḥ sa evaṃvidho gacchati yātyuttamaṃ sthānaṃ mokṣasthānamityarthaḥ ||70||
guṇaḥ etat pratyākhyānamanaśanapratipattirūpaṃ yaḥ ko'pyanyo'pi kariṣyati maraṇadeśakāle dhīro vidvān na mūḍhā saṃjñā jñānaṃ yasya sa amūḍhasaṃjñaḥ saṃpūrṇajñāna ityarthaḥ | sa gacchati yātyuttamaṃ sthānaṃ mokṣasthānamityarthaḥ ||7||

પ.પૂ. પંડિત શ્રીરૂપવિજયજી કૃત પીસ્તાલીસ આગમની
મોટી પૂજા શ્રીઆતુરપ્રત્યાખ્યાન પ્રકીર્ણક સૂત્ર પૂજા
|| દુહો || રાગ દ્વેષને છેદરે, ભેદજે આઠે કર્મ | સ્નાતક પદને અનુસરી, ભજે શાશ્વત શર્મ III
રાગઃ ઈડર આંબા આંબલી રે. દેશી દેશવિરતિ ગુણઠાણમેં રે, વરતે શ્રાવક જેહ | આણંદાદિકની પરે રે, તજે મિથ્યાત્વને તેહ IIII
સગુણનર, પૂજા શ્રી જિનદેવ... એ આંકણી બારે વ્રતના પરિહરે રે, પ્રત્યેકે અતિયાર | કરમાદાન પનર તજી રે, સમકિતના પંય છાર |રા સુ. || જ્ઞાન દર્શન ચારિત્રના રે, તપ વીરજના જેહ | અતિચાર અલગા કરી રે, ભજ જિનવર ગણગેહ II3II સુ. || પારંગત પદ પૂજીયે રે, તજી ત્રેસઠ દુર્ગાન | ઈન્દ્રિય કષાયને ઝીપીને રે, પામે સમકિત જ્ઞાન llll સુ. || આઉર પચ્ચકખાણ સૂત્રની રે, કરે આરાધના જેe I ત્રીજે ભવે શિવ સંપદા રે, નિશ્ચય પામે તેહ પાસુ. | તેણે એ સૂત્રની પૂજના રે, કરજો ધરી સુહ ઝાણ રૂપવિજય કહે પામજો રે, શાશ્વત સુખ નિર્વાણ III સુ. ||

khaṇḍaḥ -2 prakīrṇakoktaduānā'ntargatadṛṣṭāntasamuccayaḥ 1. ajñānadhyāne'śakaṭāpituḥ sampradāyaḥ saṅghācāravidhigranthāt | pramādyan sūrireko'tra, pretyābhūd vigataśrutaḥ | tatrodyacchan sa evābhūt, pāraddaśvā śrutāmbudheḥ ||1|| tathāhi-ekasmin bhrātarau gacche, gaṅgākūlanivāsinau | vrataṃ jagṛhatuḥ śāntau tatraiko'bhūd bahuśrutaḥ ||2|| sūrirjajñe krameṇāsau, śiṣyaiḥ sūtrārthamicchabhiḥ | sevyamāno dinaṃ sarvaṃ, viśrāmaṃ nāśnute kvacit ||3|| niśāyāmapi sūtrārthacintanapṛcchanādibhiḥ | nāsasāda sukhānidrāmanvahaṃ vyagramānasaḥ ||4|| bhrātā tasya dvitīyastu, nityamāste yathāsukham | taṃ ca paśyannasau sūrirdadhyau durbuddhibādhitaḥ ||5|| aho me bāndhavo dhanyo, yo'yamāste sadā sukhī | jñānavijñānahīnatvāt, kenāpyāyāsyate nahi ||6|| ajākṛpāṇakalpena, jñānenāhaṃ tvavāpnuyām | duḥkhaṃ tato'tra kenāpi, viduṣā sūditaṃ hyadaḥ ||7|| mūrkhatvaṃ hi sakhe ! mamāpi rucitaṃ tasyāpi cāṣṭau guṇā,

āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakam
śṛṇvatastasya vairāgyaṃ, babhūva laghukarmaṇaḥ | sutāmudvāhya kenāpi, dattvā tasmai dhanādikam ||17|| gacche kasmin sa niṣkramya, yogodvahanamāhṛtaḥ | kurvannadhyaiṣṭa suspaṣṭamuttarādhyayanatrayam ||18|| paṭhato'saṃskṛtākhyaṃ ca, turyādhyayanamañjasā | tad jñānāvaraṇīyākhyamāgātkarmodayaṃ tataḥ ||19|| adhīyānasya tattasyācāmāmlābhyāṃ dinadvayam | naiko'pyālāpako'syāgātkṛcchreṇāṇyabhiyogataḥ ||20|| tato'sau gurubhiḥ proce, kiṃ te'nujñāpyatāmidam ? | sa prāha bhagavannasya, yogaḥ kīdṛg ? tato guruḥ ||21|| ūce yāvadidaṃ naiti, tāvadācāmlamasya tu | sa smāha kṛtamanyena, śrutena tapasā ca me ||22|| ācāmlānyatha so'kārṣīd, dvādaśābdī samāhitaḥ | kṣayamāpaitakat karma, sukhenādhyaiṣṭha tacchrutam ||23|| śeṣaṃ cāpi śrutaṃ kṣipramadhīte sma mahāmatiḥ | śrutabhakterihāmutra, sarvatra sukhabhāgabhūt ||24|| 2. anācāradhyāne koṅkaṇasādhoḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ śrīkalpasūtrasubodhikāṭīkā'ntargataḥ |
yathā ko'pi kuṅkaṇadeśīyo vaṇig vṛddhatve pravrajitaḥ, sa caikadā airyāpathikīkāyotsarge ciraṃ sthito gurubhiḥ pṛṣṭaḥ- ‘etāvaddīghe kāyotsarge kiṃ cintitam ?' sa pratyuvāca-svāmin ! jīvadayā cintitā', kathamiti punargurubhiḥ pṛṣṭa āha- 'pūrvaṃ gṛhasthāvasthāyāṃ kṣetreṣu vṛkṣaniṣūdanapūrvakamuptāni dhānyāni bahūnyabhūvan, idānīṃ mama putrāstu niścintā yadi vṛkṣaniṣūdanaṃ na kariṣyanti tadā dhānyā'bhāvena varākāḥ kathaṃ bhaviṣyanti ?' iti ṛjutvāt svābhiprāye yathāsthite nivedite gurubhiḥ kathitaṃ- 'mahābhāga ! durdhyātaṃ bhavatā, aho ! ayuktametadyatīnām,' ityukte ca mithyāduṣkṛtaṃ dadau || 2-14. anācāradhyāne śaṅkādhyāne ca devānāmanāgamanādutpravrajitukāmasyā''ṣāḍhasūreḥ śrīmadbhāvavijayakṛtaśrīuttarādhyayana-sūtravṛtyuktakathā | vatsābhūmau bhūriśiṣyaparivārā bahuśrutāḥ | āryāṣāḍhābhidhācāryā, babhūvurviśvavatsalāḥ ||1|| yo yasteṣāṃ gaṇe bhaktaṃ, pratyākhyāya vyapadyata | taṃ taṃ niryāmya nirgrantha-mitthaṃ te sūrayo'vadan ||2|| devabhāvaṃgatenā''śu, deyaṃ me darśanaṃ tvayā | ityukte'pi bahūnāṃ tairnāgātko'pi divaṃ gataḥ ||3|| athā'nyadā svaśiṣyaṃ te, niryāmyātīva vallabham | evamucuḥ sanirbandhaṃ, guravo gadgadākṣaram ||4|| svargagatena bhavatā, vatsa ! vatsalacetasā | avaśyaṃ darśanaṃ deyaṃ tvāmiti prārthaye bhṛśam ||5|| mayā hi bahusādhunāmevamuktamabhūtparam | nā''gātko'pi tvaṃ tu vatsā''gaccheḥ snehamamuṃ smaran ||6|| tatprapadya vipadyāśu, devībhūto'pi sa drutam | nāyayau prathamotpanna-surakāyairvilambitaḥ ||7|| tasminnanāgate sadyo, viparyastamanā guruḥ | evaṃ vyacintayannūnaṃ, paraloko na vidyate ! ||8||

khaṇḍa:-2 dṛṣṭāntasamuccayaḥ
jñānadarśanacāritrārādhakāḥ śāntacetasaḥ | vihitānaśanāḥ samyagmayā niryāmitāḥ svayam ||9|| madvācaṃ pratipatrāśca, vineyo mama ye mṛtāḥ | snehaleṣvapi teṣveko-'pyā''gāno kathamanyathā ? [yugmam ||10|| tadadya yāvaccakre'sau, kriyā kaṣṭapradā mudhā | bhogān hitvā manojñāṃśca, mayātmā vañcito vṛthā ! ||11|| bhuktvā bhogāṃstadadyāpi, kariṣye saphalaṃ januḥ | paraloke hyasati kaḥ, kliśyate kuśalo mudhā ! ||12|| vimṛśyeti svaliṅgastha, eva mithyātvamāśritaḥ | utpravrajitukāmo'sau, muktvā gacchaṃ viniryayau ||13|| atrāntare'vadhijñānātsvarūpaṃ svaguroridam | jñātvā divaṃ gataḥ śiṣyo, viṣaṇṇo dhyātavāniti ! ||14|| aho ! madguravo jaināgamanetrānvitā api | vimuktimārga muñcanti, mohāndhatamasākulāḥ ! ||15|| aho ! mohasya mahimā, jagajjaitro vijṛmbhate | jātyandhā iva ceṣṭante, paśyanto'pyakhilā janāḥ ! ||16|| kulavānapi dhīro'pi, gabhīro'pi sudhīrapi | mohājjahāti maryādāṃ, kalpāntādiva vāridhiḥ ||17|| tanmohapreritā yāvannāmī duṣkarma kurvate | tāvadetānvibodhyāhaṃ, kurve sanmārgamāśritān ! ||18 || dhyātvetyāgatya sa suraḥ, svagurorgamanādhvani | grāmamekaṃ vicakre tat-pārve divyaṃ ca nāṭakam ||19|| tataḥ sa sūristannāṭyaṃ, prekṣyamāṇo manoharam | ūrdhvaṃ eva hi ṣaṇmāsīmāsītprājyapramodabhāk ||20|| śītātapakṣudhātṛṣṇāṣaṇmāsatikramaśramān | divyānubhāvānnājāsīttannāṭyaṃ sa vilokayan || 21|| tasminnṛtye'tha devena, saṃhate so'calatpuraḥ | kṣaṇamekaṃ śubhaṃ nāṭyaṃ, dṛṣṭaṃ diṣṭyeti bhāvayan ||22|| sa devo'tha tadākūtaṃ, parīkṣitumalaḍkṛtān | ṣaṭ jīvakāyasaṃjñān ṣaṭ, vidadhe bālakān vane ||23|| dṛṣṭvātha sūristeṣvādyaṃ, bhūribhūṣaṇabhūṣitam | iti dadhyau śiśorasyā'laṅkārānācchinamyaham ||24 || eṣāṃ dravyeṇa bhogecchā, ciraṃ me pūrayiṣyate | mṛgatṛṣṇāmbupānecchādeśyā dravyaṃ vinā hi sā ||25 || vimṛśyeti sa taṃ kṣīrakaṇṭhaṃ sotkaṇṭhamabravīt | re ! muñca muñcālaṅkārān, bālakaḥ sa tu nā'mucat ||26 || tato ruṣṭaḥ sa taṃ śāvaṃ, jagrāha galakandale | so'rbhako'pi bhayodbhrāntastamityūce sagadgadam ||27 || asyāmaṭavyāṃ bhīmāyāṃ, bibhyaccaurādyupadravāt | pṛthvīkāyikasaṃjño'hamasmi tvāṃ śaraṇaṃ śritaḥ ||28 || aśāśvatā hyamī prāṇā, viśvakīrtiśca śāśvatī | yaśorthī prāṇanāśe'pi, tadrakṣeccharaṇāgatam ! ||29|| bālaṃ māṃ dīnatāṃ prāptaṃ, pāhi pāhi prabho ! tataḥ | taireva bhūṣitā bhūrye, rakṣeyuḥ śaraṇāgatam ||30||
yataḥ-“vihalaṃ jo avalaṃbai, āvaipaḍiaṃ ca jo samuddharai | saraṇāgayaṃ ca rakkhai, tisu tesu alaṃkiā puhavī ||31||"

100
āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakam
ityādyukto'pi lubdhātmā, sa sūristasya kandharām | yāvanmoṭayituṃ lagnastāvacchāvaḥ punarjagau ||32|| bhagavannekamākhyānaṃ, śrutvā kuryā yathocitam | sūrijagāda tabrūhi, sopyākhyat śrūyatāmiti ||33|| grāme kvāpi kulālo'bhūtsa cānyedhurmudaṃ khanan | ākrāntaḥ patatā khānitaṭeneti vaco'vadat ||34 || yatprasādābaliṃ bhikṣāṃ, dade jñātīṃśca poṣaye | sā'pyā''krāmati bhūmirmī, tajjātaṃ śaraṇādbhayam ! ||35 || yathā hyājīvikāmukhyasaukhyārthī pṛthivīṃ śritaḥ | varākaḥ kumbhakāro'yaṃ, tayaivopahato drutam ! ||36|| bhagavannahamapyevaṃ, bhītastvāṃ śaraṇaṃ śritaḥ | tvaṃ ca muṣṇāsi māṃ tadbhīrmamāpi śaraṇādabhūt ! ||37|| tadākātidakṣo'si, re ! bāleti vadan guruḥ | tadrūṣaṇāni jagrāha, nijagrāha ca tāṃ śiśum ! ||38 | tānaśeṣānalaṅkārānakṣipatsvapratigrahe | vratāddhṛṣṭo hi dakṣo'pi, niśśūko jāyate bhṛśam ! ||39|| tataḥ puro vrajan kāñcidatikrānto vanīṃ guruḥ | bālakaṃ prāgvadadrākṣīdapkāyākhyaṃ dvitīyakam ||40|| tasmiṃstasyā'pya'laṅkārāṃstathaivā''dātumudyate | so'pyā''khyāya nijāmākhyāmākhyānaṃ khyātavāniti ||41 || “ekastālācaraścārukathākathanakovidaḥ | pāṭalāhvo'bhavadbharisubhāṣitarasahadaḥ ||42|| so'nyadā prottaran gaṅgāṃ, nīrapūraiḥ pravāhitaḥ | tīrasthairdadṛśe lokairityūce ca savismayaiḥ ||43|| bahuśrutaṃ citrakathaṃ, gaṃgā vahati pāṭalam | vāhyamānā'stu bhadraṃ te, brūhi kiñcitsubhāṣitam ||44 || samākobhayākarṇisakarṇastajjanoditam | ślokamekamanaślīlaṃ, pāṭalo'pyevamabravīt ||45|| yena rohanti bījāni, yena jīvanti karṣakāḥ | tasya madhye vipadyeta, jātaṃ me śaraṇādbhayam ! ||46||" kathāṃ procyeti tadbhāvaṃ, cāviṣkṛtya sthite śiśau | kṛpāṃ hitvā''dade sūristasyāpyābharaṇavrajam ||47|| tato'pyagre vrajastejaskāyikākhyaṃ tṛtīyakam | vīkṣyārbhakamabhūtsūristadbhūṣāgrahaṇodyataḥ ||48|| tataḥ so'pi śiśuḥ prāgvatprāduṣkṛtya nijābhidhām | itthaṃ kathāṃ kathayituṃ, paṭuvākyaiḥ pracakrame ||49|| "kvāpyāśrame tāpaso'bhūtsarvadā vahnipūjakaḥ | tasyoṭaje'nale naivā'nyadā dagdhe sa ityavak ||50|| yamahaṃ madhusarpibh‘, tarpayāmi divāniśam | dagdhastenaivoṭajo me, jātaṃ taccharaṇādbhayam ! ||51|| yadvāraṇyaṃ gataḥ kaścidvahniṃ vyāghrabhiyā niśi | ajvālayat pramattaśca, dagdhastenā'bravīditi ||52|| mayā hi vyāghrabhītena, pāvakaḥ śaraṇīkṛtaḥ || dagdhaṃ tena ca gātraṃ me, jātaṃ śaraṇato bhayam ||53||" ityuktvākhyānakaṃ tasyopanayaṃ ca prakāśya saḥ | tasthau śiśustatastasya, bhūṣaṇānyādade guruḥ ||54 || tato'pya'gre'rbhakaṃ vāyukāyākhyaṃ vīkṣya pūrvavat | lātuṃ tasyāpyalaṅkārān, sūrirudyamavāna'bhūt ||55 || so'pi śāvo nijaṃ nāma, prāgvattasmai prakāśayan | ākhyānaṃ vaktumārebhe, vāgmitvaṃ nāṭayanijam ||56||

khaṇḍa:-2 dṛṣṭāntasamuccayaḥ
101
"ekaḥ ko'pi yuvā bhūribalo'bhūtpīnabhūghanaḥ| vātarogagṛhītaṃ taṃ, prekṣya ko'pīti pṛṣṭavān ||57 || laṅghanaplavanodyogī, prāgbhūtvāpyadhunā bhavān | yāti yaṣṭimavaṣṭabhya, kasya vyādherupadravāt ? ||58 || so'vādīdyo marujjyeṣṭhā''ṣāḍhayoḥ saukhyado bhavet | sa eva bādhate'ṅga me, jātaṃ hi śaraṇādbhayam ! ||59|| ākhyānamityuditvā tadbhāvayitvā ca pūrvavat | śiśoḥ sthitasya tasyāpi, bhūṣaṇānyagrahīdguruḥ ||60 || bhūyopi purato bālaṃ, prāgvadābharaṇairbhūtam | sa vanaspatikāyākhyaṃ, pañcamaṃ sūriraikṣata ||61|| tasyāpi bhūṣaṇagaṇaṃ, grahītuṃ sodyame gurau | so'pītyākhyānamācakhyau, svābhikhyākhyānapūrvakam ||62|| “drume puṣpaphalākīṇe, kvāpi ke'pya'vasan khagāḥ | vṛkṣo hyayaṃ naḥ śaraṇamiti viśrabdhacetasaḥ ||63|| teṣāṃ ca vasatāṃ tatra, nirābādhamathānyadā | apatyāni bahūnyantīṃḍaṃ krīḍanti jajñire ||64|| itaśca tasya vṛkṣasya, pārvātkā'pyudgatā latā| taṃ taruṃ pariveṣṭyoccairāruroha drumopari ||65 || tayā ca latayā'nyedhurvilagya bhujago mahān | āruhya taṃ drumaṃ tāni, khagāpatyānyabhakṣayat ||66|| tataste vihagāḥ svīyāpatyavidhvaṃsaduḥkhitāḥ | kurvantastumulaṃ proccairitthamāhurmuhurmithaḥ ||67|| adya yāvatsukhaṃ vṛkṣe, sthitamatrānupadrave | asmādeva latāyuktādadyābhūccharaṇādbhayam ||68||" ityudīrya kathāṃ tasyā, bhāvaṃ prāgvat prakāśya ca | tasthuṣastasya śāvasyā'pyādade bhūṣaṇāni saḥ ||69|| tato'gre prasthitaḥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ, trasakāyākhyamarbhakam | vīkṣya tasyāpyalaṅkārān, so'bhūdācchettumutsuka: ||70 || nijāmākhyāṃ samākhyāya, so'pyā''khyānacatuṣṭyam | avādīdvīndriyādīnāṃ, caturṇāṃ tatra sambhavāt ||71|| "tathā hi nagare kvāpi, parīte parito'ribhiḥ | bhītā bahisthā mātaṅgā, purāntaḥ prāviśan drutam ||72|| tāṃśca madhyasthitaiokerannādikṣayabhīrubhiḥ | niṣkāśyamānānnagarādvidviṣo'pīḍayan bhṛśam ||73|| puraṃ naḥ śaraṇaṃ bhāvītyāśayā viśato'pi tān | nirīkṣya durdaśāṃ prāptāstadā ko'pītya'bhāṣata ||74 | | bhītāḥ paurāḥ karṣayanti, yuṣmānnijanti ca dviṣaḥ | tatkvāpi yāta mātaṅgā !, jātaṃ śaraṇato bhayam ||75 ||" prāgvat sopanaye tena, prokte'pyevaṃ kathānake | amuñcati gurau bālau, dvitīyāmabravītkathām ||76|| “nagare kvāpyabhūdbhūpaḥ, sa ca duṣṭo nijairnaraiḥ | svīya eva pure caurya, sarvadā'cīkaradṛśam ||77 || rājñastasya purodhāstu, sarvaṃ janamabhaṇḍayat | khinnāstato'khilā lokāḥ, parasparamado'vadan ||78 || yatra rājā svayaṃ cauro, bhaṇḍakaśca purohitaḥ | yāta paurāḥ ! purāttasmājjātaṃ hi śaraṇādrayam ||79||" kathāṃ sopanayāṃ prāgvadimāmūcānamapyamum | nā'nūcāno'mucadgrastaṃ, janaṃ duṣṭa iva grahaḥ !||8|| tatastṛtīyamākhyānaṃ, vaktuṃ prāka़sta so'rbhakaḥ | “tathā hi kvāpyabhūdgrāme, dvijanmā ko'pi kāmukaḥ ||81||

102
āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakama
tasya cāsītsutā madhyavayobhūṣitabhūghanā | udagrarūpalāvaṇyā, jagannetrasudhāñjanam ||82|| anyadā tāṃ sutāṃ vīkṣya, riraṃsuḥ sa dvijo'bhavat | na hi prabalabhogecchaḥ, sthānāsthāne vicārayet ||83|| tāṃ ca kāmayamāno'pi, na siṣeve sa lajjayā| tatkāmasyānivṛtteśca, jajñe kṣīṇatanubhṛśam ||84 || taṃ cātidurbalaṃ prekṣya, sanirbandhaṃ tadaṅganā | aprākṣītkṣāmatāhetuṃ, so'pyācakhyau yathātatham ||85|| tataḥ sā vyamṛśaddakṣā, yadyenāṃ nāpnuyādayam | tadāvaśyaṃ vipadyeta, drāg daśāṃ daśamīṃ gataḥ ||86|| vidhāyākāryamapyetattadenaṃ jīvayāmyaham | nijo bhartā hi patnībhirjīvanīyo yathātathā ||87||| sā vicintyeti taṃ proce, mā kārṣīradhṛtiṃ priya ! | ahaṃ kenā'pyupāyena, kariṣyāmi tavehitam ||88|| tamityāśvāsya sā putrīmiti provāca dambhinī | pūrvaṃ hi naḥ sutāṃ yakṣo, bhuṅkte paścādvivāhyate ||89|| kṛṣṇabhūteṣṭāniśāyāṃ, tattvaṃ yakṣālayaṃ vrajeḥ | tvāṃ bhoktumudyataṃ tatrā''gataṃ yakṣaṃ ca mānayeḥ ||10|| he putri ! tatrodyotaṃ ca, mā kārṣīryakṣamīkṣitum | udyote hi kṛte yakṣaḥ, saroṣamupayāsyati ||91|| tacchrutvā mātṛvisrambhā, svīcakre sā'pi tadvacaḥ | vistrabdho hi jano'kāryamapi sadyaḥ prapadyate ! ||12|| rātrau ca mātṛproktāyāṃ, sā yakṣekṣaṇakautukāt | śarāvasthagitaṃ dīpaṃ, lātvā yakṣālayaṃ yayau ||13|| tanmātrā prahito bhaṭṭo'pyā''gāttadyakṣamandiram | tāṃ copabhujyaṃ ni:śaṃkaṃ, rataśrānto'svapītsukham ||94 || śarāvasampuṭāddīpamāviṣkṛtyā'tha kautukāt | paśyantī tatsutā tatra, tātaṃ dṛṣṭvetyacintayat ||95 || aho mayā samaṃ māyā, mātrā'pi mahatī kṛtā | bhartā tadayamevāstu, mama kiṃ lajjayā'dhunā ? ||96 || kiñca svatātamapyenamapaśaṃkaṃ bhajāmyatha | narttanodyuktanatakyā, vadanāvaraṇena kim ? ||97|| sā vimṛśyeti pitrā'pi, samaṃ reme yathāruci | rataśrāntau ca tau suptau, prābudhyetāṃ prage'pi na ||98 || mātā tasyāstataḥ kāntaviyogodagraduḥkhataḥ | alabdhanidrā yāminyāṃ, prātastāvityabhāṣata ||99|| udgate'pi ravau viśvaṃ, viśvaṃ spṛśati cā''tape | prabuddhe'pya'khile loke, hale ! jāgarti no sukhī ||100 || tatsavitrīvacaḥ pūrvaprabuddhā sā tadaṅgajā | śrutvā tadīyabhāvaṃ cā'vagamyetyuttaraṃ dadau ||101|| mātastvayaiva proktaṃ me, yadyakṣaṃ bahu mānayeḥ | yakṣeṇa cāhatastātastadanyaṃ tātameṣayaḥ ||102|| imāmākarṇya tadvācaṃ, brāhmaṇītyabravītpunaḥ | nava māsān svīyakukṣau, kaṣṭenā'dhāri yā mayā ||103|| viṇmūtre ca ciraṃ yasyā, mardite sā'pi nandanā | matkāntamaharattanme, jātaṃ śaraṇato bhayam ||104||" pūrvavadbhāvanāpūrvamityuktepi kathānake | tenā'muktaḥ śiśusturyamākhyānamidamuktavān ||105 || “tathā hi kvapyabhūdgrāme, vipraḥ ko'pi mahādhanaḥ | sa ca dharmadhiyā mūḍhaḥ, sarovaramacīkhanat ||106||

tasya pālyāṃ devakulamārāmaṃ ca vidhāpya saḥ | pravartya chāgayajñaṃ ca, muhustatra cakāra saḥ ||107 || ayaṃ hi dharmastrāṇaṃ me, paralokaṃ bhaviṣyati | dhyāyanniti sa yajñeṣu, chagalānavadhībahūn ||108|| bhūdevaḥ so'nyadā mṛtvā, chāgeṣvevodapadyata | so'pi chāgaḥ kramāvṛddhi, prāpto'bhūtpīnabhūghanaḥ ||109 || yajñe hantuṃ nīyamānaḥ, svaputraireva so'nyadā | svopajñaṃ tattaṭākādi, dṛṣṭvā svāṃ jātimasmarat ||110 || mayaiva kāritamidaṃ, mamaivābhūdvipattaye | nindannevaṃ svakṛtyaṃ sa, 'bubu' śabdaṃ vyadhānmuhuḥ ||111|| tathābhūtaṃ ca taṃ vīkṣya, jñānī ko'pi mahāmuniḥ | tatpūrvabhavavṛttāntaṃ, vijñāyaivamavocata ||112 || khānitaṃ hi tvayaivedaṃ, saro vṛkṣāśca ropitāḥ | pravartitā makhāścā'tha, kiṃ 'bubū' kuruṣe paśo ! ||113|| iti sādhuvacaḥ śrutvā, sa chāgo maunamāśrayat | svakarmaṇyudite kiṃ hi, pūtkārairiti cintayan ||114 || tūṣṇīkaḥ sādhuvācā'yamajo'bhūdityavetya te | athā'pṛcchan dvijāḥ sādhumityāścaryabharākulāḥ ||115 || kimeṣa meṣo bhagavannākarṇya bhavatāṃ vacaḥ | tūṣṇīkatvaṃ dadhau nāga, iva mantravaśīkṛtaḥ ? ||116|| muni gau bhavattātau, mṛtvā'sau chagalo'bhavat | dṛṣṭvā caitattaṭākādi, jātismaraṇamāsadat ||117 || tato duḥkhābubudhvānamuccaiḥ kurvanmayoditam | svakarmaṇāṃ doṣamamuṃ, jñātvā maunaṃ dadhau drutam ||118 | | tatastadaṅgajāḥ procuḥ, kaḥ pratyaya iha prabho ! | vinā pratyayamuktaṃ hi, parokṣaṃ śraddadhīta kaḥ ? ||119 || sādhurūce samakṣaṃ vaḥ, prāgbhave nihitaṃ svayam | nidhiṃ ceddarśayatyeṣa, tadā hyetadyathātatham ||120 || tadākarṇya nidhisthānaṃ, darśayetyuditaḥ sutaiḥ | chāgo gatvā nidhisthāne, pādāgreṇā'khanadbhuvam ||121|| tatastattanayairjātapratyayairyatisannidhau | sa chāgo mumuce jainadharmaśca pratyapadyata ||122|| dharmaṃ śrutvā munestasmānmeṣo'pi pratipadya saḥ | vihitānaśanaḥ sadyo, devabhūyamavindata ||123|| pretya me śaraṇaṃ bhāvītyāśayā sa dvijo yathā | taṭākādi vyadhāttacca, tasyāśaraṇatāmagāt ||124 || evaṃ mayā'pi bhītena, bhavantaḥ śaraṇīkṛtāḥ | cenmuṣṇanti tadā me'pi, trāṇamatrāṇatāṃ gatam ||125||" itthaṃ caturbhirākhyānairgurostenoditairapi | na durbhāvo nyavarttiṣṭā'sādhyo roga ivauṣadhaiḥ ||126 || tatastasyā'pyalaṅkārān, sūrirjagrāha pūrvavat | lubdho jano hi no dravyaistṛpyatyabdhirivāmbubhiḥ ||127 || evaṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ kumārāṇāmāttairābharaṇavrajaiḥ | pratigrahaṃ durvikalpairātmānaṃ ca babhāra saḥ ||128 || tato drutaṃ drutaṃ sūriḥ, puro gantuṃ pracakrame | sambandhyeṣāṃ śiśūnāṃ māṃ, mādrākṣīditi cintayan ||129 || devo'pyevaṃ parīkṣābhistaṃ praṇaṣṭavratāśayam | jñātvaikāṃ vyakarotsādhvīṃ, tatsamyaktvaṃ parīkṣitum ||130 || tāṃ ca gurvīmalaṅkāranikaraiḥ parimaṇḍitām | vīkṣya sūriḥ sasaṃrambhārambhamevamuvāca saḥ ||131||

añjitākṣī bhūribhūṣābhūṣitā tilakāṅkitā | śāsanoḍḍāhakṛduṣṭasādhvi ! tvaṃ kuta āgatā ? ||132 || sūrestasyeti vacanaṃ śrutvā roṣabharākulā | sā vratinyapi niḥśaṅkaṃ pratyuvāceti taṃ drutam ||133|| re sūre ! sarṣapābhāni, paricchidrāṇi paśyasi ? | ātmano bilvamātrāṇi paśyannapi na paśyasi ? ||134 || kiñcaivaṃ śikṣayannanyaṃ, nirdoṣaḥ khalu śobhate | svayaṃ sadoṣastu paraṃ, na śikṣayitumarhati ! || 135 || yadi ca tvaṃ manyase svaṃ, śramaṇaṃ brahmacāriṇam | samaleṣṭusuvarṇaṃ satkriyamugravihāriṇam ||136 || tadabhyehi mamābhyarṇamutkarṇaḥ kiṃ praṇaśyasi | vilokayāmi jyeṣṭhārya !, yathāhaṃ te pratigraham ||137|| tayetyuḍḍāhitaḥ sādhvyā, tūṣṇīkaḥ sa vrajan puraḥ | dadarśa sainyamāgacchat, kṛtaṃ tenaiva nākinā ||138 || bhayodbhrāntastataḥ suriḥ, sainyādhvānaṃ vihāya saḥ | naśyannapi nṛpasyaiva, purogāddaivayogataḥ || 139|| nṛpo'pi prekṣya taṃ hastiskandhāduttīrya cā'namat | āha sma cā'ho ! bhāgyaṃ me, yūyaṃ yadiha vīkṣitāḥ ! ||140|| tatkṛtvā'nugrahaṃ svāminmayīdaṃ modakādikam | eṣaṇīyaṃ prāsukaṃ ca gṛhyatāṃ gṛhyatāṃ drutam ||141 || nā'dya bhokṣye'hamityuccairvadan sūristu nā''dade | pātrastho bhūṣaṇaugho mā, dṛśyatāmiti cintayan ! ||142|| taṃ muñca muñcetyūcānaṃ, bhiyā bhūpastu nā'mucat | hriyā na neti jalpantīṃ navoḍhāṃ ramaṇo yathā ! || 143 || bhūbhujā muhurākṛṣṭamapi sūriḥ patadgraham | na mumoca navoḍhā. strī, bharnākṛṣṭamivāṃśukam ! ||144|| tataḥ prasahya tatpāṇestamācchidya patadgraham | tatra yāvannṛpaḥ kṣeptumārebhe modakādikam ||145|| tāvatsa tānalaṅkārānnirīkṣya kupito bhṛśam ! | tamācāryamuvācaivaṃ, bhrukuṭīvikaṭānanaḥ ||146|| are pāpa tvayā nūnaṃ, putrā vyāpāditā mama | no cetkathamamī teṣāmalaṅkārāstavāntike ||147|| re duṣṭa ! dviṣṭha ! pāpiṣṭha !, sādhuveṣaviḍambaka ! | yāsyasi tvaṃ kathaṃ jīvana, vyāpādya mama nandanān ? ||148|| śrutveti bhūbhṛto bhāṣāṃ, sādhvasākulamānasaḥ | aghomukhaḥ so'nūcāno'nūcāno dhyātavāniti || 149|| aho ! vimūḍhacittenā'kāryametatkṛtaṃ mayā | yadetadīyaputrāṇāmādade bhūṣaṇavrajaḥ !||150 || matpātakaṃ ca sakalaṃ, jñātaṃ bhūsvāminā'munā | tadasau māṃ kumāreṇa, mārayiṣyati kenacit ! ||151|| pāpmano nikhilasyāpi, phalametadupasthitam | idānīmeva tatko'tra, śaraṇaṃ me bhaviṣyati ? || 152 || athavā pūrvamevedamavimṛśya vyadhāmaham | tatsaṃyamasukhaṃ tyaktaṃ yanmayā bhogakāmyayā || 153 || tatraivaṃ cintayatyeva, māyāṃ saṃhṛtya tāṃ suraḥ | āvirbabhūva svatanudyutidyotitadiṅmukhaḥ ||154|| tamityūce ca bhavagan !, so'haṃ śiṣyo'smi vaḥ priyaḥ | svayaṃ niryāmya yaḥ pūjyairāgantuṃ prārthito'bhavat ||155|| ahaṃ hi vratamāhātmyātsuro'bhūvaṃ maharddhikaḥ | smṛtvā vākyaṃ ca pūjyānāṃ, svavāgbaddha ihā''gamam ||156||

madanāgamane kaścitkālakṣepo babhūva yaḥ | sa tu jñeyo navotpannadevakāryākulatvataḥ ||157 || saṃyamabhraṣṭacittāṃśca, yuṣmān bodhayituṃ mayā | tannāṭyaṃ vidadhe pūjyairyadṛṣṭamadhunā'dhvani ! ||158 || mayaiva yuṣmadākūtaparīkṣārthaṃ pariṣkṛtāḥ | ṣaṭkāyāhvā dārakāḥ ṣaṭ, sasādhvīkā vikurvitāḥ ||159|| tato'vabudhya vaḥ prājyaṃ, mohonmādamuditvaram | mayodapādi sainyādibhayaṃ taddhvaṃsanauṣadham ||160 || śaṅkātaṅkāmamuṃ tasmāttyaktvā mohasamanvitam | unmārgagaṃ mano'vāptasanmārgaṃ kurutā''tmanaḥ ||161|| kiñca-“saṃkaṃtadivcapemā, visayapasattāsamattakattavvā | aṇahīṇamaṇuakajjā, narabhavamasuiṃ na iṃti surā ||162|| cattāri paṃca joaṇasayāī gaṃdho u maṇualogassa | u8 vaccai jeṇaṃ, na hu devā teṇa āvaṃti ||163||" ityādyāgamavākyāni, jānadbhirapi sūribhiḥ | madanāgamanepyetatkarmārabdhaṃ kimīdṛśam ? ||164|| anyacca divyanāṭyādivilokanakutūhalāt | kālaṃ yāntaṃ bahumapi, naiva jānanti nirjarāḥ ! ||165 || yuṣmābhirapi taddivyanāṭakākṣiptamānasaiḥ | ūrddhasthaireva ṣaṇmāsī, ninye'śrāntairmuhūrttavat ! ||166 || tadbhadantāḥ | vimoho'yaṃ, kartuṃ vo naiva yujyate | kalpānte'pi kimu kṣīrāmbhodhirullaṅghate'vadhim ? ||167 || bhavādṛśā api yadā, kurvantyevamanīdṛśam | dṛḍhadharmā jagati kastadāhyanyo bhaviṣyati ? ||168 || taddurācaritaṃ sarvamālocyedaṃ mahādhiyaḥ ! | samācarata cāritraṃ, karmakakṣahutāśanam ||169|| gīrvāṇavāṇīṃ śrutveti, pratibuddho mahāśayaḥ | sa sūriḥ svadurācāraṃ, bhūyo bhūyo nininda tam ! ||170 || vāraṃ vāraṃ ca taṃ devamāryāṣāḍho'bravīditi | sādhu sādhu tvayā vatsa !, bodhito'haṃ mahāmate ! ||171|| ahaṃ hi narakādhvānaṃ, prapanno'pi svakarmabhiḥ | mokṣamārgaṃ tvayaivā'tha, prāpito bhāvabandhunā ||172|| dharmāddhṛṣṭasya me bhūyo, dharmadānavidhāyinaḥ | tavā'nṛṇo'haṃ naiva syāṃ, bravīmi kimataḥ param ? ||173 || taṃ devamabhinandyeti, svasthānamagamadguruḥ | ālocitapratikrāntastapotyugraṃ cakāra ca ||174 || suro'pi sūriṃ natvā taṃ, pramodabharameduraḥ | kṣamayitvā svāparādhaṃ, suralokamagātpunaḥ ||175 || nāṣāḍhasūririti darśanagocaraṃ prāk, sehe parīṣahamamuṃ na tathā vidheyam | sūriḥ sa eva sahate sma yathā ca paścātsarvaistathā vrativaraiḥ satataṃ sa sahyaḥ ||176|| 3. kudarśanadhyāne śrīsamyaktvasaptatiṭīkāyāḥ surāṣṭraśrāvakasya dṛṣṭāntaḥ | deśe śrīmatsurāṣṭrāyāṃ, sarvvarāṣṭravibhūṣaṇe | ko'pi suśrāvako jajñe, samyagjñānāditattvavit ||1|| duṣprāpabhikṣe durbhikṣe, pravṛtte tatra devataḥ | so'nyadā bauddhasārthena, pratasthe'vantimaṇḍalam ||2|| sa śākyairīcyatānyedhurasmākamupadhiṃ yadi | mārge vahasi tat tubhyaṃ, dadmahe bhojanādikam ||3||

tadvacaḥ kṣudhitaḥ so'ṅgīkṛtya tadbhāṇḍabhṛtpathi | vrajannaśnaṃśca tadbhāvabhāvitātmā'bhavadbhūśam ||4|| saugate śāsane prītimatīva dadhato'sya tu | mārge yātaḥ samutpede, rogo'sādhyatamastanau ||5|| akṣamaḥ saha sārthena, gantuṃ pathi sa tasthivān | gairikāraktavastreṇācchādya taṃ saugatā gatāḥ ||6|| so'pi dūṣitasamyaktvo, rogataḥ pañcatāṃ gataḥ | yakṣībhūyāvadhijñānaṃ prāyuṅkāvyaktacetanaḥ ||7|| yā pūrvabhave kiṃ kiṃ kṛtaṃ sukṛtamadbhutam ? | yena prāptedṛśī yakṣapadavī sampadojjvalā || 8 || raktena vāsasācchannaṃ, tadā sa svakalevaram | mārge dṛṣṭvā bahirdṛṣṭirhadyevaṃ paryabhāvayat ||9|| anubhāvena bhikṣūṇāṃ labdhā devaśriyo mayā | tanve'hamapi māhātmyamato'mīṣāṃ hi śāsane || 10 || vaṭavāsī sa yatrāmī, bhikṣavo bhuñjate maṭhe | tatra kaṅkaṇakeyūra - mudrābhirmaṇḍitaṃ karam ||11|| prakaṭīkṛtya śākyebhyo, dadāno modakādikam | samastaṃ kṛtavāṃllokaṃ, sādaraṃ bauddhaśāsane || 12 || yugmam | tatprabhāvamadonmādaprollaṇṭhāḥ śaṭhacetasaḥ | apanindyaṃ tu nindanti, bhikṣavo jinaśāsanam || 13 || kimanyairdarśanairlokāḥ !, sevadhvaṃ śākyaśāsanam | yatra devā api sadā, sevāhevākaśālinaḥ | | 14 | | ekadā ke'pi sūrīndrā, vidyāsiddhā vasundharām | viharantaḥ samājagmurujjayinyāṃ mahaujasaḥ || 15 || śrāddhaiste bauddhavṛttāntaṃ jñāpitā jñānabhānavaḥ | praiṣiṣan śikṣayitveti, gītārthau dvau mahāmunī | | 16 || saugatānāṃ maṭhe hasto, yadā niryāti daivataḥ | taṃ vidhṛtya sa vaktavyo, yuvābhyāmuñccakairidam ||17|| aho budhyasva budhyasva, mā muhya guhyakottama ! | smara pañcanamaskāraṃ, pātheyaṃ śivavartmanaḥ || 18 || maṭhāyātau yatī bauddhairnimantryetāṃ madoddhuraiḥ | antaḥ praviṣṭau tau hastamadāṣṭa ca vinirgatam ||19|| nirudhya hastaṃ tau vyaktamavaktāṃ guruvācikam | tadā''karṇanato yakṣaḥ, pratyabudhyata tatkṣaṇāt ||20|| acintayacca hā dhigmāṃ, yasya me jinaśāsane | vijñasyāpi kathaṃ moho' bhūmidhyādṛṣṭisaṅgataḥ ? ||21|| āvirbhūya tato yakṣaḥ, saugatāṃstadupāsakān | aparānapyuvācoccaiḥ, śrūyatāṃ bho ! vaco mama ||22|| anye darśaninaḥ sarve mithyārūpāḥ prakīrttitāḥ | tathyamekaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ, śrījineśvaraśāsanam || 23 || tadbho mithyādṛśastyaktvā, śrayadhvaṃ jinaśāsanam | yathā karatalakroḍīsyāt śrīḥ khargāpavargayoḥ ||24|| ityudīrya tato gatvā, sūrīnnatvā'ticārajam | pāpamālocya jainaṃ sa mārgamudbhāvya jagmivān || 25 | | nipīya ke'pi tadvākyamarandaṃ maghupā iva | śrīmajjinamatodyāne, jagurarhadguṇāvalim || 26 ||

4. krodhadhyāne śrīmadbhāvavijayakṛtaśrīuttarādhyayanavṛtyuktaṃ kūlavālakamunicaritram | sūrerekasya śiṣyo'bhūdavinīto'tiroṣaṇaḥ | cukopa kopasadanaṃ, śikṣyamāṇaḥ sa sūriṇā ||1|| dakṣayā śikṣayā sūristathāpi tamaśikṣayat | sa tu tāmapi mene'ntarviṣāktaviśikhopamām ||2|| hitaśikṣā hi duṣṭānāṃ, nopakārāya jāyate | payaḥpānamivāhīnāṃ, kintu syādviṣavṛddhaye ||3|| natvā'nyadā siddhaśaile, jinānuttarato gurūn | peṣṭuṃ duṣṭaḥ sa pṛṣṭhastho, gaṃḍaśailamaloṭhayat ||4|| śabdāyamānamāyāntaṃ, taṃ ca prekṣya gururdutam | pādau prāsārayat prājñastataḥ so'gāttadantare ||5|| akṣatāṃgastataḥ sūriḥ, kruddhastena kukarmaṇā | bhāvī pāto'ṅganāyāste, re ! pāpeti śaśāpa tam ||6|| gurogiraṃ mṛṣākartuṃ, kṣullaḥ kṣudramatiḥ sa tu | gatvā nirmānuṣāraṇye, tasthau girinadītaṭe ||7|| sa tatrātāpanāsevī, tapastepe sudustapam | pāraṇāṃ cādhvagādibhyo, māsapakṣādinā vyadhāt ||8|| athāyātāsu varṣāsu, taruṇāṃbudakāmukaiḥ | apūryatārṇavānītairnadīveśyāḥ payodhanaiḥ ||9|| enaṃ kūlaṃkaṣākūlaṃ, nikaṣāsaṃsthitaṃ munim | mānaiṣīdaṃbupuro'bdhi, duṣṭo vāha ivāṭavīm ||10|| iti dhyātvā nadīdevyā, sā'nyato'vāhi vāhinī | kūlavālaka ityūcustatastaṃ saṃyataṃ-janāḥ ||11|| (yugmam) itaśca śreṇiko rājā pure rājagṛhe'bhavat | nandā ca cillaṇā cāstāṃ, mahiṣyau tasya mañjule ||12|| tatrādyāyāḥ suto jajñe'bhayo'nyasyāḥ sunandanāḥ | kūṇikahallāvihallāsrayo'bhūvanmanoharāḥ ||13|| kālādyā bhrātarasteṣāṃ, daśā''san bhinnamātṛkāḥ | mātrā satrā'bhayastatrā''dade dīkṣāṃ jināntike ||14|| pravrajatī tadā nandā, dadau hallavihallayoḥ | kuṃḍaladvitayaṃ devadattaṃ kṣaumayugaṃ tathā ||15|| rājyaṃ jyeṣṭhasya bhāvīti, dhyātvā rājāpyadāttayoḥ | gaṃdhadvīpaṃ secanakaṃ, hāraṃ ca tridaśārpitam ||16|| kūṇikaste ca kālādyāḥ, duṣṭā baddhvā'nyadā nṛpam | vibhajyādadire rājyaṃ, rājā tvajani kūṇikaḥ ||17|| rājyādikaṃ dadau hārādikaṃ tāto'nayoḥ svayam | iti rājyavibhāgaṃ te, nāduīllāvihallayoḥ ||18|| kārāstha eva pitari, viṣaṃ bhuktvānyadā mṛte | sānutāpo ratiṃ prāya, pure tatra na kūṇikaḥ ||19|| vāsayitvā tato'nyatra, navyāṃ caṃpābhidhāṃ purīm | uvāsa vāsava iva, mahaddhiḥ kūṇiko nṛpaḥ ||20|| hārakuṃḍalavāsobhirdivyairbhūṣitabhūdhanau | gaṃdhadvīpaṃ tamārūḍhau, sāntaḥpuraparicchadau ||21|| krīḍāyai pratyahaṃ hallavihallau jagmaturnadīm | tadeti krīḍayāmāsa, tadvadhūrgandhasindhuraḥ ||22|| (yugmam) skandhe'dhyāropayatkāścicchuṇḍayā''dāya sundarīḥ | kāścinnyaveśayanmaulau, kāściddantāntareṣvadhāt ||23||

āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakam
urdhvīkṛtya kara kāścidbālikāvadviyatyadhāt | kāścidāndolayabolāmiva śuṇḍāṃ vilolayan ||24 || "kiṃ bahunā" ? yathā yathā procire tāstasmai śastāya hastine | vibhaṅgajñānavān so'pi, prāvarttata tathā tathā ||25|| tacca prekṣyādbhūtaṃ sarvo'pyevaṃ paurajano jagau | rājyaśrīphalabhoktārāvimāveva na kūṇikaḥ ||26|| tacca padmāvatī rājñī, śrutvā kūṇikabhūbhujaḥ | jātāmarṣaprakarṣeti, ciṃtayāmāsa cetasi ||27 || divyahārādinā gaṃdhahastinā cāmunā vinā | rājyaṃ na rājate prājyamapyanājyamivāśanam ||28|| tat patyā sarvamapyetadgrāhayiṣye balādapi | dhyātveti sā svamākūtaṃ, raho rājñe nyavedayat ||29|| bhūpo'vādīdādadāno, bhrātrorapi ramāmaham | kākādapi nikṛṣṭaḥ syāṃ, tadalaṃ vārttayānayā ||30|| niṣiddhāpi nṛpeṇaivaṃ, nāgrahaṃ taṃ mumoca sā | bālānāmiva bālānāmāgraho hi bhavedbalī ||31|| prapede tadviśāmīśastatpremavivaśo'tha saḥ | akāryamapi kiṃ prāyo, na kurvanti ? vaśāvaśāḥ ! ||32|| yaduktam “suvaṃśajo'pyakṛtyāni, kurute preritaḥ sriyā | snehalaṃ dadhi majāti, paśya maṃthānako na kim ?" ||33|| hārādikaṃ nṛpo'nyedhurbhrātarau tāvayācata | vihāya dūrataḥ snehamunmatta iva cīvaram ||34|| tāvūcatustātadattaṃ, taddātuṃ nārhamāvayoḥ | tathāpi dadvahe rājan ! rājyāṃśaṃ ceddadāsi nau ||35 || ityuktaḥ pārthivastābhyāṃ, kaṣāyakaluṣo'vadat | vātsalyādavimṛśyaiva, tātenādāyi kiṃ tataḥ ? ||36|| kiṃ cārhati mamaivedaṃ sāraṃ ratnacatuṣṭyam | ratnāni rājagāminītyucyate hi jaḍairapi ||37|| tatastaddīyatāṃ nocedagrahīṣyāmi balādapi | omityuktvā tato hallavihallo jagmaturgraham ||38|| dadhyatuśceti rājño'sya, śobhano nāyamāśayaḥ | vāsaḥ sasarpadhāmnīva, neha śreyāṃstadāvayoḥ ||39|| dhyātvetyādāya hārādi, sarvaṃ tau saparicchadau | caṃpāyāṃ niśi nirgatya, vaiśālī jagmatuḥ pūrīm ||40|| mātāmahāya tau tatra, ceṭakāya mahībhuje | sarvaṃ svodaṃtamāvedyāsthātāṃ tatkṛtagauravau ||4|| kūṇikastūbhayabhraṣṭatayā cintāñcitastataḥ | vaiśālyāṃ tau gatau jñātvā, preṣīdūtaṃ vacasvinam ||42|| gatvā dūto'pi vaiśālī, natvā ceṭakamityavak | rājan ! kūṇikarājastvāṃ, mayā vijñapayatyadaḥ ||43|| gajādiratnānyādāyāgatāviha kumārako | preṣaṇīyau drutaṃ pūjyastulyairmayi tayostathā ||44 || tau cennāgacchatastarhi, preṣyaṃ sadyo dvipādikam | no cedvo bhavitā bhūyānāyāso'nuśayāvahaḥ ||45|| atheti ceṭako'vocaḍhūta ! tvaṃ brūhi kūṇikam | tātadattā bhrātṛlakṣmīrgrahītuṃ yujyate na te ||46|| rakṣyante śaraṇāyātāḥ, kiṃ cānye'pi manasvibhiḥ | taddauhitrau kathaṃkāraṃ, preṣaṇīyāvimau mayā ? ||47||

109
dauhitratvātsamānā me, bhavanto yadyapi trayaḥ | nyāyitvādāśritatvācca, viśiṣyete tathā'pyamū ||48|| satyapyevaṃ dāpayāmi, dvipādi tava tuṣṭaye | dadāsi yadi rājyāṃśaṃ, nyāyopetaṃ tvametayoḥ ||49|| tacceṭakavaco gatvā, dūtaḥ svasvāmine'vadat | krodhāmātastataḥ so'pi, yātrābhaṃbhāmavīvadat ||50 || kālādyairdaśabhiryukto, bhrātṛbhirnijasannibhaiḥ | trayastriṃśatsahasrāśvarathasindhurasaṃyutaḥ ||51|| trayatriṃśatkoṭipattikalitaścalitastataḥ | kūṇiko'cchādayatsainyairbhuvaṃ dyāṃ ca rajobharaiḥ ||52|| (yugmam) tato yuto'ṣṭādaśabhirbhūpairmukuṭadhāribhiḥ | saptapañcāśatsahasra-rathahastihayānvitaḥ ||53|| ceṭako'pyabhyagātsaptapañcāśatkoṭipattiyuk | svadeśasīmni sainyai ca, vārdhivyūhamacīkarat ||54|| (yugmam) kūṇiko'pyāgatastatra, tāyavyūhaṃ vyadhābale | nyadhāccamūpatitve ca, kālaṃ kālamivotkaṭam ||55 || vyaktavīragaṇonmuktapṛṣaktācchāditāmbare | ārebhāte raṇaṃ bhīmamubhe api tato bale ||56|| niṣādinā vyadhāyuddhaṃ, niṣādī rathinā rathī | sādinā ca samaṃ sādī, padātistu padātinā ||57|| kālī jayārthamuttālaḥ, samaṃ ceṭakasenayā | yudhyamānastadā rājñaśceṭakasyāntike yayau ||58|| dinaṃ pratyekaviśikhamuktisandhādharastataḥ | ceṭako divyabāṇena taṃ kṛtāntātithiṃ vyadhāt ||59|| caṃpāpaterbalaṃ śokākulaṃ draṣṭumivākṣamaḥ | tadā bhānurjagāmāstaṃ, viśaśrāma tato raṇaḥ ||60 || dvitīye'pyahni sainyābhyāmārabdhe saṃgare punaḥ | mahākālaṃ kūṇikasya, senānyaṃ ceṭako'vadhīt ||61|| anyeṣvapi hateṣvevaṃ, tenāṣṭasvaṣṭabhirdinaiḥ | śokākrānto'śokacandraḥ, iti cetasyacintayat ||62|| rājño'syā'jānatā divyāṃ, śaktimetāṃ hahā mayā | mudhaiva prāpitāḥ kālaṃ, kālādyā bhrātaro daśa ||63|| tadadyāpi suraṃ kañcidārādhyāmuṃ jayāmyarim | no cedbhaviṣyāmyanugasteṣāmahamapi drutam ||64 || dhyātveti devatādhyāne, sthitaṃ taṃ vihitāṣṭamam | prāgjanmasaṃgatau śakracamarendrāvupeyatuḥ ||65|| kimicchasīti jalpantau, tāvityūce'tha kūṇikaḥ| yadi tuṣṭau yuvāṃ sadyaśceṭako māryatāṃ tadā ||66|| ūce śakraḥ sadharmāṇaṃ, ceṭakaṃ na hi hanmyaham | kariṣyāmyaṅgarakṣāṃ tu, tava bhaktivaśaṃvadaḥ ||67|| mahāśilākaṃṭakāhvarathādimuśale raṇe | camarendrastvadāttasmai, vairinirjayakāraṇe ||68 || tatrādye vairiṣu kṣiptāvapi karkarakaṇṭako | mahāśilāmahāśastre, iva syātāṃ mṛtipradau ||69|| yuddhe dvitīye tu rathamuśale bhramakaṃ vinā | bhrāmyataḥ parito vairipakṣapeṣaṇatatpare ||70|| tatastuṣṭo yayau duṣṭaḥ, kūṇikaḥ samarājiram | mamaṃtha vārcivyūhaṃ ca, maṃthācala ivodadhim ||71||

tamāpatantaṃ saṃhartuṃ, sāmarṣaśceṭako nṛpaḥ | mumocākarṇamākṛṣya, sadyo divyaṃ śilīmukham ||72|| kūṇikasya puro vajra-kavaca vajrabhṛddadhau | pṛṣṭhe tu lauhaṃ sannāhaṃ, tadā tasyāsureśvaraḥ ||73 || tasmin divye śare vajravarmaṇā skhalite'ntarā | bhaṭāśceṭakarājasya, menire sukṛtakṣayam ||74 || satyasandho dvitīyaṃ tu, ceṭako nāmucaccharam | dvitīye'pyahni tadbāṇaṃ, tathaivā'jani niṣphalam ||75 || ādye raṇe ṣaṇṇavatirlakṣā nṛṇāṃ yayuḥ kṣayam | lakṣāścaturaśītiśca, dvitīye tu mahāhave ||76|| teṣveko varuṇaḥ śrāddho, nāganaptā yayau divam | tatsuhṛdbhadrako nṛtvaṃ, tiryaktvaṃ narakaṃ pare ||77|| ityanvahaṃ jāyamāne, samare sainyayostayoḥ | yātsu svasvapuraṃ naṃṣṭvā'ṣṭādaśasvapi rājasu ||78|| praṇaśya ceṭakoīṃśo, vaiśālīmaviśatpurīm | rurodha sarvatastāṃ ca, kūṇikaḥ prabalairbalaiḥ ||79|| (yugmam) atha secanakārūḍhau, kūṇikasyākhilaṃ balam | upadudruvaturhallavihallau tau pratikṣapam ||80 || avaskandapradānāyā''gataṃ taṃ gandhahastinam | na haṃtumanugantuṃ vā, tatsainye ko'pyabhūt prabhuḥ ||81|| tanmārge mantriṇāṃ buddhyā, kūṇiko'cīkarattataḥ | khātikāṃ jvaladaṃgāra-pūrṇāṃ parṇādyavastṛtām ||82|| rātrau tatrāgataḥ so'tha, gajo jñātvā vibhaṃgataḥ | jvaladaṃgāragartī tāṃ, nunno'pi na puro'calat ||83|| tāvūcatustataḥ khinnau, kumārāviti taṃ dvipam | parebhyaḥ kiṃ bibheṣi ? tvaṃ, yatpuro na calasyare ! ||84 || varaṃ śvā poṣitaḥ śaśvatsvāminaṃ yo'nuvarttate | kṛtaghno'hiriva svāmikṛtyanāśī bhavānnatu ||85|| ityuktaḥ sindhurastābhyāṃ, svāmibhaktadhurandharaḥ | gṛhītvā śuṃḍayā skandhāttau balenodatārayat ||86|| svayaṃ tu tasyāṃ ga yāṃ, datvā jhaṃpāṃ vipadya ca | ādye'gānnarake dhairyamaho tasya paśorapi ! ||87|| tadvīkṣya sānutāpau tau, kumārāviti dadhyatuḥ | krodhāndhābhyāṃ dhigāvābhyāṃ, kimakāryamidaṃ kṛtam ! ||88 || kṛte yasya kṛto deśatyāgo bhrātā ripūkṛtaḥ | asmiṃśca vyasanāṃbhodhau, kṣipto mātāmaho'pyaho ! ||89|| nihatya taṃ gajaṃ yuktaṃ, naiva jīvitumāvayoḥ | jīvāvaścedvīradevaśiṣyībhūyaiva nānyathā ||90 || (yugmam) tadā śāsanadevyā tau, nītau vīrajināntike | pravrajyaikādaśāṅgāni, sudhiyau peṭhatuḥ kramāt ||11||. guṇaratnaṃ tapastaptvā, saṃlikhya ca samādhinā | hallaḥ suro jayante'bhūdvihallastvaparājite ||12|| gṛhīte'pi vrate tābhyāṃ, purīmādātumakṣamaḥ | vyadhātsandhāmityaśokacandro nistandravikramaḥ ||13|| kharayuktahalairenāṃ, nagarī na khanāmi cet | tadā tyajāmyahamasūn, bhṛgupātādinā dhruvam ||94 || tathāpi tāṃ purīṃ bhaṃktu-manīśe śreṇikatmaje | kramāt khedaṃ gate devī, kāpītyūce nabhaḥsthitā ||95 || “samaṇe jadi kūlavālae, māgadhiaṃ gaṇiaṃgamissae | rāyā ya asogacandae, vesāliṃ nagaliṃ gahissae" ||96||

khaṇḍa:-2 dṛṣṭāntasamuccayaḥ
tanniśamya nṛpastuṣṭastāṃ veśyāmevamādiśet | ihānaya patīkṛtya, bhadre ! tvaṃ kūlavālakam ||97 || tatprapadyābhavanmāyāśrāvikā sā paṇāṅganā | muneḥ kuto'pi tatrasthamajñāsīttaṃ ca saṃyatam ||98 || tatrāraṇye tato gatvā, taṃ ca natvā yathāvidhi | iti sā dambhinī proce, vacanairamṛtopamaiḥ ||99|| nantuṃ tīrthāni caṃpātaḥ, prabho ! prasthitayā mayā | sarvatīrthādhikāḥ pūjyapādā diṣṭyā'tra vanditāḥ ||100 || matpātheyāttadādāya, bhikṣāmanugṛhāṇa mām | tayeti sādaraṃ proktastatsārthe sādhurapyagāt ||101|| tasyādānmiśritadravyān, sāmodā sāpi modakān | tadbhakṣaṇādatīsārastasyāsīdatidussaha: ||102|| tataḥ sā tatra tadvaiyāvṛtyadambhena tasthuṣī | muhurmunimupāsarpatsarpavatkuṭilāśayā ||103 || udvarttanādinā svāṅgasparśa cācīkaranmuhuḥ | bheṣajāntaradānācca, tamullāghaṃ vyadhācchanaiḥ ||104 || tatkaṭākṣasarāgoktiśarīrasparśavibhramaiḥ | munermano'calattasya, strīsaṅge kvanu ? tatsthiram ||105 || tyaktavratastatastasyāmāsaktaḥ so'bhavattathā | yathā tayā vinā sthātuṃ, nābhūtkṣaṇamapi prabhuḥ ||106 || tadvaśaḥ kūṇikopāntaṃ, tato'gātkūlavālakaḥ | satkṛtya kūṇiko'pyevamabravīttaṃ munibruvam ||107 || mahātman ! gṛhyate neyamupāyairbahubhiḥ purī | tatastadgrahaṇopāyaṃ, vidhehi dhiṣaṇānidhe ! ||108|| tato daivajñaveṣeṇa, vaiśālī praviveśa saḥ | śrīsuvratārhataḥ stupaṃ, bhramaṃstatra dadarśa ca ||109|| dadhyau ca nūnamasyāsti, pratiṣṭhālagnamuttamam | abhaṃgā tanmahimnāsau, nagarī nanu varttate ||110 || kathaṃ mayā pātanīyastadasāviti cintayan | apṛcchayata purīrodhākuleneti janena saḥ ||111|| vada daivajña ! vaiśālyā, rodho yāsyatyasau kadā ? | khinnāḥ smo yadvayaṃ kārāvāsenevāmunā bhṛśam ||112 || muditaḥ sa tato'vādīt, pāpapaṅkekaśūkaraḥ | stūpo'sau yāvadatra syāttāvadudveṣṭanaṃ kva ? vaḥ ||113|| tallokāḥ ! yadyayaṃ stūpo, yuṣmābhiḥ pātyate drutam | tadā'payāti niyataṃ purīrodho'dhunaiva hi ||114 || prokto dhūrtena teneti, bālavabāliśo janaḥ taṃ stūpaṃ bhaktumārebhe, dhūtaiḥ ko na hi vañcyate ||115 | | stūpe ca bhaktumārabdhe, gatvā māgadhikādhipaḥ | sadyo'pāsārayaccaṃpādhīśaṃ krośadvayaṃ tataḥ ||116 || tataḥ sa pratyayairlokaḥ, stūpe mūlātprapātite | vyādhuṭya kūṇiko'vikṣat, purīṃ sabalavāhanaḥ ||117 || tadā cānaśanaṃ kṛtvā, smṛtvā pañcanamaskriyāḥ | ceṭako nyapatat kūpe, baddhā'yaḥputrikāṃ gale ||118 || tadā tatrāsanāsthairyādāgatya dharaṇādhipaḥ | sādharmikaṃ tamādāya, nināya bhavane nije ||119 || vidhāyārādhanāṃ samyak, prapālyānaśanaṃ ca tat | tatrasthaḥ prāpya pañcatvaṃ, ceṭakastridivaṃ yayau ||120 || itaśca sujyeṣṭhāsūnudauhitraśceṭakaprabhoḥ | vaiśālyāmāyayau daivāttadā satyakikhecaraḥ ||121 ||

āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakam
mātāmahaprajāṃ sarvāṃ, lūṃṭyamānāṃ sa rakṣitum | nināya nīlavatyadrau, drutamutpāṭya vidyayā ||122 || kopāviṣṭaḥ kūṇiko'tha, tāṃ purīṃ yuktarāsabhaiḥ | kheṭayitvā halaistīrṇapratijñaḥ svapurīṃ yayau ||123|| kūlavālakanāmā tu, mṛtvāgānnarakaṃ kudhīḥ | uddhṛtastu tato'nante, saṃsāre paryaṭiṣyati ||124 ||
kūlavālakamuneriva duḥkhāvāptirevamavinītamuneḥ syāt |
dhṛṣṭatāṃ tadapahāya suśiṣyaiḥ, sadgurovinaya eva vidheyaḥ ||125 || 4. krodhadhyāne triṣaṣṭiśalākāpuruṣacarite gośālakākhyānakaḥ | ityākhyāya tato nāthaḥ śrāvastī viharan yayau | tasyāṃ ca samavāsārṣīdudyāne koṣṭhakābhidhe ||1|| tasyāṃ prāgāgatastejoleśyāhatavirodhakaḥ | aṣṭāṃganimittajñānajñātalokamanogataḥ ||2|| ajino'pi jinaśabdamātmanā saṃprakāśayan | hālāhalākuṃbhakāryā gośālo'vasadāpaṇe ||3|| (yugmam) tasya cāhanniti khyātiṃ loka ākarṇya mugdhadhīḥ | upetyopetya vidadhe nirantaramupāsanam ||4|| itaśca samaye prāpte gautamaḥ svāmyanujñayā | prāviśat puri bhikṣārthaṃ cikīrṣuḥ ṣaṣṭhapāraṇam ||5|| gośālo'trāsti sarvajño'rhannityākarṇya tatra ca | gautamaḥ saviṣādo'gādāttabhikṣo'ntike prabhoḥ ||6|| yathāvat pāraṇaṃ kṛtvā gautamaḥ samaye prabhum | paśyatāṃ pauralokānāmapṛcchat svacchadhīriti ||7|| svāminnagaryāmatasyāṃ vyāharantyakhilā janāḥ | sarvajña iti gośālaṃ kimetad ghaṭate na vā ? ||8|| athākhyadbhagavāneṣa sūnurmakhasya maṃkhaleḥ | ajino'pi jinamanyo gośālaḥ kapaṭālayaḥ ||9|| mayaiva dīkṣitaścāyaṃ śikṣāṃ ca grāhito mayā | mithyātvaṃ pratipanno me sarvajño naiṣa gautama ! ||10|| tattu svāmivacaḥ śrutvā paurāḥ puryāmitastataḥ | evaṃ babhāṣire'nyo'nyaṃ catvareṣu trikeṣu ca ||11|| haṃ ho arhanihāyāto vīrasvāmī vadatyadaḥ | gośālo maṃkhalisuto mithyā sarvajñamānyasau ||12|| januśrutyā tataḥ śrutvā gośāla: kālasarpavat | āpūryamāṇaḥ kopena tasthāvājīvakāvṛtaḥ ||13|| itaśca svāminaḥ śiṣya ānandaḥ sthavirāgraṇīḥ | ṣaṣṭhapāraṇakaṃ kartuṃ bhikṣārthaṃ prāviśat puri ||14|| hālāhalāgṛhāsīno gośālastatpradeśagam | ānandamunimāhūya sādhikṣepamado'vadat ||15||
bho ānanda ! tavācāryo lokāt satkāramātmanaḥ |
icchan vīraḥ sabhānvakṣaṃ māṃ tiraskurutetarām ||16|| maṃkhaputramanarhantamasarvajñaṃ ca vakti mām | tejoleśyāṃ na me vetti vipakṣadahanakṣamām ||17|| bhasmarāśīkariṣyāmi tamahaṃ saparicchadam | tvāmevaikaṃ vimokṣyāmi dṛṣṭānto'tra niśamyatām ||18||

avasaraḥ prasaraśca saṃvādaḥ kārakastathā| bhalano vaṇijo'bhūvan kṣemilāyāṃ purā puri ||19|| bhāṇḍerāpūrya śakaṭānniryayuste vaṇijyayā | yāntaśca nirjalāraṇyaṃ viviśuḥ kiñcidadhvani ||20|| te pañcāpi tṛṣākrāntā marumārgagatā iva | jalaṃ gaveṣayāmāsuḥ paryanṭato mahāṭavīm ||21|| bhrāmyannavasaraḥ pañcaśikharaṃ vāmalūrakam | dadarśādarśayatteṣāṃ caturṇāṃ suhṛdāmapi ||22|| te tasya pūrvaśikharaṃ saṃbhūyā'sphoṭayan drutam | tasmāñca lebhire vāri tatpītvā susthatāṃ yayuḥ ||23|| athoce prasaro'pācyaśṛṃgamapyasya dīryatām | lapsyāmahe nūnamito vastvanyadapi kiṃcana ||24 || athābravīdavasaraḥ khananaṃ nāsya yujyate | utthāsyatyahiretasmānnākuroko hi bhoginām ||25|| avādīdatha saṃvādo visaṃvādo'pyabhūdiha | yatparisphoṭitātpūrvaśikharānnāhirutthitaḥ ||26|| bhūyo'pyavasaro'vocadaivādiha payo'bhavat | athoce kārako daivād drammā iha nu bhāvinaḥ ||27|| ityuditvā tat khanituṃ samārabhata kārakaḥ | mataṃ mamedaṃ netyuktvā gantrīmavasaro yayau ||28 | | babhāṣe bhalano yāto'vasaro yadi yātu tat | vinā'pyamuṃ khaniṣyāma iti sarve'pi te'khanan ||29|| khātāttasmānnākuśṛṃgāt sadyo drammā viniryayuḥ | vibhajyāvasaravarṇaṃ catvāro jagṛhuśca tān ||30|| cakhnuścaturthamapi te śṛṃgaṃ svarṇaṃ ca lebhire | tato rajatamapyaujjhan suvarṇādānalobhataḥ ||31|| ratnāni pañcame śṛṃge bhaviṣyantīti buddhitaḥ | cakhnustadapi lobhāndhā lābhāllobho hi vardhate ||32|| atyantamathitādabdheḥ kālakūṭa ivotkaṭaḥ | tasmācchaṃgāt khanyamānāduttasthau dṛgviṣoragaḥ ||33|| valmīkāgrasthita; so'hiḥ prāk sūryaṃ prekṣya tāndṛśā | bhasmīcakāra caturaḥ sagantrīvṛṣabhānapi ||34 | | nirlobha ityavasaraṃ sagantrīvṛṣamapyatha | tasyāherdevyadhiṣṭhātrī prāpayat sthānamīpsitam ||35 || dhakṣyāmi tvadguruṃ sarpo'dhākṣīttāṃścaturo yathā | mokṣyāmi tvāmahaṃ so'hirmumocāvasaraṃ yathā ||36|| tato'samāptabhikṣārtha evā''nando yayau prabhum | gośāloktaṃ tadācakhyāvapṛcchecceti śaṃkitaḥ ||37 || bhasmarāśīkariṣyāmītyuktaṃ gośālakena yat | unmattabhāṣitaṃ tat kiṃ tatkartumathavā kṣamaḥ ? ||38 || athācacakṣe bhagavānarhadbhyaḥ so'nyataḥ kṣamaḥ | arhatāmapi santāpamātraṃ kuryādanāryadhīḥ ||39|| tadgatvā gautamādīnāṃ śaṃsedaṃ te yathā hi tam | ihāgataṃ nodanayā dharmyayāpi nudanti na ||40|| teṣāṃ gatvā''khyadānandastadā gośālako'pi hi | tatrā''gāt svāmino'gre cāvasthāya vyabravīditi||41|| bhoḥ kāśyapa ! vadasyevaṃ gośālo maṃkhale: sutaḥ | antevāsī mametyādi tanmṛṣā bhāṣitaṃ tava ||42||

gośālastava yaḥ śiṣyaḥ sa hi śuklābhijātikaḥ | dharmadhyānasthito mṛtvā tridaśeṣūdapadyata ||43|| taddehe'sminnupasargaparīṣahasahe'viśam | udāyanāmā'hamṛṣiḥ parityajya nijaṃ vapuḥ ||44|| tato māmaparijñāya gośālaṃ maṃkhaleḥ sutam | svaśiṣyaṃ kathamākhyāsi na khalvasi gururmama ||45|| svāmyathoce yathā''rakṣaiḥ kramyamāṇo malimlucaḥ | gartaṃ durgaṃ vanaṃ vā'pi svāntardhānamavāpnuvan ||46|| ūrṇālomnā śaṇalomnā tūlāṃśena tṛṇena vā | manyate'ntaradattenātmānamāvṛtamalpadhīḥ ||47 || evaṃ tvamapi gośālo'nanyo'pyākhyān svamanyathā | kimarthaṃ bhāṣase'līkaṃ sa evāsyaparo na hi ||48|| evaṃ svāmigirā kruddho gośālo'pyabravīt prabhum | adya bhraṣṭo'si naṣṭo'si na bhavasyeṣa kāśyapa ! ||49|| sarvajñaśiṣyaḥ sarvānubhūtiṇurvanurāgataḥ | akṣamastadvacaḥ soḍhuṃ gośālakamabhāṣata ||50|| guruṇā dīkṣito'nena śikṣito'syamunaiva hi | ninuṣe hetunā kena gośāla ! tvaṃ sa eva hi ||51|| atha gośālakaḥ kopāttejoleśyāmanāhatām | sarvānubhūtaye'muñcad dṛgjvālāmiva dṛgviṣaḥ ||52|| nirdahyamānaḥ sarvānubhūtigośālaleśyayā | śubhadhyānaparo mṛtvā sahasrāre suro'bhavat ||53 || gośālo'pi hi tatkālaṃ svaleśyāśaktigarvitaḥ | nirbhartsayitumārebhe bhagavantaṃ punaḥ punaḥ ||54|| svāmiśiṣyaḥ sunakṣatrastamatha svāminindakam | gurubhaktyā'nuśāsti sma bhṛśaṃ sarvānubhūtivat ||55 || gośālamuktayā tejoleśyayā prajvalattanuḥ | prabhuṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtyā''dāya bhūyo vratāni ca ||56|| ālocyātha pratikramya kṣamayitvā'khilānmunīn | sunakṣatramunirmṛtvā'cyutakalpe suro'bhavat ||57|| (yugmam) jitakāśī ca gośālastato'tiparuṣākṣaram | samākrośanijagade svāminā karuṇājuṣā ||58|| dīkṣitaḥ śikṣitaścāsi śrutabhāk ca mayā kṛtaḥ | mamaivāvarṇavādī tvaṃ ko'yaṃ te dhīviparyayaḥ ? ||59|| svāminā svayamityukto gośālaḥ kupito bhṛśam | upetya kiñcidamucattejoleśyāṃ prabhuṃ prati ||60 || svāminyaprabhaviṣṇuḥ sā mahāvātyeva parvate | prabhuṃ pradakṣiṇīcakre bhaktibhāganuhāriṇī ||61|| saṃtāpamānaṃ svāmyaṃge'bhūttejoleśyayā tayā | tīrakakṣodbhaveneva dāvena saridambhasaḥ ||62|| akāryāya prayuktā dhiganeneti krudheva sā | tejoleśyā nivṛtyāṃge gośālasyāviśadvalāt ||63 || tayā'ntardahyamāno'pi gośālo dhāya॑māśritaḥ | bhagavantaṃ mahāvīramabhyadhattaivamuddhataḥ ||64 || mattejoleśyayā dhvastaḥ ṣaṇmāsānte hi kāśyapa ! | pittajvaraparābhūtaśchadmastho'pi vipatsyase ||65 || svāmyathovāca gośāla ! mṛṣā te vāgahaṃ yataḥ | anyāni ṣoḍaśābdāni vihariṣyāmi kevalī ||66||

khaṇḍa:-2 dṛṣṭāntasamuccayaḥ
117
śrāvastīmālikhastatra śiṣyāḥ śapathamuktaye | tathā gośālamākarṣan kurvanto ghoṣaṇādikam ||115 || tato niṣkramayāmāsustadgośālakalevaram | ṛddhyā mahatyā tacchiṣyāścakruśca jvalanātithim ||116||
triṣaṣṭhi0 parva-10 śloka-354 taḥ 469] 4. krodhadhyāne śrīuttarādhyayanabṛhadvṛttikathitaḥ śrīskandakācāryahantṛpālakasampradāyaḥ |
sāvatthīe nayarīe jiyasattūrāyā, dhāriṇī devī, tīse putto khaṃdao ṇāma kumāro, tassa bhagiṇī puraṃdarajasā, sā kuṃbhakārakaḍe nayare daṃḍagī nāma rāyā tassa dinnā, tassa ya daṃḍakissa raṇṇo pālago ṇāma maruto purohito | annayā sāvatthīe muṇisuvvayasāmī titthayaro samosario, parisā niggayā, khaṃdato'vi niggato, dhamma soccā sāvago jāo | annayāṃ so pālakamaruto dūyattāe āgato sāvatthiṃ nayariṃ, atthāṇimajhe sāhūṇaṃ avaṇṇaṃ vayamāṇo khaṃdaeṇaṃ nippiṭṭhapasiṇavāgaraṇo kato, patosamāvaṇṇo, tappabhiī ceva khaṃdagassa chiddāṇi cārapurisehiṃ maggāviṃto viharai, jjāva khaṃdago paṃcajaṇasaehiṃ kumārolagaehiṃ saddhiṃ muṇisuvvaya sāmisagāse pavvatito, bahusuto jāto, tāṇi ceva se paṃca sayāṇi sīsattāe aṇuṇṇāyāṇi | annayā khaṃdao sāmimāpucchai-vañcāmi bhagiṇīsagāsaṃ, sāmiṇā bhaṇiyaṃ-uvasaggo māraṇaṃtito, bhaṇai-ārāhagā virāhagā vā ? sāmiṇā bhaṇiyaṃ-savve ārāhagā tumaṃ mottuṃ, so bhaṇai-laṭuṃ, jadi ettiyā ārāhagā, gao kuṃbhakārakaḍaṃ, marueṇa jahiṃ ujjāṇe ṭhio tahiṃ āuhāṇi ṇūmiyāṇi, rāyā buggāhio-jahā esa kumāro parīsahaparāito eeṇa uvāeṇa tumaṃ mārittā rajjaṃ gihihitti, jadi te vipañcato ujjāṇaṃ paloehi, āuhāṇi olaiyāṇi diṭṭhāṇi, te baṃdhiūṇa tassa ceva purohiyassa samappiyā, teṇa savve purisajaṃteṇa pīliyā, tehiṃ samma ahiyāsiyaṃ, tesiṃ kevalaṇāṇaṃ uppaṇṇaṃ siddhā ya | khaṃdato'vi pāse dhario, lohiyacirikkāhiṃ bharijjaṃto savvato pacchā jaṃte pīlito ṇidāṇaṃ kāūṇa aggikumāresu uvavaṇṇo | taṃpi se rayaharaṇaṃ ruhiralittaṃ purisahatthotti kāuṃ giddhehiṃ puraṃdarajasāte purato pāḍiyaṃ, sāvi taddivasaṃ adhitiṃ karei jahā sādhū ṇa dīsaṃti, taṃ ca ṇāe diṭuṃ, pañcabhinnāo ya kaṃbalo, ṇisijjāto chiṇṇāto, tāe ceva diṇṇo, tāe nāyaṃ-jahā te māriyā, tāe khisito rāyā-pāva ! viṇaṭṭho'si, tāe ciṃtiyaṃ-pavvayāmi, devehiṃ muṇisuvvayasagāsaṃ nīyā, teṇavi deveṇa ṇagaraṃ daṭuṃ sajaṇavvayaṃ, ajjavi daṃḍagāraṇṇaṃti bhaṇṇai | 4. krodhadhyāne puṣpamālāyāṃ namucimantrī | kurujaṇavayamajjhagayaṃ asthi puraṃ hatthiṇāuraṃ nāma | ṭhāṇaṭṭhāṇanivesiyasuvannarāsīhiṃ jattha sayā ||1||
avagayaparamatthāṇavi bahukaṇayagirīṇa jāyae saṃkā | puṇaravi sā viṇiyattai dāṇāvasarammi tavvigame||2||

bahugayaghaḍacchaleṇaṃ sāisayamahīharattavijayaṃtaṃ | sevaṃti jaṃ athaddhaṃ kulagiriṇo vihiyabahurūvā ||3|| so paumottararāyā taṃ pālai jassa sayalaguṇakaliyā | sāvayadhammammi daḍhā jālā nāmeṇa varabhajjā ||4|| sīhasumiṇaparikāhio viṇhukumārotti tāṇa paḍhamasuo | caudasasumiṇapisuṇio bīo putto mahāpaumo ||5|| viṇhū tattha nirīho iyaro u samīhae tao ranā | juvarāyatte ṭhavio lahuo'vi hu so mahāpaumo ||6|| etto ujjeṇīe sirimuṇisuvvayajiṇassa varasīso | suvvayanāmāyario samāgao kahavi viharaṃto ||7|| siridhammo nāma nivo vaṃdaṇaheuṃ samāgao tassa | namuī ya nāma maṃtī teṇa samaṃ āgao duṭṭho ||8|| teṇa vitaṃḍāvāo āḍhatto uvasamappahāṇīe | paḍiṇīotti ya kaliuṃ moṇeṇa ṭhio khaṇaṃ sūri ||9|| teṇa bhaṇi kimeso āyario jāṇae balīho ? | guruparibhavamasahaṃto bhaṇai tao khuḍao ekko ||10|| niya'guruyayāe suyaṇaṃ khalo mahaṃtaṃpi gaṇai asamatthaṃ | niyayasahāvasaricchaṃ jeṇa jaṇo pecchaḍ paraṃpi ||11|| ciṭuṃtu tāva nīsesasatyapāraṃgayā ime sūrī | jai tuccha ! kāi sattī tā bhaṇasu maevi saha tāva ||12|| aha āḍhatto bhaṇiuṃ niruttaro khuḍḍaeṇa so vihio | tā sāhūsu pauṭṭho vilakkhavayaṇo gao sagihaṃ ||13|| asamaṃjasāiṃ subahuṃ ciṃtaṃto uṭṭhiūṇa rayaṇīe | sāhūṇa haṇaṇaheuṃ satthaṃ gahiuṃ gao tattha ||14|| sammadiṭṭhīe devayāe to thaṃmio tahiṃ ceva | diṭṭho pabhāyasamae sapauraloeṇa naravaeṇā ||15|| to āuṭṭho loo bhattiṃ sāhūṇa kuṇai savisesaṃ | mukko namuīvi ya devayāe lajjāe nīhariuṃ ||16|| avayāratthaṃ sāhūṇa ciṃtayaṃto uvāyalakkhāiṃ | gaṃtūṇa hatthiṇaure olaggai so mahāpaumaṃ ||17|| eso rāyā jāo kāhī maha vaṃchiyaṃti buddhīe | maṃtittaṃ paḍivanno ciṭṭhai tasseva pāsammi ||18|| etto ya mahāpaumassa saṃtiyaṃ bhaṃjae sayaladesaṃ | siṃhabalo nāma nivo baleṇa duggassa koṭṭassa ||19|| so baṃdhiūṇa gahio buddhīऍ namuiṇā tao tuṭṭho | dei varaṃ kumaro se dejjasu samayammi so bhaṇai ||20|| aha annayā karāvai jālādevī rahaṃ jiṇāyayaṇe | tīe savakkī lacchī u kārae baṃbharahametto ||21|| baṃbharaho nīharihī paḍhamaṃ lacchīऍ pabhaṇio rāyā | taṃ souṃ jālā'viya kuṇai paiṇṇaṃ imaṃ tatto ||22|| giṇhāmi aṇasaṇamahaṃ jai paḍhamaṃ jiṇaraho na nīharai | paumuttareṇa rannā tao niruddhā rahā do'vi ||23|| māūṇaṃ avamāṇaṃ taṃ mannato tao mahāpaumo | nīhario rayaṇīe egāgī aḍavimaṇupatto ||24|| tattha ya hiṃḍaṃteṇaṃ patteṇaṃ siṃdhunaṃdaṇe nayare | pariṇīyaṃ kaṇṇāyaṃ mahaseṇanivassa sayamegaṃ ||25|| vegavaīe vijjāharīe tatto ya avahareūṇaṃ | veyaḍḍammi nagavare sūrodayapuravare neuṃ ||26||
