āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakama
cintitamanena svapāpataruphalamidaṃ, tat kiṃ dārasaṅgraheṇa ? karomi tadunmūlane yatnamiti sañcintyāṭatā tena dṛṣṭaḥ kaścidācāryaḥ, dharmaṃ śrutvā pravrajitastatsakāśe | abhyastakriyākalāpo gṛhītāgamaśca pañcaśatikagacchasya gṛhītābhigraho vaiyāvṛttyaṃ kartumārabdhaḥ | kurvataśca sotsāhaṃ kṛtakṛtyamātmānaṃ bhāvayato gato bahuḥ kālaḥ |
anyadā svaḥsabhāyāṃ dhanyaḥ kṛtārtho nandiṣeṇo yo devairapi na dharmāccyāvyata iti ślāghitaḥ śakreṇa | tadaśraddhāno'vatīrṇaḥ kaściddevaḥ sthitvā sādhūpāśrayadvāre sa prāha-aṭavyāṃ glānatapasvī tiṣṭhatīti | tadākarṇya ṣaṣṭhapāraṇake gṛhītaprathamakavalastaṃ parityajya sahasā nirgato nandiṣeṇa: paripṛcchya taṃ pradeśamavasthāṃ ca praviṣṭaḥ pānakauṣadhādyarthaṃ gocare | devavihitāneṣaṇāmadīnamanasko lābhāntarāyakṣayopaśamasyotkaṭatayā nirjitya gṛhītvaiṣaṇīyaṃ pānakādikaṃ gatastaṃ pradeśam | darśito devenāśucibībhatso dhigdurmuṇḍa ! svodarabharaṇakṣaṇika ! ityādikarkaśavacanairvirāraṭyamāno glānasādhuḥ |
tato bata mayā'dhanyenā'sya mahāmunermanaḥ khedaḥ sampādita;, kathaṃ cāyaṃ praguṇo bhaviṣyatīti cintayatā tena kṣālitastaddehaḥ | dhīro bhava, karomi nirujaṃ bhavantam, āgacchopāśrayamiti samāśvāsito madhuravacanairnandiṣeṇena sa prāha - 'āḥ pāpa ! na jānīṣe me'vasthāṃ, na śaknomyahaṃ padamapi gantum | tataḥ samāropya svapṛṣṭhadeśe tamitaro gantumārabdhaḥ | devo'pi muñcati durgandhyaśucyādīni, 'dhig durātman ! vegavighātaṃ karoṣi !' ityādibhiḥ śapate ca kaṭukavākyaiḥ | itaro'pi pravardhamānatīvrataraśubhapariṇāmaḥ kathamayaṃ mahātmā svasthaḥ syāditi cintayan mithyāduṣkṛtamidānīṃ śobhanaṃ nayāmīti vadan gacchati sma | tato devastaccaritāvarjitamānasaḥ sthāne śakrasya pakṣapāta iti sañcintya māyāṃ saṃhṛtya prakaṭitadivyarūpaḥ patitaḥ pādayornivedya vṛttāntamāha ca-kiṃ mayā kartavyaṃ ? munirāha-yathāśakti dharmodyama iti | gato'sau svasthānam | muniśca pṛcchatāṃ sādhūnāṃ kathitavān yathāvṛttam |
paścādavasānakāle smṛtagṛhasthāvasthādaurbhāgyeṇa manuṣyabhave'haṃ subhago bhūyāsamiti kṛtamiti tena nidānam | gato divam, tataścayutvā jāto daśamadaśāro vasudevanāmā | prāptayauvanena ca tena bhramatā kṛtā hṛtahṛdayāḥ surasundaryaḥ svagṛhakarmāpi tyaktavatyaḥ | tato nāgarikavijñaptasamudravijayoparodhānnirgatena deśakālikayā paryaṭatā vasudhām | rūpātiśayākṣiptamanobhiḥ sarvalokairaparāparasthāneṣu nīyamānena pariṇītā bahusahasrasaṅkhyā vidyādharanarapativarakanyakāḥ, prāpto vaiṣayikasukhātirekaḥ | paścāmmilitena ca bandhubhiḥ samutpanne'rdhacakravartinī kṛṣṇe sutottame, jāteṣu pradyumnādiṣu tadvaratanayeṣu labdhaṃ harivaṃśapitāmahattvamiti |

23. nidānadhyāne śrīsaṃvegaraṅgaśālāyāṃ gaṅgadattasyāvasaraḥ | puranagaranigamasaṃkula- kulagirigurudevabhavaṇaramaṇijje | vacchāvisae nayara, jayabaddhaṇamā''si supasiddhaṃ ||1|| siddhaṃtapasiddhavisuddhadhamma-kammekkabaddhapaḍibaṃdho | baṃdhupio nāma tahiṃ, ahesi siṭṭhī navisi ||2|| siṭṭhā'ṇumao putto ya, gaṃgadatto tti tassa suviṇīto | soya kameṇa'ṇupatto, tāruṇṇaṃ taruṇimaṇaharaṇaṃ ||3||
taṃ ca tahāvihama'valoiūṇa, piuṇā sayaṃbhunāmassa ! vaṇiṇo dhūyā variyā, vīvāha'ṅkaṃ pahidveṇaṃ ||4|| aha supasatthe hatthaggahassa, joggammi tihimuhuttammi | sā harisamuvagaeṇaṃ, uvvūḍhā gaṃgadatteṇaṃ ||5|| navaraṃ jabvelaṃ ciya, tīse so pāṇipallave laggo | tavvelaṃ ciya tīe, viyaṃbhio dussaho dāho | | 6 | |
kiṃ yavaṇa āliṃgiyamhi sittamhi kiṃ visaraseṇa |
iti citaṃtī palhattha - vāmahatthā''ṇaṇā vimaṇā ||7||
,

vitto ya vivāhavihī, sayaṇajaṇo vi ya gao sagehesu |

pāsāyasiharamā''ruhiya, tīe maraṇa'ṭṭhayā lahu vimukko | appā nivaḍiyamettā ya, sā gayā jhatti paṃcattaṃ ||18|| miliyā ammāpiuṇo, sayaṇajaṇo vi ya samāgato turiyaṃ | vihio ya se samaggo, sarīrasakkārapamuhavihī ||19||| tammaraṇanimittaṃ pi ya, savvattha purammi tattha vitthariyaṃ | niyadohaggeṇa daḍhaṃ ca, lajjio gaṃgadatto vi ||20|| navaraṃ piuṇā bhaṇio, mā vaccha ! visāyamettha thevaṃ pi | kāhisi tahā jahassaṃ jaha avarā hojja tuha bhajjā ||21|| aha kahavi dūrapuravāsi-vaṇiyadhūyaṃ puṇo vi so teṇa |

migapoyago vva māyaṇhiyāe. nivvisayavisayataṇhāe | viṇaḍijjāmi hayā''sāe, ahaha ! etto suhaṃ katto ||28|| iya jāva so viciṃtai, tāva piyā se samā''gao bhaṇai | vaccha | pariñcaya sogaṃ, niratthayaṃ kuṇasu kāyavvaṃ ||29|| cirabhavaparaṃparovajjiyāṇa, pāvāṇa vilasiyaṃ eyaṃ |
vayaṇijjamettha kassa vi, parama'ttheṇaṃ ao natthi ||30 || tā ehi putta ! jāmo, bhayavaṃ guṇasāyaro haṃ sūrī | suvvai samosaḍho taṃ ca, vaṃdimo nāṇarayaṇanihiṃ ||31|| paḍivannaṃ teṇa tato, sūrisamīve gayā viṇayapūvvaṃ | vaṃdittu taṃ nisannā, sanihiyammi dharāvaḍhe ||32||

sūrī vi divvanāṇo-vaogavināyasavvanāyavvo |
akkhevaṇivikkhevaṇi-sarūvama'ha kahai dhammakahaṃ ||33|| aha patthāvaṃ uvalabbha, gaṃgadatteṇa pucchio sūrī | bhayavaṃ ! purā mae kiṃ ?, dohaggakaraṃ kayaṃ kammaṃ ||34 || jeṇehabhave paramaṃ, viddesaṃ pāvio mhi juvaīṇa | iti teṇa pucchiyammi, bhaṇai gurū bho ! nisāmehi ||35 || nayarammi sayaduvāre, ranno siriseharassa taṃ bhajjā | añcaṃtavallahā āsi, gāḍhakāmā'ṇubaṃdhā ya ||36|| tassa ya ranno nimmerarūvalāyannamaṇaharaṃ'gīṇa | bhajjāṇama'ṇūṇāī, huntāṇi sayāṇi kira paṃca ||37||
tāṇi ya tumae viddesaṇā''i-bahukūḍamaṃtataṃtehiṃ |
haṇiyāṇi jahicchama'viggha-mavaṇivairamaṇavaṃchāe ||38|| samuvajjiuṃ ca vajjaṃ va, dāruṇaṃ bhūripāvasaṃbhāraṃ | tappañcayaṃ ca añcaṃta-duggadohaggakammaṃ pi ||39|| pajjante ya sudussaha-sāsā''ipabhūyapabalarogehiṃ | mariūṇa narayatiriesu, ṇegaso sahiya dukkhāī ||40||
kahakahavi kammalāghava-vaseṇa etthovaladdhamaṇuyatto |
puvvakayapāvadoseṇa, inhi dohaggama'ṇuhavasi ||41|| evaṃ soccā saṃjāya-dhammasaddho bhavāu uvviggo | āpucchiūṇa piyaraṃ, so pavvajjaṃ pavajjittā ||42||
gāmā''garanagaresuṃ, viharai vāu vva cattapaḍibaṃdho | gurukulavāso'vagato, sutta'tthavibhāvaṇujjutto ||43|| aha kettiyaṃ pi kālaṃ, pavvajjaṃ nikkalaṃkama'ṇucariuṃ | bhattaparinnāvihiṇā, uvaṭṭhio aṇasaṇaṃ kāuṃ ||44 || bhaṇio therihaṃ tao, aho mahābhāga ! aṇuciyamimaṃ te |
uvaciyasoṇiyamaṃsassa, aṇasaṇaṃ ettha patthāve ||45 || cattāri vicittāī, vigaīnijūhiyāiṃ cattāri | iccā''iṇā kameṇaṃ, tamhā saṃlehaṇaṃ kuṇasu ||46||
saṃlihiyadavvabhāvo, pacchā'ṇuḍhejja vaṃchiya'tthaṃpi | iharā visottiyā vi hu, bhavejja bahuvigghamimamā''hu ||47|| iya pannavio vi bahū, taggirama'vaganniūṇa sacchaṃdo |
ghettūṇa aṇasaṇaṃ giri-silāyale so nisanno tti ||48|| aha tassa tahā jhāṇa-ṭṭhiyassa paḍiruddhaduṭṭhajogassa | aṇasaṇagayassa jaṃ kira, vittaṃ taṃ saṃpai suṇei ||49||

tāvicchagucchasacchaha-sukaṃtakuṃtalakalāvakaliyāhiṃ | chaṇamayalaṃchaṇasacchaha-muhajonhādhavaliyadisāhiṃ || 50 ||

āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakam
tādhī ! niratthayaṃ majjha, jīviyaṃ duṭṭu, māṇusa jammaṃ | niruvahayaṃ'go vi tahā, viḍaṃbaṇaṃ jo'ṇupatto mhi || 59 ||
kuviyappavasagato, to jaṃpai jai imassa phalamathi | sāmannassa tayā haṃ, imo vva hojjāmi parajamme || 60 ||
eyaṃ niyāṇabaṃdhaṃ, kāūṇa mao mahiṃdakappammi | uvavanno pavarasuro, tattha ya visae nisevittā || 61 || āugaṃvigamammi cuo, uvavanno bhūmitilayabhūyāe | ujjeṇīe purīe, ranno sirisamarasīhassa || 62 || bhajjāe somanāmāe puttabhāveṇa pavarasumiṇehiṃ | kayasūo nīroo, samuciyasamae pasūo ya || 63 || vihiyaṃ baddhāvaṇayaṃ patthāve ṭhāviyaṃ ca se nāmaṃ | raṇasūro tti kameṇaṃ, tārunnaṃ panara'ṇupatto ||64|| aha puvvajjiyaniravagga- huggasohaggasaṃgato jattha |
so bhai tattha juvaīṇa, tammi nikkhittakkhūṇa ||65 ||
mayaṇavasavihiyabahuhāva-bhāvavibbhamavilāsalīlāṇa | avahatthiyalajjāṇaṃ, avare viramaṃti vāvārā || 66 || rāisaraseṇāvai-mahibbhasāmaṃtamaṃtidhūyāhiṃ | esī'mha paī ahavā vi, huyavaho ii vayaṃtīhiṃ || 67 || uvvūḍho gāḍhaparūḍha-paṇayasārāhiṃ tāhiṃ samagaṃ ca | paṃcavihavisayasokkhaṃ, aṇurbhujai dīharaṃ kālaṃ || 68 || mariuṃ ca bhavutthasutikkha- dukkhalakkhāṇa bhāyaṇaṃ bhūo | niyaduvvilasiyavasao, cirakālaṃ gaṃgadatto tti || 69 ||
[ saṃvegaraṃgaśālā śloka 4094 taḥ 4162]

khaṇḍaḥ-2 dṛṣṭāntasamuccayaḥ
23, 63. nidānadhyāne śrītriṣaṣṭiśalākāpuruṣacaritrā'ntargatā citrasaṃbhūtimuneḥ kathā | vārāṇasyāṃ tato'bhūtāṃ bhūtadattābhidhasya tau | mahādhanasamṛddhasya mātaṃgādhipateḥ sutau ||1|| citrasaṃbhūtanāmānau tau mithaḥ snehaśālinau | na kadāpi vyayujyetāṃ saṃbaddhau nakhamāṃsavat ||2|| vārāṇasyāṃ tadā cābhūcchaṃkha ityavanīpatiḥ | āsīcca sacivastasya namucirnāma viśrutaḥ | | 3 || aparedyuḥ so'parādhe mahīyasi mahībhujā | arpito bhūtadattasya pracchannaṃ vadhahetave ||4|| tenoce namuciśchannaṃ tvāṃ rakṣāmi nijātmavat | pāṭhayasyātmajau me tvaṃ yadi bhūmigṛhasthitaḥ ||5|| pratipannaṃ namucinā tanmātaṃgapatervacaḥ | jano hi jīvitavyārthī tannāsti na karoti yat ||6|| vicitrāścitrasaṃbhūtau sa tathādhyāpayat kalāḥ | reme'nuraktayā sārdhaṃ mātaṃgapatibhāryayā ||7|| jñātvā tadbhūtadattenārebhe mārayituṃ tu saḥ | saheta kaḥ svadāreṣu pāradārikaviplavam ||8|| jñātvā māṃtagaputrābhyāṃ sa dūreṇāpasāritaḥ | saivāsmai dakṣiṇā dattā prāṇarakṣaṇalakṣaṇā || 9 || tato niḥsṛtya namucirgatavān hastināpure | cakre sanatkumāreṇa sacivaścakriṇā nijaḥ || 10 || itaśca citrasaṃbhūtau bhaturnavayauvanau | kuto'pi hetorāyātau pṛthivyāmāśvināviva || 11 | | tau svādu jagaturgītaṃ hāhāhūhūpahāsinau | vādayāmāsaturvīṇāmatituṃburunāradau ||12|| gītaprabaṃdhānugataiḥ suvyaktaiḥ saptabhiḥ svaraiḥ | tayorvādayatorvīṇāṃ kiṃkaraṃti sma kinnarāḥ || 13 || murajaṃ dhīraghoṣaṃ tau vādayantau ca cakratuḥ | gṛhītamurakaṃkālātodyakṛṣṇaviḍaṃbanām ||14|| śivaḥ śivorvaśīraṃbhāyuṃjakeśītilottamāḥ | yannāṭyaṃ na vidāṃcakrustau tadapyabhininyatuḥ ||15|| sarvagāndharvasarvasvamapūrvaṃ viśvakārmaṇam | prakāśayadbhyāṃ ca tābhyāṃ na jahre kasya mānasam ||16|| tasyāṃ puri pravatṛte kadācinmadanotsavaḥ | nirīyuḥ pauracarcaryastatra saṃgītapeśalāḥ || 17 || carcarī niryayau tatra citrasaṃbhūtayorapi | jagmustatraiva tadgītākṛṣṭāḥ paurā mṛgā iva || 18 || rājño vyajñapi kenāpi mātaṃgābhyāṃ purījanaḥ | gītenākṛṣya sarvo'yamātmavanmalinaḥ kṛtaḥ ||19|| kṣmāpenāpi purādhyakṣaḥ sākṣepamidamājñapi | na praveśaḥ pradātavyo nagaryāmanayoḥ kvacit ||20|| tataḥ prabhṛti tau vārāṇasyā dūreṇa tasthatuḥ | pravṛttaścaikadā tatra kaumudīparamotsavaḥ || 21 | | rājaśāsanamullaṃghya lolendriyatayā ca tau | praviṣṭau nagarīṃ bhṛṃgau gajagaṃḍataṭīmiva || 22 || utsavaṃ prekṣamāṇau tau sarvāṃgīṇāvaguṃṭhanau | dasyuvannagarīmadhye channaṃ channaṃ viceratuḥ || 23 ||

192
āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakam
kroṣṭuvat kroṣṭuśabdena poragītena tau tataḥ | agāyatāṃ tāratāramalaṃghyā bhavitavyatā ||24|| ākarṇya karṇamadhuraṃ tadgītaṃ yuvanāgaraiḥ | madhuvanmakṣikābhistau mātaṃgau parivāritau ||25|| kāvetāviti vijñātuṃ lokaiḥ kṛṣṭāvaguṃṭhanau | are tāveva mātaṃgāvityākṣepeṇa bhāṣitau ||26 || nāgaraiḥ kuṭyamānau tau yaṣṭibhirloṣṭubhistataḥ | śvānāviva gṛhātpuryā natagrīvau nirīyatuḥ ||7|| tau sainyaśaśavalokairhanyamānau pade pade | skhalatpādau kathamapi gabhīrodyānamīyatuḥ ||28|| tāvacintayatāmevaṃ dhiṅnau durjātidūṣitam | kalākauśalarūpādi payo ghrātamivāhinā ||29|| upakāro guṇairāstāmapakāro'yamāvayoḥ | tadidaṃ kriyamāṇāyāḥ śāntervetāla utthitaḥ ||30|| kalālāvaṇyarūpāṇi syūtāni vapuṣā saha | tadevānarthasadanaṃ tṛṇavattyajyatāṃ kvacit ||31|| iti niścitya tau prāṇaparihāṇaparāyaṇau | mṛtyuṃ sākṣādiva draṣṭuṃ celaturdakṣiṇāmabhi ||32|| tato dūraṃ prayātau tau girimekamapaśyatām | yatrārūḍhairbhuvīkṣyaṃte kariṇaḥ kiripotavat ||33|| bhṛgupātecchayā tābhyāmārohadbhayāṃ mahāmuniḥ | dadṛśe parvate tasmijaṃgamo guṇaparvataḥ ||34 || prāvṛṣeṇyamivāṃbhodaṃ muniṃ giriśiraḥ sthitam | dṛṣṭvā praṇaṣṭasaṃtāpaprasarau tau babhūvatuḥ ||35 || tau prāgduḥkhamivojjhantāvānandāśrujalacchalāt | tatpādapadmayo gāviva sadyo nipetatuḥ ||36|| samāpya muninā dhyānaṃ kau yuvāṃ kimihāgatau | iti pṛṣṭau svavṛttāṃtaṃ tāvaśeṣamaśaṃsatām ||37 || sa ūce bhṛgupātena vapureva hi śīryate | śīryate nāśubhaṃ karma janmāntaraśatārjitam ||38 || tyājyaṃ vapuridaṃ vāṃ cedgṛhṇītaṃ vapuṣaḥ phalam | taccāpavargasvargādikāraṇaṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ ||39 || ityādideśanāvākyasudhānidhautamānasau | tasya pārśve jagṛhaturyatidharmamubhāvapi ||40 || adhīyānau krameṇātha tau gītārthī babhūvatuḥ | āderaṇa gṛhītaṃ hi kiṃ vā na syānmanasvinām ||41|| ṣaṣṭāṣṭamaprabhṛtibhistau tapobhiḥ sudustapaiḥ | kraśayāmāsaturda prāktanaiḥ karmabhiḥ saha ||42|| tato viharamāṇau tau grāmādgrāmaṃ purāt puram | kadācitpratipedāte nagaraṃ hastināpuram ||43|| tau tatra bahirudyāne ceraturduścaraṃ tapaḥ | saṃbhogabhūmayo'pi syustapase śāntacetasām ||44 || saṃbhūtamuniranyedhurmāsakṣapaṇapāraṇe | pure praviṣṭo bhikṣārthaṃ yatidharmo'ṅgavāniva ||45|| gehādnehaṃ paribhrāmyannīryāsamitipūrvakam | sa rājamārgāpatito dṛṣṭo namucimaṃtriṇā ||46|| mātaṃgadārakaḥ so'yaṃ madvṛttaṃ khyāpayiṣyati | maṃtrīti cintayāmāsa pāpāḥ sarvatra śaṃkitāḥ ||47 || yāvanmanmarma kasyāpi prakāśayati na hyasau | tāvannirvāsayāmyenamiti pattīnnyayuṃkta saḥ ||48||

khaṇḍa:-2 dṛṣṭāntasamuccayaḥ
193
sa tāḍayitumārebhe tena. pūrvopakāryapi| kṣīrapāṇamivāhīnāmupakāro'satāṃ yataḥ ||49 || lakuṭaiḥ kuṭyamānaḥ sa sasyabījamivotkaṭaiḥ | sthānāttato'pacakrāma tvaritatvaritaṃ muniḥ ||5|| amucyamānaḥ kuṭṭākairniryannapi munistadā | śāṃto'pyakupyadāpo'pi tapyante vahnitāpataḥ ||51|| nirjagāma mukhāttasya bāṣpotpīḍaṃ samaṃtataḥ | akālopasthitāṃbhodavibhramaṃ bibhradaṃbare ||52|| tejoleśyollalāsātha jvālāpaṭalamālinī | taḍinmaṃḍalasaṃkīrṇāmiva dyāmabhitanvatī ||53|| atiprakupitaṃ taṃ ca tejoleśyādharaṃ tataḥ | prasādayitumājagmuḥ paurāḥ sabhayakautukāḥ ||54|| rājā sanatkumāro'pi jñātvā tatra samāyayau | uttiṣṭhati yato vahnistatra vidhyāpayetsudhīḥ ||55|| natvoce taṃ nṛpaḥ kiṃ vo yujyate bhagavannidam | candrāśmākśutapto'pi nācimu~cati jātucit ||56 || ebhiratyaparāddhaṃ yatkopo'yaṃ bhavatā mataḥ | kṣīrābdhermathyamānasya kālakūṭamabhūnna kim ||57|| na syātsyāccecciraṃ na syācciraṃ cettatphale'nyathā | khalasneha iva krodhaḥ satāṃ tabrūmahe'tra kim ||58 || tathāpi nātha nāthāmi kopaṃ muṃcetarocitam | bhavādṛśāḥ samadṛśo hyapakāryupakāriṣu ||59|| citro'pyatrāntare jñātvā saṃbhūtamunimabhyagāt | sāṃtvayituṃ bhadramiva dvipaṃ madhurabhāṣitaiḥ ||60|| tasya kopa upaśāmyaccitravākyaiḥ śrutānugaiḥ | payovāhapayaḥpūraigireriva davānalaḥ ||61|| tīvrakopatamomuktaḥ śaśāṃka iva pārvaṇaḥ | kṣaṇādāsādayāmāsa prasādaṃ sa mahāmuniḥ ||62 || vaṃditvā kṣamayitvā ca lokastasmānyavartata | saṃbhūtaścitramuninā tadudhānamanīyata ||63|| paścāttāpaṃ cakratustau paryaṭadbhirgṛhe gṛhe | āhāramātrakakṛte prāpyate vyasanaṃ mahat ||64|| śarīraṃ gatvaramidaṃ hyāhāreṇāpi poṣitam | kimanena śarīreṇa kiṃ vāhāreṇa yoginām ||65 || cetaseti viniścitya kṛtasaṃlekhanau purā | ubhau caturvidhāhārapratyākhyānaṃ pracakratuḥ ||66|| kaḥ parābhūtavān sādhuṃ vasudhāṃ pāti mayyapi | iti jijñāsato rājño maṃtrī vyajñapi kenacit ||67 || ardhyānnārcati yaḥ so'pi pāpaḥ kimuta haṃti yaḥ | ityānāyayadurvīśo dasyuvatsaṃyamayya tam ||68 || anyo'pi sādhuvidhvaṃsaṃ mā vidhāditi śuddhadhīḥ | taṃ baddhaṃ puramadhyena so'naiṣītsādhusaṃnidhau ||69|| namannupaśiroratnabhāniraṃbhomayīmiva | kurvanurvī sa urvīśapuṃgavastāvavandata ||70|| savyapāṇigṛhītāsyavastrikāpihitānanau | uddakṣiṇakarau tau tamāśaśaṃsaturāśiṣā ||71|| yo vo'parāddhavān so'stu svakarmaphalabhājanam | rājñā sanatkumāraṇetyadarśi namucistayoḥ ||72|| amoci namuciḥ prāptaḥ paṃcatvocitabhūmikām | sanatkumāratastābhyāmurago garuḍādiva ||73||

āturapratyākhyānaprakīrṇakam
nirvāsya karmacaṃḍālacaṃḍāla iva pattanāt | vadhyo'pyamoci rājñā sa mānyaṃ hī guruśāsanam ||74 || sapatnībhiścatuḥṣaṣṭisahasraḥ parivāritā | vaṃdituṃ tau sunaṃdāgāt strīratnamatha cakriṇaḥ ||75|| sā saṃbhūtamuneḥ pādapadmayorkhalitālakā | papātāsyena kurvāṇā bhuvamindumatīmiva ||76|| tasyāścālakasaṃsparśa saṃbhūtamuniranvabhūt | romāṃcitaśca sadyo'bhūcchalānveṣī hi manmathaḥ ||7|| atha sāntaḥpure rājñi tāvanujñāpya jagmuṣi | rāgābhibhūtaḥ saṃbhūto nidānamiti nirmame ||78 || duṣkarasya madīyasya yadyasti tapasaḥ phalam | tataḥ strīratnapatirahaṃ bhūyāsaṃ bhāvijanmani ||79|| citro'pyūce kāṃkṣasīdaṃ mokṣadāttapasaḥ phalam | mauliyogyena ratnena pādapīṭhaṃ karoṣi kam ||80 || mohātkṛtaṃ tannidānamidānīmapi mucyatām | mithyāduṣkṛtamasyāstu muhyaṃti na bhavādṛśāḥ ||81|| evaṃ nivāryamāṇo'pi saṃbhūtaścitrasādhunā | nidānaṃ nāmucadaho viṣayecchā balīyasī ||82|| niyū~ḍhānaśanau tau tu prāptāyuḥ karmasaṃkṣayau | saudharma samajāyetāṃ vimāne sundare surau ||83||
[śrī triṣaṣṭiśalākā0 parva-6 śloka-1 taḥ 102] 23. nidānadhyāne śrīpāṇḍavacaritroktaṃ draupadīkathānakam || athāvasaramāsādya, deśanānte munīśvaram | kiṃ dhavāḥ pañca pāñcālyā ?, ityapṛcchajjanārdanaḥ ||1|| so'bravīdetayā pūrvajanmopāttanidānayā | vṛtāḥ pañca dhavāstadbhoḥ!, kṛtaṃ mīmāṃsayā'nayā ||2|| purā hi puri campāyāṃ, bhūmidevāstrayo'bhavan | somadeva-somabhūti-somadattāḥ sahodarāḥ ||3|| teṣāmāsan kramāttisraḥ, premajanmabhuvaḥ priyāḥ | nāgaśrīratha bhūtaśrīryakṣaśrīśceti nāmataḥ ||4|| mithaḥ snigdhatayā teṣāmiyamāsīvyavasthitiḥ | yat paryāyeṇa bhoktavyaṃ, sarvairekasya veśmani ||5|| atha kramāt samāyāte, bhuktivārakavāsare | nāgaśrīrāśritānekarasāṃ rasavartī vyadhāt ||6|| kaṭutumbīphalaṃ tasyāmanekadravyasaṃskṛtam | apākṣīdiyamajñāsīt, pākānte ca kathaṃcana ||7|| nānāvastuvyayodbhūtaṃ, kārpaṇyena na sā'tyajat | kevalaṃ kvacidekānte, nidhāya tadadhārayat ||8|| tadanyairvividhaibhojyaiḥ, svādubhiḥ patidevarān | bhojayāmāsa sā prītā, kṣaṇāt te'pi bahiryayuḥ ||9|| tadā subhūmibhāgākhye, purodyāne mahāmanāḥ | dharmaghoṣābhidha; sūrikha़navān samavāsarat ||10|| tasya dharmaruciḥ śiṣyo, māsakṣapaṇapāraṇe | kirātyā iva kalpadruhaṃ nāgaśriyo yayau ||11|| vṛthā mā bhūdidaṃ tāvadbhavedeṣo'pi toṣitaḥ| ityālocya tayā tasmai, tattumbīvyañjanaṃ dade ||12|| apūrvamiti saṃcintya, tadgrahānugrahecchayā | asau vasatimāgatya, gurūṇāṃ tadadarśayat ||13||

khaṇḍa:-2 dṛṣṭāntasamuccayaḥ
te'pi tadgandhamāghrāya, vātsalyādidamūcire | yadīdaṃ bhokṣyase vatsa !, sadyo mṛtyumavāpsyasi ||14 || viśuddhe sthaṇḍile maṅgha, tat pariṣṭhāpaya kvacit | ityādeśādguroḥ so'pi, jagāma nagarādvahiḥ ||15|| bindau nipatite tasya, kathaṃcit tatra pātrataḥ | saktāḥ sa muniradrākṣīnmiyamāṇāḥ pipīlikāḥ ||16|| dadhyau ca baddhasaṃvego, bindurapyasya yadyasau | prāṇināmiyatāṃ hantā, tatsarvaṃ kiṃ kariṣyati ? ||17 || varamekasya me mṛtyuḥ, koṭiśo na tu dehinām | ityālocya saromāñcastadasau bubhuje svayam ||18|| siddhapratyakṣamālocya, samādhimadhuraṃ manaḥ | dadhat sarvārthasiddhe'gānmuniḥ prāṇān vihāya saḥ ||19|| bahirdharmaruceḥ kasmādvilamba ? iti veditum | guruḥ śrīdharmaghoṣo'tha, nidideśa parān munīn ||20|| taṃ parāsuṃ bahirvīkṣya, tadrajoharaṇādikam | ādāya gurupādānāṃ, te'pi sarvaṃ nyavedayan ||21|| vijñāyātīndriyajñānopayogena yathāsthitam | sarva munīnāṃ nāgaśrīvṛttāntaṃ te'pyacīkathan ||22|| jñātvā kathaṃcillokānāṃ, vākparamparayā janāḥ | somadevādiviprāṇāṃ, tasyā duśceṣṭitaṃ jaguḥ ||23|| kāmamākruśya viprāste, tāṃ gṛhāniravāsayan | lokairdhikriyamāṇā ca, sā'pi babhrāma sarvataḥ ||24 || kāsa-śvāsa-jvarotkampakuṣṭhādyaiḥ krāntavigrahā | rogaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhilebhe, sā'traiva narakavyathām ||25 || kṣutpipāsārditā lokairnindyamānā pade pade | duḥkhaṃ bhramantī sā mṛtvā, ṣaṣṭhaṃ narakamāsadat ||26 || tasmādutpadya mīneṣu, saptamaṃ narakaṃ yayau | punarmīnatvamāsādya, tasminneva jagāma sā ||27|| dviddhirevaṃ siṣeve'sau, narakān sakalānapi | pṛthvīkāyādiṣūtpede, taduddhattā ca bhūriśaḥ ||28 || campāyāmatha sā karmalāghavāt sukumārikā | sutā sāgaradattasya, subhadrākukṣibhūrabhūt ||29|| jinadattātmajastatra, bhadrājaścāsti sāgaraḥ | veśmasthāṃ tatpitā'nyedhurdadarśa sukumārikām ||30 || tanayasya madīyasya, yogyeyamiti cintayan | saha bandhubhirabhyetya, pitaraṃ tāmayācata ||31|| so'pyūce'tipriyā me'sau, na bhavāmyanayā vinā | mamāstu gṛhajāmātā, sāgarastat tavātmajaḥ ||32|| sutamālocayāmīti, jinadatto'gamad gṛham | sāgarasya tadākhyañca, maunamālambya sa sthitaḥ ||33|| aniṣiddhaṃ hyanumatamiti nyāyena tatpitā | mene sāgaradattāya, gṛhajāmātaraṃ sutam ||34|| āścaryakṛttayorjajñe, pāṇigrahamahotsavaḥ | naktaṃ śiśriyatustau ca, palyata vāsaveśmani ||35 || pūrvakarmavaśāttasyāḥ, sparśamaṅgārasodaram | āsādya sāgarastasthau, kṣaṇaṃ tatra kathaṃcana ||36|| tasyāṃ sukhaprasuptāyāṃ, sa praṇaśya gṛhaṃ yayau | nidrātyaye'rudat kāntamapaśyantī ca sā bhṛśam ||37 || athādiṣṭā tayordantaśaucahetoḥ subhadrayā | prātaraikṣiṣṭa tāṃ ceṭī, rudantīṃ vallabhojjhitām ||38 ||

sā gatvā''khyat subhadrāyai, sā'pi svapreyase kṣaṇāt | pitureṣo'pi jāmātu-rupālambhaṃ dadau svayam ||39 || so'pyāha tanayaṃ vatsa !, na yuktaṃ vidadhe tvayā | gacchādhunā'pi tat tatra, mā'nyathā madvacaḥ kṛthāḥ ||40 || sāgaro'pyūcivānagnau, varaṃ jhampāṃ tanomyaham | na punastāta ! gantāsmi, veśma tasyāḥ kadācana ||41|| idaṃ sāgaradatto'pi, tatkuḍyāntarito'śṛṇot | jagāda ca gṛhaṃ gatvā, nirāśaḥ sukumārikām ||42|| kathaṃcit sarvathā vatse !, viraktaḥ sāgarastvayi | tanmā khidyasva ko'pyanyaḥ, patistava vidhāsyate ||43|| kaupīnāmbaramātraikakaparaṃ makṣikāvṛtam | bhikṣukaṃ kaṃcidadrākṣīt, sa gavākṣasthito'nyadā ||44 || āhūya tena so'mbhobhiḥ, snapayitvā sugandhibhiḥ | vilipya candanairdivyavāsāṃsi paridhāpya ca ||45||
ūce tubhyaṃ mayā dattā, putrīyaṃ sukumārikā |
___madīyāṃ vilasan lakṣmī, sukhamāssva sahānayā ||46|| (yugmam) ityuktaḥ so'viśadvāsaveśma sākaṃ tayā niśi | mene ca tadvapuḥśleṣamagniploṣamivātmanaḥ ||47 || sahasotthāya veṣaṃ svamādāya sa palāyitaḥ | tathaiva rudatīṃ tāṃ ca, vilokya janako'bravīt ||48|| so'yaṃ prākkarmaṇāṃ vatse!, vipāko'nyanna kāraṇam | tadāssva dadatī dānaṃ, śāntātmā mama veśmani ||49|| tathaiva kurvatī tasthau, sā dharmaṃkaparāyaṇā | prāpustadgahamanyedyuḥ, sādhvyo gopālikā iti ||50 || śuddhairaśanapānādyaiḥ, sā bhaktyā pratilābhya ca | tanmukhāddharmamākarṇya, viraktā vratamagrahīt ||51|| tapo'tha kurvatī nityaṃ, turyaṣaṣṭhāṣṭamādikam | āryikābhiḥ sahaitābhirvihāramakarodiyam ||52|| āryāstāḥ sā kadā'pyūce, tanomyātāpanāmaham | subhūmibhāgodyānasthā, dattadṛṣṭi vivasvati ||53|| sā pratyabhidadhe tābhiriti ha smāgamoktayaḥ | ātāpanā na sādhvīnāṃ, kalpate vasaterbahiḥ ||54 || anākarṇya ca tadvācaṃ, vane tasminnupetya sā | yāvadārabhate kṣiptacakṣurātāpanāṃ ravau ||55 || tāvadutsaṅgamekasya, śrayantīmaparasya tu | aṅkenyastāṅghimanyena, badhyamānāvataṃsakām ||56|| pareṇa vidhṛtacchatrāṃ, vījitāmitareṇa ca | gaṇikāmāgatāṃ tatra, devadattāṃ dadarśa sā ||57||
tāṃ vīkṣyāpūrṇabhogecchā, nidānamiti sā'karot | (tribhirviśeṣakam)
bhaveyaṃ tapasā'nena, pañcapreyasyasāviva ||58|| tanvatī dehaśaucādyamabhyukṣantī kṣaṇe kṣaṇe | vāryamāṇeyamāryābhirmanasīdamadhārayat ||59|| purā bahumatā'bhūvamāryikāṇāmagāriṇī | tiraskurvanti māmetā, bhikṣukīmadhunā punaḥ ||60|| ityālocya vinirgatya, vibhinnavasatisthitā | vrataṃ sā pālayāmāsa, ciraṃ svacchandavartinī ||61 ||

māsān saṃlekhanāmaṣṭau, kṛtvā'nālocya saṃsthitā | navapalyopamāyuṣkā, saudharme devyabhūdiyam ||62 || cyutā'bhavacca kṛṣṇeyaṃ, prācīnāñca nidānataḥ | bhartāro jajñire'muṣyāḥ, pañcaite ko'tra vismayaḥ ? ||63 ||
. [śrī pāṇḍavacaritre śloka-324 taḥ 386] 24. snehadhyāne śrītriṣaṣṭiśalākāpuruṣacaritroktaḥ marudevīmātṛvistāraḥ |
ito'pi ca vinītāyāṃ, vinīto bharateśvaraḥ | ājagāma namaskartuṃ, marudevāṃ divāmukhe ||1|| tanūjavirahodbhūtairaśrāntairasravāribhiḥ | jātanīlikayā luptalocanābjāṃ pitāmahīm ||2||
jyeṣṭhaḥ pautro namatyeṣa, devi ! tvatpādapaṅkaje |
svayaṃ vijñapayannevaṃ, bharataḥ praṇanāma tām ||3|| (yugmam) svāminī marudevā'pi, bharatāyā'śiṣaṃ dadau | hṛdyamāntīṃ śucamiva, giramityujjagāra ca ||4|| māṃ tvāṃ mahīṃ prajāṃ lakṣmī, vihāya tṛṇavat tadā | ekākī gatavān vatso, durmarā marudevyaho ! ||5|| sūnoścandrātapacchāyamātapatraṃ kva mūrddhani ? | sarvāṅgasantāpakaraḥ, kvedānīṃ tapanātapaḥ ? ||6|| salīlagatibhiryānairyānaṃ hastyādibhiḥ kva tat ? | vatsasya pādacāritvaṃ, vedānīṃ pathikocitam ? ||7|| kva tad vāravadhūkṣiptacārucāmaravījanam ? | matsūnoḥ kvā'dhunā daṃśamaśakādyairupadravaḥ ? ||8|| kka tad devasamānītadivyāhāropajīvanam ? | kva bhikṣābhojanaṃ tasyā'bhojanaṃ vā'pi samprati ? ||9|| ratnasiṃhāsanotsaṅge, mahaddheḥ kva tadāsanam ? | matsūnoḥ khaḍgina iva, kva nirāsanatā'dhunā ? ||10|| ārakṣarātmarakṣaiśca, rakṣite va pure sthitiḥ ? | sūnoḥ kva vāsaḥ siṃhāhiduḥśvāpadapade vane ? ||11|| kva tad divyāṅganāgītaṃ, karṇāmṛtarasāyanam ? sūnoḥ kvonmattapheruṇḍaphetkārāḥ karṇasūcayaḥ ||12|| aho ! kaṣṭamaho ! kaṣṭaṃ, yanme sūnustapātyaye | padmakhaṇḍa iva mṛduḥ, sahate vārividravam ||13|| himattau himasampātasaṃkleśavivaśāṃ daśām | araṇye mālatīstamba, iva yāti nirantaram ||14|| uṣṇatvuṣṇakiraṇakiraṇairatidāruṇaiḥ | santāpaṃ cā'nubhavati, stamberama ivā'ṭikam ||15|| tadevaṃ sarvakāleṣu, vanevāsī nirāśrayaḥ | pṛthagjana ivaikākī, vatso me duḥkhabhājanam ||16|| tattaduḥkhākulaṃ vatsaṃ, paśyantyagre dṛśoriva | vadantī nityamapyevaṃ, hā ! tvāmapi dunomyaham ||17|| iti duḥkhākulāṃ devīṃ, marudevīmudañjaliḥ | vācā'vocanavasudhāsadhrīcyā vasudhādhavaḥ ||18|| sthairyādrervajrasārasya, mahāsattvaśiromaṇeḥ | tātasya jananī bhūtvā, kimevaṃ devi ! tāmyasi ? ||19|| tātastarītuṃ sahasā, saṃsārāmbhodhimudyataḥ | kaṇṭhabaddhaśilāprāyān, sthāne tatyāja naḥ prabhuḥ ||20||

24. snehadhyāne śrīpariśiṣṭaparvamadhye vajraṃ prati sunandāyāḥ snehavilāpaḥ || itaśca vajrastatrasthaḥ krameṇābhūtrihāyanaḥ | tadā ca dhanagiryādyāstatra sādhava āyayuḥ ||1|| āyāsyati dhanagirirgrahīṣyāmi svamātmajam | sunandaivaṃ cintayantī teṣvāyāteṣvamodata ||2|| sunandāpi maharṣibhyaḥ svanandanamayācata | te punarnārpayāmāsuḥ pratyabhāṣanta cedṛśam ||3|| ayācitastvayā datto mugdhe'smabhyamayaṃ śiśuḥ | vāntānnamiva ko dattaṃ punarādātumicchati ? ||4|| vikrīḍeṣviva datteṣu svāmitvamapagacchati | mā yāciṣṭhāḥ sutaṃ dattvā tvayaiṣa parasātkṛtaḥ ||5|| pakṣayorubhayorevamuccairvivadamānayoḥ | loko'vādīdamuṃ vādaṃ rājā nirdhārayiṣyati ||6|| tataḥ sunandā lokena sahitā nṛpaparṣadi | jagāma saṅghasahitāḥ śramaṇā api te yayuḥ ||7|| rājño nyaṣīdadvāmena sunandā dakṣiṇena tu | śrīmānsaṅghaḥ samasto'pi yathāsthānamathāpare ||8|| paribhāvya dvayorbhāṣāmuttaraṃ cāvadannṛpaḥ | yenāhūtaḥ samāyāti bālastasya bhavatvasau ||9|| taṃ nirṇayamamaṃsātāṃ tau tu pakṣāvubhāvapi | iti cocaturādau kaḥ sūnumāhvātumarhati ||10|| strīgṛhyāḥ procire paurā vatināmeṣa bālakaḥ | cirasaṅghaṭitapremā tadvaco nātilaṅghate ||11|| mātaivāhvayatāmādāviyaṃ duṣkarakāriṇī | nārīti cānukampyāpi bhavatyetaddhi nānyathā ||12|| tataḥ sunandā bahuśo bālakrīḍanakāni ca | vividhāni ca bhakṣyāṇi darśayantyevamabhyadhāt ||13|| hastino'mī amī aśvāḥ pattayo'mī amī rathāḥ | tava krīḍārthamānītāstadgṛhāṇaihi dāraka ! ||14 || modakā maṇḍakā drākṣāḥ śarkarāścānyadapyadaḥ | yadicchasi tadastyeva gṛhyatāmehi dāraka ! ||15|| tavāyuṣmankṛṣīyāhaṃ sarvāṅgamavatāraṇe | ciraṃ jīva ciraṃ nanda sunandāmāśu modaya ||16|| mama devo mama putro mamātmā mama jīvitam | tvamevāsīti māṃ dīnāṃ pariṣvaṅgeṇa jīvaya ||17|| vilakṣāṃ mā kṛthā vatsa ! māṃ lokasyāsya paśyataḥ | hṛdayaṃ me'nyathā bhāvi pakvavāluṅkavad dvidhā ||18|| ehi haṃsagate vatsa ! mamotsaṅgaṃ pariṣkuru | kukṣivāsāvakrayo me na labhyaḥ kimiyānapi ||19|| evaṃ krīḍanakairbhakṣyaprakāraiścāṭukairapi | saunandeyaḥ sunandāyā nābhyagcchanmanāgapi ||20|| na māturupakāraṇāṃ ko'pi syādanṛṇaḥ pumān | evaṃ vidannapi sudhīrvajra evamacintayat ||21|| yadi saṅghamupekṣiṣye kṛtvā mātuḥ kṛpāmaham | tadā syānmama saṃsāro dīrghadīrghataraḥ khalu ||22|| iyaṃ ca dhanyā mātā me'lpakarmā pravrajiṣyati | upekṣyamasyā hyāpātamātrajaṃ duḥkhamapyadaḥ ||23|| dīrghadarśī vimṛśyaivaṃ vajro vajradṛḍhāśayaḥ | pratimāstha iva sthānānna cacāla manāgapi ||24||

[śrī pariśiṣṭaparva śloka0 100 taḥ 136] 24. snehadhyāne śrīuttarādhyayanasūtre arhannakamātṛkathā || __ tagarā nayarī, tattha arihamitto nāma āyario, tassa samīve datto nāma vāṇiyao bhaddāe bhāriyāe putteṇa ya ahannaeṇasaddhiṃ pavvaio, so taṃ khuḍugaṃ ṇa kayāi bhikkhāe hiṃḍāvei, paḍhamāliyāīhiṃ kimicchaehiṃ poseti, so sukumālo, sāhūṇa appattiyaṃ, ṇa taraṃti kiṃci bhaṇiuṃ | annayā so khaṃto kālagato, sāhūhiṃ do tinni vā divase dāuṃ bhikkhassa oyārio, so sukumālasarīro gimhe uvariṃ hiṭṭhā ya ḍajjhati pāse ya, tihābhibhūto chāyāe vīsamaṃto pautthavatiyāe vaṇiyamahilāe diṭṭho, orālasukumālasarīrottikāuṃ tīse tahiṃ ajjhovavāo jāo, ceḍīe saddāvito, kiṃ maggasi ?, bhikkhaṃ, diṇṇā se moyagā, pucchito-kīsa tumaṃ dhammaṃ karesi ?, bhaṇaisuhanimittaṃ, sā bhaṇai-to mae ceva samaṃ bhoge bhuṃjāhi, so ya uṇheṇa tajjio uvasaggijjaṃto ya paḍibhaggo bhoge jati | so sāhūhiṃ savvahiṃ maggito ṇa diṭṭho appasāgāriyaṃ paviṭṭho, pacchā se māyā ummattiyā jāyā puttasogeṇa, ṇayaraṃ paribhamaṃtī arahannayaṃ vilavaṃtī jaṃ jahiṃ pāsai taṃ tahiṃ savvaṃ bhaṇati

atthi te koi arahannao diṭṭho ?, evaṃ vilavamāṇī bhamai, jāva annayā teṇa putteṇa oloyaṇagaeṇa diṭṭhā, pañcabhinnāyā, taheva oyarittā pāesu paḍio, taṃ picchiūṇa taheva satthacittā jāyā, tāhe bhaṇṇai - putta ! pavvayāhi, mā duggaiṃ jāhisi, so bhaṇai-na tarāmi kāuṃ saṃjamaṃ, jadi paraṃ asaṇaṃ karemi, evaṃ karehi, māya asaṃjao bhavāhi, mā saṃsāraṃ bhamihisi, pacchā so taheva tattāe silāe pāovagamaṇaṃ karei, muhutteṇa sukumālasarīro uṇheṇa vilāo, puvviṃ teṇa nāhiyāsio pacchā te'hiyāsito |
25. kāmadhyāne śrīaṣṭāhnikāpravacane kumāranandisuvarṇakārasambandhaḥ |
caṃpāyāmājanma strīlaṃpaṭaḥ kumāranaṃdiḥ svarṇakṛt yāṃ yāṃ surūpāṃ kanyāṃ śṛṇoti tāṃ svarṇapaṃcaśatīṃ dattvā gṛhṇāti, evaṃ paṃca śatāni (500) striyo jātāḥ, tābhiḥ sahaikastaṃbhasaudhe'raṃsīt, tasya nāgilaśrāddhaḥ mitramasti, ekadā paṃcaśailādhipavyaṃtaradevyau śakrājñayā naṃdīśvare prāsthiṣātāṃ tadānīṃ tatpatirvidyunmālī cyutaḥ, tataste 'hāsā' 'prahāsā' nāmnyau kāmukaṃ taṃ svarṇakṛtaṃ dṛṣṭvā tatrāvātaratāṃ, dṛṣṭvā parirabdhukāmaḥ svarṇakāraḥ provāca ke yuvām ? kimarthamāgate ?, te procatuḥ - bhavadartham, prāptasaṃjñaḥ sa hemakṛt prārthanāṃ cakre, te ūcatuḥ tvaṃ paṃcaśaile samāgaccheḥ, tatrāvayoḥ saṃgo bhāvītyuktvā te utpatite, so'pi svarṇakāra, bhūbhuje svarṇaṃ dattvā iti paṭahamavādayat, “yo māṃ paṃcaśailadvīpe netā sa dravyakoṭiṃ lapsyate" ekena niryāmakasthavireṇa paṭaho dhṛtaḥ koṭidhanaṃ cādade, tena yānapātraṃ sajjīkṛtaṃ, svarṇakṛtā sākaṃ potastho vṛddho'bdhivartmani sudūramāgatya tamityūce - bho kumāra ! dūre prekṣase kiṃcit ?, so'vocat dṛśyate vṛkṣa ekaḥ, sa ūce - abdhikūle tacchailapādajāto'yaṃ vaṭo dṛśyate, yadā poto'syādho yāti tadā'sya śākhāyāṃ tvaṃ vilageḥ, niśyatra bhāraṃḍāḥ sameṣyaṃti, teṣu supteṣu kasyacidraṃghrau paṭena svaṃ baddhvā dṛḍhamuṣṭinā vilageḥ, prātaruḍḍīnāste tvāṃ paṃcaśaile neṣyaṃti, yadi vaṭe'vilagnastvaṃ tadā etadyānapātramiva mahāvartte patitvā vinaṃkṣyasi tathā kṛte sa pakṣiṇā tatra ninye, sa krameṇa, hāsāprahāsābhyāṃ dṛṣṭaḥ, tenāpi bhogārthaṃ prārthite, tābhyāṃ proce - anenāṅgenāvayoḥ saṃgo na bhavet, kiṃtu agnipraveśādinā kṛtanidānena tvaṃ paṃcaśaileśo bhava, sa dadhyau - ubhayato bhraṣṭo'hamiti, ciṃtārttastābhyāṃ khapure muktaḥ, tataḥ sa devāṅganāṅgamohito'gnisādhanaṃ prārebhe tadā nāgilaśrāddhenoktammitra ! bālamaraṇaṃ nārhaṃ tavetyādi, tena vāryamāṇo'pi nidānenāgniśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā sa paṃcaśaileśo jajñe, nāgilastadvairāgyātpravrajyācyute devo jātaḥ | anyadā naṃdīśvarayātrārthaṃ prasthitānāṃ devānāṃ puro gātuṃ tadājñayā hāsāprahāse calite svapatimūcatuḥ - svāmin ! tvaṃ paṭahaṃ vādaya, so'haṃkāreṇa no'vādayat, tadā duṣkarmaṇā paṭahaḥ kaṃṭhe vyalagat, tadānīṃ tābhyāmuktaṃ - bho prāṇeśa ! kulocitaṃ kuru mā tvaṃ

lajjasva, tato vidyunmālī devo gāyantībhyāṃ tābhyāṃ saha paṭahaṃ vādayan tridivaukasāṃ purataścacāla, tadānīṃ nāgilaḥ svargī yātrāyāṃ vrajan vidyunmālinaṃ nijapūrvabhavamitraṃ avadhinā dadarśa, tataḥ svargiṇā sa bhāṣitaḥ-bhadra ! kimahaṃ karomi ?, acyutasureṇoktaṃ-mitra ! gārhasthyena citragṛhe kāyotsarge sthitasya bhāvasādho:ravibhormūrti tvaṃ kāraya, tava bodhibījamutpatsyate, taduktamurarīkṛtya gṛhe pratimāsthaṃ śrīvīramapaśyat, tato devo himavati śaile gatvā gośīrṣacaṃdanaṃ chittvā yathādṛṣṭaṃ mūrti savibhūṣaṇāṃ cakre | 25. kāmadhyāne śrītriṣaṣṭiśalākāpuruṣacaritre rāvaṇaḥ | śokena tena vaidehīvipralambharujāpi ca | phālacyuta iva dvīpī talpe tasthau nipatya saḥ ||1|| atha mandodarī devī tamupetyābhyadhāditi | kathaṃ prākṛtavat svāminniśceṣṭa iva tiṣṭhasi ||2|| rāvaṇo'pyabravīdevaṃ vaidehīvirahajvarāt | na ceṣṭituṃ na vaktuṃ ca na cālokayituṃ kṣamaḥ ||3|| mayā cejjīvatā te'rthastanmānaṃ projjhya mānini | gatvānunaya vaidehīṃ yathā mayi riraṃsate ||4|| nānyanārīmanicchantī bhuñje jātucidapyaham | argalāniyamo hyatra mamāsti gurusākṣikaḥ ||5|| pīḍitā pīḍayā patyuḥ kulīnā sāpi tatkṣaṇam | jagāma devaramaṇodyāne sītāmuvāca ca ||6|| eṣā mandodarī nāma daśānanamahiṣyaham | prapatsye tvayi dāsītvaṃ bhajasva daśakandharam ||7|| sīte tvameva dhanyāsi yāṃ siseviṣate'niśam | viśvasevyākimalaḥ patirmama mahābalaḥ ||8|| adyāpi tava rāmeṇa bhūcareṇa tapasvinā | pattimātreṇa kiṃ patyā prāpyate ceddaśānanaḥ ||9|| ruṣā babhāṣe sītaivaṃ kva siṃhaḥ kva ca jambukaḥ | kva suparṇaḥ kva vā kākaḥ kva rāmaḥ kva ca te patiḥ ||10|| dampatitvamaho yuktaṃ tava tasya ca pāpmanaḥ | rimsureko'nyastrīṣu dūtībhavati cāparā ||11|| druṣṭumapyucitā nāsi kimu sambhāṣituṃ hale | sthānādito gaccha gaccha tyaja dṛṣṭipathaṃ mama ||12|| rāvaṇo'pi tadā tatrājagāma nijagāda ca | kupitāsi kutaḥ sīte dāsī mandodarī tava ||13|| dāsaste svayamapyasmi prasādaṃ kuru devi me | jānaki tvaṃ janamamuṃ prīṇāsi na dṛśāpi kim ||14|| sītā parāṅmukhībhūyetyabhāṣata mahāsatī | kṛtāntadṛṣṭyā dṛṣṭo'si haranmāṃ rāmagehinīm ||15|| dhigāśāṃ te hatāśasyāprārthitaprārthakasya re | jīviṣyasi kiyadrāme sānuje dviṣadantake ||16||

brahmavirativratam | samāpte'bhigrahe samāyātāḥ sādhavaḥ, sthūlabhadraṃ vihāyetare duṣkarakārakā iti vadatopabaṃhitāḥ śeṣasādhusamakṣaṃ guruṇā | samāgataḥ sthūlabhadraḥ, taṃ prati sasambhramaṃ duṣkaraduṣkarakāraka iti vadatā kṛtopabṛṃhaṇā | tataḥ prāktanamaiśvaryamadhikṛtyā'yaṃ sukarakārako'pi varṇyate gurubhiḥ | aho lokācāraparatāmīṣām ! iti jātamitareṣāṃ cittakāluṣyam | tataḥ punaḥ samāhiṇḍyā'nyatra anyasmin varṣe tatraivāgateṣvabhigrahagrahaṇodyateṣu sādhuṣu siṃhaguhāvāsinoktamahaṃ kośābhaginyupakośāsadane cāturmāsaṃ niraśanaḥ sthāsyāmīti | nocitastvamasyeti nivārito guruṇā, pratikūlya tadvaco gato tadbhavanam | dṛṣṭastayā, sthūlabhadrā'sūyayā'yamāgata iti bhāvajñatayā saṃlakṣyānujñātā tayā tasya vasatiḥ | tato darśayāmyasya tadguṇā'sahanaphalamiti sañcintya rātrāvaticarcitaśarīrā madanotkocakāribhirgātravacanavibhranairupasargarupasargayitumārabdhā | tataścalacittatayā vismṛtātmāvasthena tena prārthitā sā prāha, na vayaṃ nirdhānānāṃ bhavāmaḥ | so'cintayaduttarāpathe śrūyate'pūrvasādhorlakṣamūlyakambaladātā rājā tad gacchāmi, tatra gataḥ, labdhvā kambalamāgataḥ samarpitavāśca tasyāḥ | tayāpi tasya paśyata eva nikṣipto'sau vaka़gṛhe sa prāha-kimayaṃ tvayā vināśitaḥ !' itarā''ha-'kimidaṃ jānīṣe ?' sa prāha- 'kimatra jñātavyaṃ ?' itarā''ha 'yadyevaṃ tarhi kimityaśucipūrṇena māmakadehenā'narghyaguṇaratnāvyatiriktātmā samparkamabhilasati ?' tadākārya punarāgataścaraṇapariṇāmaḥ | aho ! 27. kalahadhyāne śrīāvaśyakahāribhadrīyavṛttidarśitaṃ nāradodāharaṇam | ___ vāravaīe baladevaputtassa nisaḍhassa putto sāgaracaṃdo rūveṇaṃ ukkiṭṭho, savvesiṃ saṃbādīṇaṃ iṭṭho, tattha ya yāravaīe vatthavvassa ceva aṇṇassa raṇṇo kamalāmelānāma dhūā ukkiṭhṭhasarīrā, sā ya uggaseṇaputtassa ṇabhaseṇassa vareliyā, ito ya ṇārado sāgaracaṃdassa kumārassa sagāsaṃ āgato, abbhuṭṭhio, uvaviḍhe samāṇe pucchati-bhagavaṃ ! kiṃci accherayaṃ diṭuṃ ?, āmaṃ diṭuṃ, kahiṃ ? kaheha, iheva vāravaīe kamalāmelāṇāma dāriyā, kassai diṇṇiā ?, āmaṃ, kathaṃ mama tāe samaṃ saṃpaogo bhavejjā ?, ṇa yāṇāmitti bhaṇittāgato | so ya sāgaracaṃdo taṃ soūṇa ṇavi āsaṇe ṇavi sayaṇe dhitiṃ labhati, taṃ dāriyaṃ phalae lihaṃto ṇāmaṃ ca giṇhaṃto acchati, ṇārado'vi kamalāmelāe aṃtiaṃ gato, tāevi pucchio-kiṃci accherayaṃ diṭṭhapuvvaṃti, so bhaṇati-duve diṭṭhāṇi, rūveṇa sāgaracaṃdo virūvattaṇeṇa ṇabhaseṇao, sāgaracaṃde mucchitā ṇahaseṇae virattā, ṇāraeṇa samāsāsitā, teṇa gaṃtuṃ āikkhitaṃ-jahā icchātitti | tāhe sāgaracaṃdassa mātā aṇṇe a kumārā ādaṇṇā maraitti, saṃbo āgato jāva pecchati sāgaracaṃdaṃ vilavamāṇaṃ, tāhe ṇeṇa pacchato ṭhāiūṇa acchīṇi dohivi hatthehi chāditāṇi, sāgaracaṃdeṇa bhaṇitaṃ-kamalāmelatti, saṃbeṇa bhaṇitaṃ-ṇāhaṃ kamalāmelaḥ, kamalāmelo'haṃ, sāgaracaṃdeṇa bhaṇitaṃ-āmaṃ tumaṃ ceva mamaṃ vimalakamaladalaloaṇiṃ kamalāmelaṃ melihisi, tāhe tehiṃ kumārehiṃ saṃbo majjaṃ pāettā abbhuvagacchāvio, vigatamado ciṃteti-aho mae ālo abbhuvagao, idāṇīṃ ki sakkamaṇṇahākāuṃ ?, ṇivahiyavvaṃti pajjaṇṇaṃ paṇṇattiṃ maggiūṇa jaṃdivasaṃ tassa ṇabhaseṇassa vivāhadivaso taddivasaṃ te sāgaracaṃdasaṃbappamuhā kumārā ujjāṇaṃ gaṃtuṃ ṇāradassa sarahassaṃ dāsyiā suraṃgāe ujjāṇaṃ ṇettuṃ sāgaracaṃdo pariṇāvio, te tattha kiḍaṃtā acchaṃti | itare ya taṃ dāriyaṃ ṇa pecchaṃti, maggaṃtehiṃ ujjāṇa diṭṭhā, vijjāhararūvā viuvviyā, ṇārāyaṇo sabalo ṇiggao, jāva apacchimaṃ saṃbarūveṇaṃ pāesu paḍio, sāgaracaṃdassa ceva diṇṇā, ṇabhaseṇa taṇayā a khamāviyā | ettha sāgaracaṃdassa saṃbaṃ kamalāmelaṃ maṇṇamāṇassa aṇaṇuogo ṇāhaṃ kamalāmeleti bhaṇite aṇuogo | 28 yuddhadhyāne śrītriṣaṣṭiśalākāpuruṣacaritrā'ntargataṃ ceṭakena saha koṇikanṛpasya yuddhāvasaram | atha secanakārūḍhau divyakuṃḍalamaṃḍitau | divyahārāṃśukadharau bhūgatau svargiṇāviva ||1|| atyadbhutaśriyau hallavihallau nijadevarau | dṛṣṭvā padmāvatī dadhyau svasya strītvasya sannibham ||2|| vyasane prāpte'muṣmiṃstau nā''nayāmi cet | parābhavasahiṣṇorme vaṇijaśca kimantaram ||14|| anuśiṣya tato dūtaṃ vaiśālyāmupaceṭakam | preṣīd bhrātrormārgaṇāya ratnānyādāya jagmuṣoḥ ||15|| dūto'pi puryāṃ vaiśālyāṃ gatvā ceṭakaparṣadi | praṇamya ceṭakaṃ sthāne cā''sitvoce sasauṣṭhavaḥ ||16|| iha naṃṣṭvā samāyātau ratnaiḥ saha gajādibhiḥ | kumārau hallavihallau kūṇikasya samarpaya ||17|| anarpayannimau rājyabhraṃśamāsādayiṣyasi | kīlikārthe devakulaṃ na bhraṃśayitumarhasi ||18|| ceṭako'vocadanyo'pi nāpyeta śaraṇāgataḥ | dauhitrau kiṃ punarimau viśvastau putravatpriyau ||19|| dūto'bravīccharaṇyastvaṃ na hyarpayasi cedimau | tadratnānyanayorhatvā rājanmatsvāmine'rpaya ||20|| ceṭakaḥ smāha dharmo'yaṃ samāno rājaraṃkayoḥ | anyasya vittaṃ na hyanyo dātumīśīta jātucit ||21|| na prasahya na vā sāmnā'nayorgṛhṇami kiṃcana | dharmapātraṃ hi dauhitrau dānāhoṃ me viśeṣataḥ ||22|| dūto'pyāgatya caṃpāyāṃ tāṃ giraṃ ceṭakoditām | ākhyat svasvāminaḥ krodhavahnivātyāmanākula: ||23|| kūṇiko'pi hi tatkālaṃ jayabhaṃbhāmavīvadat | siṃhā iva parākṣepaṃ na sahante mahaujasaḥ ||24 || sainyānyananyasāmānyatejasastasya bhūpateḥ | sadyaḥ sarvābhisāreṇa sajjībhūyāvatasthire ||25|| kālādayaḥ kumārāśca daśāpi hi mahābalāḥ | puro babhūvuḥ saṃnā sarvasannahanena te ||26|| sahasrāṇi trīṇi gajāstāvanto vājino'pi hi | tāvantaśca rathāḥ koṭyastisro'pi ca padātayaḥ ||27 || teṣāṃ daśānāṃ pratyekaṃ kumārāṇāmidaṃ balam | etāvatkūṇikasyāpi prabhutvaṃ tvatiricyate ||28 ||
(yugmam) sainyenaitāvatā gacchaṃścaṃpeśaśceṭakaṃ prati | tirayāmāsa dharaṇiṃ taraṇiṃ ca rajobharaiḥ ||29||

prabhāvaṃ ceṭakasyā'muṃ mayā hā dhigajānatā | mayaiva nidhanaṃ nītā devābhā bhrātaro daśa ||55 || babhūva yā gatisteṣāṃ bhaviṣyati mamāpi sā | yujyate nāpasartuṃ ca dṛṣṭabhrātṛvadhasya me ||56|| taddevatāṃ samārādhya tatprabhāvājjayāmyarim | divyaprabhāvo divyena prabhāveṇa hi bādhyate ||57 || upāyamiti niścitya kṛtvā ca hṛdi devatām | tasthāvaṣṭamabhaktena rājā śreṇikanandanaḥ ||58 || pūrvajanmasaṃgatena nunnau tattapasāpi ca | śakraśca camarendraśca tatkālaṃ tamupeyatuḥ ||59|| ūcāte tau ca devendrāsurendrau bhoḥ kimicchasi ? | so'pyūce yadi tuṣṭau sthaśceṭakastanihanyatām ||60 || śakro bhūyo'pyabhāṣiṣṭā'paraṃ kimapi yācyatām | sādharmikaśceṭako me taṃ nihanmi na jātucit ||61|| tathāpi deharakṣāṃ te kariṣyāmi mahīpate ! | yathā na jīyase tena so'bravīdevamastviti ||62|| camarendro'pyatha mahāśilākaṃṭakamāhavam | anyaṃ ca rathamuśalaṃ jaitraṃ kartumamanyata ||63||| ādye śilāsannibhaḥ syāt patitaḥ karkaro'pi hi | kaṃṭako'pi ca jāyeta mahāśastrādhikaḥ khalu ||64 || dvitīye rathamuśale bhrāmyatī bhramikaṃ vinā | paritaḥ piṣyate tābhyāṃ vairisainyaṃ raṇotthitam ||65|| surendraścāsurendraśca narendraścāpi kūṇikaḥ | tatastrayo yuyudhire saha ceṭakasenayā ||66|| pautro'tha nāgarathino dvādaśavratapālakaḥ | samyagdṛṣṭiḥ ṣaṣṭabhojī sadā bhavaviraktadhīḥ ||67 || rājābhiyogataḥ ṣaṣṭabhaktānte'pi kṛtāṣṭamaḥ | śrīceṭakakṣitīśena svayamatyarthamarthitaḥ ||68|| saṃgrāme rathamuśale duḥsahe tādṛśe'pi hi | prāviśadvaruṇo nāma senānīḥ satyasaṃgaraḥ ||69||
(tribhirviśeṣakam) sa caṃpāpatisenānyaṃ raṇahetoḥ samākṣipan | prasasāra mahāsāro rathenā'sahyaraṃhasā ||70 || tau saṃmukhībhūtasthau ḍuḍhaukāte raṇecchayā | vairāyamāṇau bhūmiṣṭhabhānusvarbhānubhīṣaṇau ||71|| caṃpāpateścamūnātho varuṇaṃ raṇamārgaṇam | prahara praharetyuccaiājahāra puraḥ sthitam ||72|| varuṇaḥ smāha he doṣman ! śrāvakasyāsti me vratam | anighnate na prahāraṃ karavai vairiṇe'pi hi ||73|| sādhu sādhu mahāsattvetyuktvā caṃpeśasainyapaḥ | mumoca bāṇaṃ varuṇo vividhe tena marmaṇi ||74 || tataḥ kūṇikasenānyaṃ varuṇo'ruṇalocanaḥ | ekenaiva prahāreṇa nināya yamasadmani ||75 || gāḍhaprahāravidhuro niḥsṛtya varuṇo raṇāt | tṛṇasaṃstaramāsūtrya niṣadyedamacintayat ||76|| sarvātmanā śarīreṇa svāmikāryamanuṣṭhitam | idānīmantakālo me svārthasyāvasaraḥ khalu ||77 || arhadbhaṭṭārakāḥ sarve siddhāḥ sarve ca sādhavaḥ | kevalyupajño dharmaśca bhūyāsuḥ śaraṇaṃ mama ||78 ||

kṣamayāmi sarvāñjīvān sarve kṣāmyantu te mayi | maitrī me sarvabhūteṣu vairaṃ mama na kenacit ||79 | | māmakīnaṃ na kimapi na vā'hamapi kasyacit | mamakāramakārṣaṃ yaṃ tamapi vyutsṛjāmyaham ||80 || kāni kāni na mūḍho'haṃ pāpasthānānyaseviṣi | tanmithyā duṣkṛtaṃ me'stu gatarāgasya saṃprati ||81 || devatvanṛtvatiryaktvanārakatveṣu yanmayā | kṛtaṃ duṣkarma tadgaheṃ śrīvīro'rhan gatirmama ||82|| evamārādhanāṃ kṛtvā pratyācakhyau caturvidham | āhāramatha dadhyau ca namaskāraṃ samāhitaḥ ||83|| tadā ca varuṇasyaikaḥ suhṛnmithyādṛgāhavāt | bahirbhūtvopavaruṇamāgatyaivamavocata ||84 || vayasya ! tava sauhārdakrīto'hamapi saṃprati | tvadāsevitamadhyānaṃ prapanno'smyavidannapi ||85 || namaskāraparāvartī dharmadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ | samādhimaraṇaṃ prāpya saudharme varuṇo yayau ||86|| tadvimāne'ruṇābhākhye catuḥpalyopamapramam | pūrayitvāyurutpadya videheṣu sa setsyati ||87 || ajñānasevitenāpi varuṇasya tu vartmanā | vipadya tatsuhṛdbhūyo manuṣyaḥ sukule'bhavat ||88|| videheṣu manuṣyatvaṃ punarāsādya satkule | muktimārga samārādhya sa mokṣapadamāpsyati ||89 || hate ca varuṇe'bhūvaṃśceṭakasya camūbhaṭāḥ | yuddhāya dviguṇotsāhāḥ kāṇḍaspṛṣṭavarāhavat ||90 || gaṇarājasanāthaistaiśceṭakasya camūbhaṭaiḥ | akuṭṭi kūṇikacamūrdaśadbhiradharaṃ ruṣā ||91|| kuṭyamānaṃ balaṃ dṛṣṭvā svakīyamatha kūṇikaḥ | loṣṭāhataḥ siṃha iva krodhoddhatamadhāvata ||92|| sarasīva raṇe krīḍan kūṇiko vīrakuñjaraḥ | diśo diśi parabalaṃ padmakhaṃḍamivākṣipat ||13|| kūṇikaṃ durjayaṃ jñātvā ceṭako'thātyamarṣaṇaḥ | taṃ divyaṃ mārgaṇaṃ śauryadhano dhanuṣi sandadhe ||94 || itaśca vajrakavacaṃ kūṇikasya puro hariḥ | vyadhatta camarendrastu pṛṣṭhe sannāhamāyasam ||95 || cāpamākarṇamākṛṣya vaiśālīpatinā'pyatha | sa muktaḥ sāyako vajravarmaṇā skhalito'ntarā ||96|| amoghasyāpi bāṇasya tasya moghatvadarśanāt | camūbhaṭāśceṭakasya puṇyakṣayamamaṃsata ||97|| dvitīyaṃ nā'mucadbāṇaṃ satyasandhastu ceṭakaḥ | apasṛtya dvitīyasmin dine tadvadayudhyata ||18|| tathaiva moghabāṇo'bhūd dvitīye'pyahni ceṭakaḥ | evaṃ dine dine yuddhamatighoramabhūttayoḥ ||99|| lakṣāśītyā'dhikā koṭirbhaṭānāṃ pakṣayordvayoḥ | vipede yā sodapādi tiryakṣu narakeṣu ca ||100 || naṃṣṭvā svasvapuraṃ yātsu gaṇarājeṣu ceṭakaḥ | praṇaśya prāviśat puryāṃ kūṇiko'pi rurodha tām ||101|| tadā secanakārūḍhau caṃpeśasyākhilaṃ balam | vīrau hallavihallau tau rātrāvabhibabhūvatuḥ ||102|| na prahartuṃ navā dhartuṃ sa hastī svapnahastivat | kenāpyaśāki caṃpeśāśibire sauptikāgataḥ ||103||

mārayitvā mārayitvā niśi hallavihallayoḥ | kṣemeṇa gacchatormaṃtrimaṃḍalī smā''ha kūṇikaḥ ||104 || ābhyāṃ vidrutamasmākaṃ prāyeṇa sakalaṃ balam | tad brūta ka ihopāyo jaye hallavihallayoḥ ||105 || maṃtriṇo'pyūcire tau hi jetuṃ śakyau na kenacit | adhirūḍhau hi yāvattaṃ hastinaṃ narahastinau ||106|| tasmāttasyaiva kariṇo vadhāya prayatāmahe | khādirāṃgārasaṃpūṇāṃ kāryatāṃ pathi khātikā ||107|| chādayitvā ca vārīva durlakṣyā sā kariṣyate | tasyāṃ secanako vegādabhidhāvan patiṣyati ||108 || caṃpeśā'kārayadatha khādirāṃgārapūritām | khātikāmuparicchannāṃ tadāgamanavama॑ni ||109|| atha hallavihallāvapyavaskandakṛte niśi | nirīyatuḥ secanakādhirūḍhau jitakāśinau ||110 || aṃgārakhātikopāntametya secanako'pi hi | tāṃ vibhaṃgena vijñāya tasthau yātamamānayan ||111|| tato hallavihallābhyāmiti nirbhartsataḥ karī | paśurasyakṛtajño'si kātaro yadabhū raṇāt ||112|| videśagamanaṃ bandhutyāgaśca tvatkṛte kṛtaḥ | asmindurvyasane kṣiptastvatkṛte cāryaceṭakaḥ ||113 || varaṃ śvā poṣitaḥ śreyān bhaktaḥ svāmini yaḥ sadā | na tu tvaṃ prāṇavāllabhyādyo'smatkāryamupekṣase ||114 || iti nirbhartsato hastī kumārau nijapṛṣṭhataḥ | vegāduttārayāmāsa bhaktaṃmanyo balādapi ||115 || svayaṃ tu tasminnaṃgāragarte jhampāṃ dadau karī | sadyo vipadya cādyāyāmutpede narakāvanau ||116 || kumārau dadhyatudhigdhigāvābhyāṃ kimanuṣṭhitam | paśutvamāvayoḻaktaṃ na tu secanakaḥ paśuḥ ||117 || āryapādā yasya kṛte kṣiptā durvyasane ciram | taṃ svayaṃ nidhanaṃ nītvā jīvāvo'dyāpi durdhiyau ||118 | | āryasainyasya mahato nāśapratibhuvāviva | akṛṣvahi vṛthā nāśaṃ nīto bandhurabandhutām ||119 | | tannā'dya jīvituṃ yuktaṃ jīvāvaścedataḥ param | śiṣyībhūyārhato vīrasvāminaḥ khalu nānyathā ||120 || tadā śāsanadevyā tau bhāvaśramaṇatāṃ gatau | nītau śrīvīrapādānte parivavrajaturdvatam ||121 ||

211
bhagavaddeśanāṃ śrutvā'ṣṭanavatibharatānujairvrataṃ bheje | teṣāṃ rājyāni bharatanṛpo jagrāha | kā senāpatirnaradevaṃ natvaivaṃ vyajijñapat - "svāmin ! bāhubalī bhūpālastvayā na jitaḥ tasminnajite tvayā ṣaṭkhaṇḍaṃ jitamapyajitaṃ, yataścakraṃ śastrāgāre na praviśati ” | iti śrutvā vāgmī dūtaḥ takṣaśilāṃ prati preṣitaḥ | stokaprayāṇairgacchan bahuladeśe grāme grāme nagare nagare bahujanamukhato bāhubalino yaśaḥ śṛṇvan takṣaśilāyāṃ puri samāgataḥ | tataḥ pratihāreṇa suvego dūto bāhubalina ājñayā svarṇavarṇābhamantaḥsabhaṃ praveśitaḥ | taṃ bhūpaṃ namaskṛtya puraḥ sthitaḥ | bāhubalinā bāndhavanagarādikuśalaṃ pṛṣṭaṃ, taduttaraṃ dattvā prāha-“bho nṛpa ! tavāgrajanmā tvanmilanotsukastvāmāhvayati | atastvamekavāraṃ taṃ natvā tasyājñāṃ śirasi dhṛtvā'tra samāgaccheḥ | yato bāndhavo'pi bharatājñāṃ na manyate tarhyasyākiñcitkaraḥ parākrama iti lokāpavādavidhvaṃsāya, anyathā tava rājyasaṃśayo bhaviṣyati | yataḥ

senāpatimasranirjityākāśagatyā cakrigajasenāṃ vyagāhata, kandukāniva gajānullālya punarbhūmau patato muṣṭyā jaghāna | cakrī tamajayyaṃ vīkṣya cakraṃ mumoca | tadvīkṣya bhīto naṣṭaḥ merukandarasāgarādiṣu yatra yatra yāti tatra tatra cakraṃ pūrvārjitakarmavatpṛṣṭhaṃ na muñcati, tadā vidyayā vajrapañjaraṃ nirmāya tatra praviṣṭaḥ | __tadā cakrasurāḥ prāhuḥ-“re re raṅka ! kiṃ paurūṣaṃ viḍambayasi ?" | tataḥ ṣaṇmāsānte jātāmoṃ bahiniḥsṛtaḥ, tāvañcakraṃ, tacchiraśchitvā cakrihaste'tiṣṭhat | evaṃ yudhyamānayorddhādaśavarṣāṇi vyatīyuḥ | ekadā cakrijyeṣṭhātmajaḥ sūryayaśā bāhubalisainye dāvāgniriva prasasāra, kṣaṇāt pitṛvyābhyaṇe āgataḥ | taṃ balī prāha-"vatsa ! tvaṃ laghurasi, yadevaṃ mama senāṃ vigāhase tenāhaṃ hṛṣṭo'smi, tvayā putreṇāsmākaṃ vaṃśaḥ samudyotitaḥ, paraṃ trailokye'pi sa kaścinnāsti yo mama krodhaṃ sahate, tasmānmama dṛṣṭiṃ tyaktvā dūrato yohi" |
sa prāha-"tāta ! adya tvayā vinā ko mama saṅgrāmābhilāṣaṃ pūrayati ?" ityuktvā dhanuṣṭaṅkāravaṃ cakāra | tadā surāḥ saṃbhūya parasparasubhaṭayuddhaṃ niṣidhya dvayorbandhvoḥ śikṣāmiti prāha:-“bho yugādīśaputrau ! tātenedaṃ viśvaṃ pālitaṃ adhunā yuvāṃ kiṃ saṃhartuṃ pravṛttau ? etanna yujyate yuvayoḥ | balaparīkṣārthamubhābhyāmevāsmadādiṣṭairdaṣṭi 1 vāg 2 muṣṭi 3 bāhu 4 daṇḍāstra 5 lakṣaṇaiḥ pañcabhiryuddheryoddhavyaṃ" | iti suravākye dvābhyāmapi pratipanne suranarāḥ sākṣībhūya tasthuḥ | tadā cakrisainikā dadhyuḥ-"anena saha dvandvayuddhe śakrasyāpi jayaprāptau saṃdehastarhi asmatsvāmī kathaṃ jayaṃ prāpsyati ?" iti dhyāyato bhaṭān vīkṣya cakrī svabaladarśanāyaikaṃ mahākūpaṃ kārayitvā tasyopakaṇṭhe sthitvā svasya vāmabhujaṃ mahālohaśṛṅkhalābhirbaddhvā sarvasainikān prāha-“yūyaṃ sarve'pi saṃbhūya śṛṅkhalākarṣaṇena māṃ gartāyāṃ kṣipata" | taiḥ sarvaiḥ karṣito'pi manāg na cacāla | yataścakriṇaṃ bharatotpannasarvanaranārīgajāśvādayaḥ saṃbhūyākarṣaṇaṃ kartuṃ tilamātramapi na kṣamāḥ tarhi senābhiḥ kiṃ bhavet ? | tataścakrī hṛdayalepakaraṇamiṣeṇa svahastamācakarṣa, tena te sarve'pi śṛṅkhalālagnā latālagnāḥ pakṣiṇa iva lambante sma | iti parākramaṃ vīkṣya hṛṣṭāḥ sākṣiṇa iva dūre tasthuḥ |
tataścakribhūpau prāgdṛṣṭiyuddhārthaṃ saṃmukhaṃ sthitau animeṣadṛṣṭyā'valokayataḥ sma | tāmralocanau bhayaṃkarāvabhūtāṃ | tato bāhubalimukhaṃ bhīṣaṇākṣaṃ vilokayataścakriṇo locane bāṣpākule vegātpramilite | tatsainyaṃ sarvaṃ nyagmukhaṃ jātaṃ | tato lajjitaṃ cakriṇaṃ sa prāha- "bhrātaḥ ! kimudvigno'si ? kuru vāgyuddhaṃ mayā saha" | iti tenoktaścakrī mahāghoraṃ siṃhanādaṃ cakāra, tena jātaṃ badhiraṃ skandhāvāramaṇḍalam | tataḥ sāmarṣo bāhubalī siṃhanādaṃ cakāra tena sainikāḥ sarve'pi mūrchitā iva babhūvuḥ | punarapi cakrī bhūpaśca siṃhanādaṃ duḥsahaṃ cakratuḥ | tadā cakriṇo dhvaniḥ pūrvāhnacchāyeva durjanasauhṛdamiva

yathākramaṃ hīyamāno'bhūt | tena vilakṣaṃ cakriṇaṃ bhūpa: smāha-“he bhrātaḥ ! mā viṣīda, mayā kākatālīyanyāyena yadi jitaṃ tarhi kiṃ jātaṃ ? idānī sajjo bhava muṣṭiyuddhāya" iti bhūpenotsāhitaḥ proccairbhujāsphoṭaṃ kṛtvotplutyānyo'nyamāśliṣya bhujābhyāṃ pīḍayantau jagarjatuḥ | tato bhūpaścakriṇaṃ kareṇa gṛhītvā līlayā kandukavaduñcairākāśe udalālayat | kṣaṇādadṛśyo'bhūt | tena sainyadvaye'pi hāhāravaḥ prasṛtaḥ | bhūpaścintayā dadhyau-“yāvadāryo'yaṃ bhūmau patitvā na viśīryate tāvadantarikṣādeva gṛhṇāmi” iti vicintya talpākārau karau kṛtvordhvadṛṣṭistasthau | cireṇa sa āyayau | balinā bāhū prasārya dhṛtaḥ | tataḥ krodhādhmātaścakrī tanmūrdhni muṣṭighātena tamamūrchayat | bhūpo'pi cireṇa prāptacaitanyaścakriṇaṃ hṛdi muṣṭighātaṃ kṛtvā mūrchā gamitavān | kṣaṇāllabdhasaṃjñaścakrī roṣāruṇo daṇḍena bhūpamūrdhyatāḍayat, tena mukuṭaḥ śatakhaṇḍaḥ kṛtaḥ | kṣaṇaṃ netre nimīlya bhūpo lohamayadaṇḍena cakrisannāhaṃ śakalīcakāra | tataścakrī daṇḍaghātena bhūpaṃ jānupramāṇaṃ bhūmau magnīcakāra | tato nirgatyāyasadaṇḍena bhūpena nihataścakrī ākaṇṭhaṃ bhūmau magnaḥ | kṣaṇāccaitanyaṃ prāpya nirgato dadhyo-“bhūpaiścakrī na jīyate" itīyaṃ śāśvatī sthitirasti, anenāhaṃ pañcabhiryuddhairjitaḥ tarhi mayi cakritvaṃ na hi" iti dhyāyatastasya pāṇau cakraratnamupāgatam | tadā cakrī bhūpaṃ smāha-“adyāpi tava kiñcinna naṣṭaṃ, mamājñāṃ manyasva, no cedanena cakreṇa yamātithirbhaviṣyasi" | bhūpaḥ smāha-"vākyāḍambaraṃ vṛthā kiṃ vahasi ? varise 2 paḍāyaṃ giṇhai, rāyā ciṃtei esa annāo rajjāo āgaṃtūṇa paḍāyaṃ harai, esa mama ohāvaṇatti paḍimalaṃ maggaḍa़, teṇa ego macchio diṭṭho vasaṃ pibaṃto, balaṃ ca se vinnāsiya, nāūṇa posio, puṇaravi aṭṭaṇo āgao, so ya kira maho hohititti aṇāgayaṃ ceva sayāo ṇayarāo appo patthayaṇassa avallaṃ bhariūṇa avvābāheṇaṃ ei, saṃpatto ya sopārayaṃ, juddhe parājio macchiyamalleṇaṃ, gao ya sayaṃ āvāsaṃ ciṃtei, eyassa vuḍḍī taruṇayassa mama hāṇī, aṇṇaṃ mallaṃ maggai, suṇai ya-suraṭṭhāe atthitti, eeṇa bharuyacchāharaṇīe gāme dūrullakūviyāe karisago diṭṭho- egeṇa hattheṇa halaṃ vāhei egeṇa phalahio uppāḍei, taṃ ca daṭṭhūṇa ṭhioṃ pecchāmi se āhāraṃti, āvallā mukkā, bhajjā ya se bhattaṃ gahāya āgayā, patthiyā, kūrassa ujjhamajjīe ghaḍao pecchai, jimio saṇṇābhūmi gao, tatthavi pecchai savvaṃ vattiyaṃ, vegālio vasahiṃ tassa ya ghare maggai dinnā, ṭhio, saṃkahāe pucchai,kā jīviyā ?, te kahie bhai- ahaṃ aṭṭaṇo tumaṃ īsaraṃ karemitti, tīse bhajjāe se kappāsamollaṃ dinnaṃ, avallā ya, sā savaladdhā ujjeṇiṃ gayā, vamaṇavireyaṇāṇi kayāṇi posio nijuddhaṃ ca sikkhāvio, puṇaravi mahimākāle teṇeva vihiṇā āgao, paḍhamadivase phalahiyamallo macchiyamallo ya juddhe egovi na parājio, rāyā biiyadivase hohititti aigao, imevi sae sae ālae gayā, aṭṭaṇeṇa phalahiya-mallo bhaṇio - kahehi puttā ! jaṃ te dukkhāviyaṃ, teṇa kahiyaṃ, makkhittā'kkheveṇaṃ puṇaṇṇavīkayaṃ, macchiyassavi raṇṇā saṃmaddagā pesiyā, bhaṇai ahaṃ tassa piupi ṇa bibhemi ko so varāo ?, bitiyadivase samajujjhā, tatiyadivase aṃbiyapahāro vaisāhaṃ ṭhio macchio, aṭṭaṇeṇa bhaṇio phalihitti, teṇa phalahiggāheṇa gahio sīse, taṃ kuṃḍiyanālagaṃpiva egaṃte paḍiyaṃ, sakkārio gao ujjeṇi, paṃcalakkhaṇāṇa bhogāṇa ābhāgī jāo, iyaro mao |

mā bhūdvināśo jalakāyikānāṃ, maddehasaṅgena tadeti vṛṣṭyām | bhikṣānivṛtto rathanemisādhurviveśa kasyāṃcana kandarāyām ||1|| vyāvartamānā praṇipatya nāthaṃ, rājīmatī sā'pi bhayena vṛṣṭeḥ | pūrvapraviṣṭaṃ tamajānatī drāgviveśa tāmeva guhāṃ nagasya ||2|| gatā'pi madhye na tamandhakāre, dadarśa koṇe sthitametadīye | klinnāni kiñcidvasanāni sādhvī, tataśca niḥśoṣayituṃ pravṛttā ||3|| svargalokajayāyaiva, sādhayantīṃ tapaḥkriyām | dṛṣṭvā vivasanāṃ tanvīṃ, babhūvotkalikākula: ||4|| etasya sodareṇāhamājanmāpi tiraskṛtaḥ | iti bhrātṛvirodhena, smaro munimapīḍayat ||5|| piṇḍitaṃ sṛṣṭisarvasvamivāsau hā mṛgekṣaṇā | naikaśo'pi mayā bhuktā, dhimme janma nirarthakam ||6|| īṣatkampavapurbhṛtya, ivotthāya visaṃsthulaḥ |

bhavadattaṃ tataścaikamapi tatrā''diśad guruḥ | ekākino'pyarhati hi vihāro vaśino muneḥ || 18 || bhavadatto jagāmātha sveṣāṃ saṃsāriṇāṃ gṛhe | pravrajyāgrāhaṇenānugrahītumanujaṃ nijam ||19|| nāgadattasya tanayāṃ vāsukīkukṣisambhavām | upayeme bhavadevo bhavadattānujastadā ||20|| kṛtodvāhotsavāḥ sarve bandhavastaṃ mudā'bhyayuḥ | manyamānā utsavoparyutsavaṃ tatsamāgamam ||21|| sadyaḥ pādyena tatpādau prakṣālya prāsukena te | pādodakamavandanta matvā tīrthodakādhikam ||22|| bhavābdhimajjanabhayādavalambamivecchavaḥ | lagitvā pādayoḥ sarve bandhavastaṃ vavandire || 23 || munirapyabhyadhādvandhūn vivāhavyākulāḥ stha bhoḥ ! | yāmo vihartumanyatra dharmalābho'stu vo'naghāḥ ! ||24|| tamṛṣiṃ bandhavaḥ sarve bhaktapānadibhirmudā | eṣaṇīyakalpanīyaprāsukaiḥ pratyalābhayan ||25|| tadānīṃ bhavadevo'pi kulācāraṃ prapālayan | sevyamānāṃ vayasyābhirnavoḍhāṃ maṇḍayannabhūt ||26|| cakre'ṅgarāgaṃ preyasyā śrīcandanarasena saḥ | candrātaparasenevākṛṣṭena śaśimaṇḍalāt ||27|| tasyā mūrdhni ca dhammillaṃ sumanodāmagarbhitam | babandha grasta śaśinaḥ svarbhāṇoḥ śrīmalimlucam ||28|| tatkapolaphalakayoḥ kastaryā patravallarīm | mīnaketoriva jayapraśastimalikhatsvayam ||29|| kucayormaṇḍanaṃ yāvannavoḍhaḥ sa pracakrame | tāvadāgamamaśrauṣīdbhavadattamahāmuneḥ || 30 || sa bhrātṛdarśanottālaḥ kitavo jayavāniva | drāgudasthādvihāyārdhamaṇḍitāmapi vallabhām ||31|| hitvā'rdhamaṇḍitāṃ kāntāṃ na gantumucitaṃ tava | tasyāḥ sakhīnāmityuktiṃ sa eḍa iva nāśṛṇot || 32 || sāgrahaṃ vārayantīnāṃ tāsāṃ cetyuttaraṃ dadau | kṛtvā gurupraṇipātaṃ punareṣyāmi bālikāḥ ! ||33|| bhavadattastataḥ sthānātplavamānaḥ plavaṅgavat | abhyetya bhavadattarṣiṃ tatra sthitamavandata || 34 || vanditvotthitamātrasyānujasya ghṛtabhājanam | muniḥ śrāmaṇyadānāya satyaṅkāramivārpayat ||35|| bhavadattastato'gārādanagāraśiromaṇiḥ | nirjagāma dhiyāṃ dhāma manāgbhrātari dattadṛk || 36 || bhavadevo'pi tatsarpirbhājanaṃ dhārayankare | anvagādbhavadattarṣiṃ tatpadāmbhojaṣaṭpadaḥ || 37 || anye'pi bahavo nāryo narāśca bhavadevavat | anvayurbhavadattarṣimudūrmipramadahūdāḥ || 38 || muni kañcisṛjat munīnāmucitaṃ hyadaḥ | avisṛṣṭāśca muninā na vyāvavṛtire janāḥ || 39 || dūraṃ gatvā ca nirviṇṇāstaṃ vanditvā mahāmunim | svayameva vyājughuṭurādau nāryo narāstataḥ ||40|| bhavadevastu bhadrātmā cintayāmāsivānidam | apyavisṛṣṭā vyāghuṭantvete naite hi sodarāḥ | | 41 | | ahaṃ tu sodaro'muṣya dvāvāvāṃ snehalau mithaḥ | tadanenāvisṛṣṭasya nyāyyaṃ vyāghuṭanaṃ na me ||42||

yūthabhraṣṭeva hariṇī pāśabaddhava śārikā | sā manye dainyabhāglokānukampyaiva bhaviṣyati ||68 || (yugmam) yadi prāpsyāmi jīvantīṃ tāṃ priyāmāyatekṣaṇām | tadadyāpi hi gārhasthyātṛpto raṃsye tayā saha ||69 || cintātantubhirevaṃ svaṃ niyacchannūrṇanābhavat | sthavirarṣīnanāpṛcchya bhavadevo viniryayau ||70 || tūrṇaṃ jagāma ca grāmaṃ sugrāmaṃ rāṣṭrakūṭabhūḥ | tasthau ca saṃvṛtadvārabāhyāyatanasannidhau ||71|| gandhamālyadharā nārī brāhmaṇyā samamekayā | tannābhyāgānmunirasāvityavandata taṃ ca sā ||72|| papraccha bhavadevastāṃ rāṣṭrakūṭaḥ sa āryavān | patnī ca revatī tasya bhadre ! jīvati vā na vā ? ||73|| kathayāmāsa sāpyevamāryavānnevatī ca sā | vyapadyetāṃ tayoścāgād bhūyānkālo vipannayoḥ ||74 || bhūyo'pyapṛcchatsa munirāryavatsūnunā priyā | bhavadevena yā tyaktā navoḍhā sā'sti vā na vā ? ||75 || sā dadhyau bhavadevo'yaṃ nūnamāttavrato'grajāt | yadi vā vārtayāmyetamanenasamihāgatam ||76|| uvāca cintayitvaivamāryavadrevatīsutaḥ | tvameva bhavadevo'si ? kimihāgāstapodhana ! ||77 || bhavadevo'vadatsādhu tvayā'hamupalakṣitaḥ | sa eva bhavadevo'smi nāgilājīviteśvaraḥ ||78 || tadā'grajoparodhena tāṃ vimucya nirīyuṣā | anicchatāpi hi mayā vratamādāyi duṣkaram ||79|| vipanne sāmprataṃ bhrātaryahamaṅkuśavarjitaḥ | nāgilā sā kathamabhūdityāgāṃ taddidṛkṣayā ||8|| nāgilā cintayāmāsa cirād dṛṣṭāṃ hi māmasau | na hi pratyabhijānāti parāvṛttavayoguṇām ||81|| ātmānaṃ jñāpayāmyenamiti provāca nāgilā | nāgilā sā'smyaṃha hanta navoḍhā'tyāji yā tvayā ||82|| etāvatā ca kālena yauvane'pi vyatīyuṣi | kiṃ nāma mayi lāvaṇyaṃ puṇyāśaya ! vimṛśyatām ||83|| muktvā ratnatrayaṃ svargāpavargaphaladāyakam | varāṭikāmātranibhāṃ mā grahīta~ mahāśaya ! ||84 || atyantaghoranarakapātapratibhuvāmaho | viṣayāṇāṃ smarāstrāṇāṃ mā gāstvaṃ bhedanīyatām ||85|| grāhito'si vrataṃ bhrātrā chadmanā'pi hitaiṣiṇā | tamapyanāptaṃ mā maṃsthā mayi pāpakhanau rataḥ ||86|| tadadyāpi nivartasva gurupādānanuvraja | mayi rāgakṛtaṃ cāghamālocaya tadantike ||87|| yāvadevaṃ bhavadevaṃ nāgilā bhṛśamānvaśāt | brāhmaṇyā dārakastāvattatrā''gād bhuktapāyasaḥ ||88|| ūce ca pāyasaṃ bhuktaṃ yanmayā'dya sudhopamam | tadvamiṣyāmyahaṃ mātaradho dhāraya bhājanam ||89|| nimantrito'hamanyatra lapsye tatra ca dakṣiṇām | avāntapāyaso mātabhktuṃ śakṣyāmi no punaḥ ||10||

brāhmaṇyuvāca vāntāśī jugupsyastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi | alaṃ jugupsanīyena karmaṇā'nena dāraka !||92 || tacchrutvā bhavadevo'pi nijagādeti he baṭo ! | tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi vāntāśī nikṛṣṭaḥ kukkurādapi ||13|| nāgilovāca tamṛṣi yadyevaṃ vetsi vakṣi ca | tanmāmudvamya kimiti bhūyo'pyupabubhukṣase ||94 || māṃsamṛgasthiviṇmūtrapūrṇā'hamadhamādhamā | vāntādapi jugupsyā'smi māmicchan kiṃ na lajjase ? ||95 || paśyasyadrau jvaladagniṃ na punaḥ pādayoradhaḥ | yatparaṃ śikṣayasyevaṃ na svaṃ śikṣayasi svayam ||96|| kā hi puṃgaṇanā teṣāṃ ye'nyaśikṣāvicakṣaṇāḥ | ye svaṃ śikṣayituṃ dakṣāsteṣāṃ puṃgaṇanā nṛṇām ||97 || bhavadevo'vadatsādhu śikṣito'smi tvayānaghe ! | ānītaḥ pathi jātyandha iva yānahamutpathe ||98 || tadadya svajanāndṛṣṭvā yāsyāmi gurusannidhau | vratātīcāramālocya tapsye'haṃ dustapaṃ tapaḥ ||99 || nāgilā'pyavadat kiṃ te svajanaiḥ svārthabhāgbhava | mūrtimanto hi te vighnā bhāvino gurudarśane ||100 || tad gaccha gurupādānte dāntātmā vratamācara | pravrajiṣyāmyahamapi vratinījanasannidhau ||101|| bhavadevo'tha vanditvā'rhadvimbāni samāhitaḥ | gatvā gurvantike'kārṣītkṛtyamālocanādikam ||102|| śrāmaṇyaṃ niratīcāraṃ bhavadevaḥ prapālayan | kālaṃ kṛtvā''dikalpe'bhūcchakrasāmānikaḥ suraḥ ||103||

kilissasi ?, teṇa bhaṇiyaṃ-kilesasahaṃ me sarīraṃ vāvāraṃtaraṃ ceyāṇi natthi mahagghāṇi ya vāsāratte dārugāṇitti nivvahiyavvā ya paiṇṇatti ao karemitti, raṇṇā bhaṇiyaṃ-pujjaṃtu te maṇorahā, tumaṃ ceva bitijjagaṃ pūriuṃ samattho na puṇa ahaṃti niggao, teṇa kāleṇa pūrio | 33. krayavikrayadhyāne śrīsaṃvegaraṅgaśālāmadhye nandakathā | pāḍaliputtaṃ nagare, bahusamaraviḍhattavijayajasapasaro | āsi jayaseṇanāmo, naranāho bhūriguṇakalio ||1||
tattha ya purammi adhariya-kuberadhaṇavittharā parivasaṃti |
naṃdapamokkhā vaṇiṇo, jiṇadāsā''ī susaḍḍhā ya ||2|| aha egammi avasare, samuddadattā'bhihāṇavaṇieṇa | cirakāliyaṃ sarovaramā''raddhaṃ khāṇiuṃ ekkaṃ ||3||
tattha khaṇijaṃtammi, uḍḍehiṃ puvvapurisapakkhittā | laddhā suvannakusayā, cirakāliyakiṭṭacayamaliṇā ||4|| to lohamaya tti viyāṇiūṇa, vaṇiyāṇa tehiṃ uvaṇīyā |

kiṃ re ! kusayā gahiyā, na va tti teṇaṃ payaṃpiyaṃ tāya ! | bahumulla tti na gahiyā, to tāḍito uraṃ naṃdo ||17|| hā ! hā ! muṭṭho tti payaṃpiro ya, jaṃghāu bhaṃjau kuseṇa |
eyāṇamesa doso, jeṇa gao haṃ paragihe tti ||18 || to vajjho āṇatto, so rannā avahaḍaṃ ca savvassaṃ | emā''ī bahū dosā, icchāviṇivittirahiyāṇaṃ ||19|| iya khamaga ! maṇāṃga pi hu, pariggahe mā maṇo dharijjāsi | khaṇadiṭṭhanaṭṭharūve, kā vaṃchā tattha dhīrāṇa ||20|| evaṃ pariggahavisayaṃ, paṃcamagaṃ pāvaṭhāṇagaṃ vuttaṃ | kovasarūvā''veyaṇa-parametto bhannai chaṭuṃ ||21||
[śrīsaṃvegaraṃgaśālā śloka 5888 taḥ 5909] 34. anarthadaṇḍadhyāne śrītriṣaṣṭiśalākāpuruṣacaritre gaṅgadattasyāvasaraḥ | itaśca hāstinapure śreṣṭhyabhūt ko'pi tasya ca | lalitākhyo'bhavat putro māturatyaṃtavallabhaḥ ||1|| śreṣṭhinyānyadā jajñe garbhaḥ saṃtāpado bhṛśam | tayā ca vividhadravyaiḥ pātyamāno'pi nāpatat ||2|| jātaśca putraḥ śreṣṭhinyā dāsyai tyaktuṃ ca so'rpitaḥ | dṛṣṭaśca śreṣṭhinā pṛṣṭā kimetaditi dāsyatha ||3|| asāvaniṣṭaḥ śreṣṭhinyātyājyateti tayodite | śreṣṭhī gṛhītvā taṃ channamanyatra samavardhayat ||4|| gaṃgadatta iti nāmādatta tasya śiśoḥ pitā | mātuśchannaṃ lalito'pi lālayāmāsa taṃ sadā ||5|| pitaraṃ lalito'nyedhurabhyadhatta madhūtsave | bhojyate gaṃgadatto'dya saha cecchobhanaṃ tadā ||6|| śreṣṭhayūce yadi te mātā taṃ paśyettanna śobhanam | lalito'pyabravīttāta yatiṣye tadadarśane ||7|| śreṣṭhināpi tathādiṣṭo lalito gaṃgadattakam | tiraskariṇyāṃtaritaṃ bhojanārthaṃ nyaṣedayat ||8|| purastāt svayamāsīnau tau śreṣṭhilalitau tadā | bhuṃjānau gaṃgadattāya bhojyaṃ channaṃ ca yacchataḥ ||9|| vātoddhṛte'kāṃḍapaṭe śreṣṭhinī taṃ dadarśa ca | keśaiḥ kṛṣṭvā kuṭṭayitvā gṛhaśrotasi cākṣipat ||10|| udvignau śreṣṭhilalitau śreṣṭhinyāśchannameva tam | snapayitvābodhayatāṃ gaṃgadattaṃ mahāmatī ||11|| tadā ca sādhavastatra bhikṣārthaṃ samupāgatāḥ | pṛṣṭāśca tābhyāṃ śreṣṭhinyāstatputradveṣakāraṇam ||12|| athaikaḥ sādhurityākhyadekasmin saṃniveśane | abhūtāṃ bhrātarau tau tu bahiḥ kāṣṭhārthamīyatuḥ ||13|| kāṣṭhairbhūtvā tathā gaṃtrīmagrastho jyeṣṭha āpatat | dadarśa pathi vellatī cakkaluṇḍā mahoragīm ||14|| so'pyavocat kanīyāṃsaṃ gaṃtryāḥ sārathimagrataḥ | gaṃtrīto rakṣaṇīyeyaṃ cakkaluṃḍā varākikā ||15||

kāṃpīlyapureśasya brahmarājñaścatvāri mitrāṇi santi-kāśīdeśapaḥ kaṭakaḥ, gajapureśaḥ kareṇuḥ (ṇeruḥ), kośaleśo dīrghaḥ, caṃpādhīśaḥ puṣpacūlaśca | ete nṛpāḥ snehenaikaikaṃ varṣaṃ mitho rājyeṣu samuditāstiṣṭhanti | anyadā te sarve brahmapārśve samāgatāḥ | tāvatā śiroroge samutpanne brahmarājā tañcaturṇāmutsaṃge sutaṃ muktvā 'yuṣmābhiretadvājyasaṃpradāyaḥ kārayitavyaḥ' ityuktvā mṛtaḥ | te'pi dīrgha muktvā svarājye gatāḥ | tato dīrghaśculaṇyāsakto jātaḥ | tadbhāvaṃ dhanurmaṃtrī jñātvā svaputrasya varadhanurnāmno jñāpitavān-yathaitatsvarūpamekānte cūlaneyasya brahmadattasya jñāpyam | tena jñāpito'sau kokilākākayoḥ saṃbandhaṃ kārayannantaraḥpure gatvā kākaṃ hatvovāca-'yo'nyo'pyevaṃ kariṣyati, tamahaṃ nigrahīṣyāmi'| tadvārtā dīrghaśculanī pratyāha-'ahaṃ kākastvaṃ kokilā' iti | sāha-'bālo'yaṃ kiṃ vetti āvayoḥ svarūpaṃ'| punarekadā mahiṣahastinīyugamānīya pūrvoktavākyamuktvā mahiṣo hataḥ | tato dīrghaḥ-'matpāpamanena jñātaṃ, anyoktyā māṃ śikṣayati' iti vicārya rājñī prati jñāpito'yaṃ vicāraḥ| tataḥ sā prāha-“ayaṃ putro hanyate, āvayoḥ kuśalatve bahavo'pi putrā bhaviṣyanti” | tenāpi svīkṛtam | tataḥ sāmantasutāṃ pariṇāyya tacchayanārthaṃ jatugṛhaṃ kārayitvā dattam | varadhanurmatriṇā guptavṛttyā tagṛhamadhyataḥ purodyānaṃ yāvatsuraṃgā dāpitā | śubhadine jatugṛhe praviṣṭasya savadhūvaradhanuḥkumārasya rātriyāmadvaye gate gṛhaṃ prajvālitam | tau tu suraṃgāya nirgatya pūrvadvāre muktāśvayugalamārā nirgatau | pṛthvī bhramato brahmadattasya sārvabhaumasaṃpadamilat | varadhanuḥ sainyapatipade'bhiṣicya dīrghasamīpe preṣitaḥ | tadanu brahmadatta āgatya nijacakreṇa dīrghavapuḥ kabandhīkṛtya svageha āgataḥ | anukrameṇa ṣaṭkhaṃḍabharataṃ sādhitam |

37. ṛṇāviladhyāne śrīpiṇḍaniyuktivṛttau tailakarṣalastryāḥkathānakam |
kośalāviṣaye ko'pi grāmastatra devarājo nāma kuṭumbī, sārikābhidhā tasya bhāryā, tasyāśca sammatapramukhā bahavaḥ sutāḥ, sammatiprabhṛtayaśca prabhūtā dārikāḥ, tañca sakalamapi kuṭumbaṃ paramaśrāvakaṃ, tathā tasminneva grāme śivadevo nāma śreṣṭhī, tasya bhāryā śivā ||
anyadā ca samudraghoṣābhidhāḥ sūrayaḥ samāgacchan, teṣāṃ samīpe jinapraṇītaṃ dharmamākarṇya jātasaṃvegaḥ sammato dīkṣāṃ gṛhītavān, kālakrameṇa ca gurucaraṇaprasādato'tīva gītārthaḥ samajani | sa cānyadā cintayāmāsa-yadi madīyaḥ ko'pi pravrajyāṃ gṛhṇāti tataḥ śobhanaṃ bhavati, idameva hi, tāttvikamupakārakaraṇaṃ yatsaṃsārārṇavāduttāraṇamiti, tata evaṃ cintayitvā gurūnāpṛcchaya nijabandhugrāme samāgamat tatra ca bahiḥ pradeśe kamapi pariṇatavayasaṃ pṛṣṭavān puruṣaṃ-yathā'tra devarājābhidhasya kuṭumbinaḥ satkaḥ ko'pi vidyate ? iti, sa prāha-mṛtaṃ sarvamapi tasya kuṭumbaṃ kevalamekā sammatyabhidhā vidhavā putrikā jīvatīti, tataḥ sa tasyā gṛhe jagāma, sā ca bhrātaramāyāntaṃ dṛṣṭvā manasi bahumānamudvahantī vanditvā kañcikatkālaṃ paryupāsya ca tannimittamāhāraṃ paktumupatasthe, sādhuśca tāṃ nivāritavān-yathā na kalpate'smākamasmannimittaṃ kimapi kṛtamiti, tato bhikṣāvelāyāṃ sā durgatatvenānyatra kvacidapi tailamātramapyalabhamānā kathamapi śivadevābhidhasya vaṇijo vipaṇestailapalikādvayaṃ dine dine dviguṇavṛddhirūpeṇa kālāntareṇa samānīya bhrātre dattavatī, bhātrā ca taṃ vṛttāntamajānatā śuddhamiti jñātvā pratijagṛhe, sā ca taddinaṃ bhrātuḥ sakāśe dharmaṃ śrutavatī, tena na pānīyānayanādinā tattailapalikādvayaṃ praveśayituṃ prapāritavatī, dvitīye ca dine bhrātā (yathā) vihārakrama gataḥ, tatastasminnapi dine tadviyogaśokākīrṇamānasatayā na tattailapalikādvayaṃ dviguṇībhūtaṃ praveśayituṃ śaktavatī, tṛtīye ca dine karṣadvayamṛṇe jātaṃ taccātiprabhūtatvānna praveśayituṃ śaktam, apica bhojanamapi pānīyānayanādinā karttavyaṃ, tato bhojanāyaiva yatnavidhau sakalamapi dinaṃ jagāmeti na ṛṇaṃ praveśayit śaknoti, tato dine dine dviguṇavṛddhyā pravarddhamānamṛṇamaparimitaghaṭapramāṇaṃ jātaṃ, tataḥ śreṣṭhinā sā babhaṇe-yathā mama tailaṃ dehi yadvā me dāsī bhava, tataḥ sā tailaṃ dātumaśaknuvatī dāsatvaṃ pratipede, kiyatsu ca varṣeṣvatikrānteṣu bhūyo'pi sammatābhidhaḥ sādhustasminneva grāme yathāvihārakramamāgamat, sā ca bhaginī svagṛhe na dṛṣṭā, tata āgatā satī papracche, tayā ca prācīnaḥ sarvo'pi vyatikarastasmai nyavedi yāvaddāsatvaṃ śivadevagṛhe jātamiti, nivedya ca svaduḥkhaṃ rodituṃ pravṛttā, tataḥ sādhuravocat-mā rodīracirādahaṃ tvāṃ mocayiṣyāmi, tatastasyā mocanopāyaṃ cintayan prathamataḥ śivadevasyaiva gṛhe praviveśa, śivā ca tasya bhikṣādānārthaṃ jalena hastau prakṣālayituṃ lagnā, tāṃ ca sādhurnivārayāmāsa,

khaṇḍa:-2 dṛṣṭāntasamuccayaḥ
227
yathaivamasmākaṃ na kalpate bhikṣeti, tataḥ samīpadeśavartī śreṣṭhī provāca-ko'tra doṣaḥ ?, tataḥ sādhuḥ kāyavirādhanādīn doṣān yathāgamaṃ savistaramacīkathat tataḥ sa āto bhaṇati yathā bhagavan ! kutra yuṣmākaṃ vasatiryena tatrāgatā vayaṃ dharmaṃ śṛṇumaḥ tataḥ sādhuravādīt-nāsti me'dyāpi pratiśrayaḥ, tatastena nijagṛhaikadeśe vasatiradāyi, pratidinaṃ ca dharmaṃ śṛṇoti, samyaktvamaṇuvratāni ca pratipannāni, sādhuśca kadācanāpi vāsudevādipūrvapuruṣācīrṇānanekānabhigrahan vyāvarṇayāmāsa, yathā vāsudevenāyamabhigraho jagṛhe-yadi madīyaḥ putro'pi pravrajyāṃ jighṛkṣati tato'haṃ na nivārayāmītyādi, evaṃ ca śrutvā śivadevo'pyabhigrahaṃ gṛhītavān-yadi bhagavan ! madīyo'pi ko'pi pravrajyāṃ pratipadyate tato'haṃ na nivārayāmīti, atrāntare ca śivadevasya tanayo jyeṣṭhaḥ sā ca sādhubhaginī sammatiḥ pravajyāṃ grahītumupatasthe, śreṣṭhinā ca tau dvāvapi visarjitau, tataḥ pravrajyāṃ pratipannāviti | 38. vairadhyāne śrīupadeśamālāheyopādeyāyāṃ paraśurāmasubhumayoḥ vṛttāntaḥ |
gajapure'nantavīryarājabhāryāyā bhaginī reṇukābhidhānā pariṇītā jamadagnitāpasena | sā anyadā''yātā gajapure bhaginīsamīpe rājñā ca samutpāditastasyāstanayaḥ, nītā ṛṣiṇā | tasyāśca vidyādharadattaparaśuvidyo rāmaḥ jyeṣṭatanaya āsīt | tena kulakalaṅkabhūteyamiti jātakrodhena vyāpāditā sā sasutā | tat śrutvā anantavīryeṇāgatya ṛṣerāśramo vināśitaḥ, rāmeṇāpi tacchiraśchinnaṃ paraśunā, jāto'nantavīryasutaḥ kārtavīryo rājā tenākarṇya vṛttāntaṃ jātamatsareṇāgatya hato yamadagniḥ, rāmeṇāpyasāviti | tadbhāryā sutārā''pannagarbhā bhayātprapalāyamānā gatā tāpasāśramam | tatra ca vihvalatvātpatito niṣpannadārako bhūmiṃ gṛhṇannāsyena | kṛtaṃ taddvāreṇa tasya nāma subhūma iti | rāmeṇāpi gatvādhiṣṭhitaṃ tadrājyaṃ, krodhavaśāt kṛtā saptakṛtvo niḥkṣatriyā vasudhā, bhṛtaṃ pradhānakṣatriyadaṃṣṭrāṇāṃ sthālam | anyadā pṛṣṭastena naimittikaḥ kuto me maraṇamiti | sa prāha-yatsannidhānādetāḥ sthālasthitā daṃṣṭrāḥ pāyasībhaviṣyanti tata iti | tatastadupalambhārthaṃ kāritaṃ tena siṃhāsanatasyāgrato niveśya sthālamavāritasatram | itaśca naimittikasūcitasutāvaratvānmeghanādavidyādhareṇa sevyamāno gato vṛddhiṃ subhūmaḥ | sa mātaramapṛcchat kimiyāneva loka iti ? tat śrutvā vaco ruditaṃ tayā, so'vocad-amba kimetat ? tayā kathito vṛttāntaḥ | tato'sāvabhimānena gato gajapuraṃ, praviṣṭaḥ satramaṇḍapam, adhiṣṭhitaṃ siṃhāsanaṃ, pāyasībhūtā daṃṣṭrā bhakṣayitumārabdhāḥ, śrutaṃ tadrāmeṇa, samāgatastatra sa sabalaḥ, tataḥ subhūmapuṇyānubhāvādyaḥ prāk kṣatriyasannidhānena jajvāla sa vidhyātaḥ paraśuḥ, praharadvalaṃ bhagnaṃ meghanādena, paraśurāmaṃ pratyupasthito bhuktvā gṛhītasthālaḥ subhūmaḥ, kṛtaṃ taddevatayā taccakram tatastena taṃ nipātya rājyamadhiṣṭhāyaikaviṃśativārānnirbrāhmaṇīkṛtya pṛthivīṃ gataḥ saptamapṛthivīmiti ||150 ||

38. vairadhyāne śrīupadeśaprāsāde kṛṣīvaladṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ekadā jaṃgamaḥ kalpataruriva mārge vihāraṃ kurvanmahāvīrajino gautamaṃ pratyuvāca-“he vatsa ! yo'yaṃ kṛṣīvalaḥ purastāta prekṣyate, tasya pratibodhāya tvaṃ satvaraṃ tatra gaccha, tvatto mahānasya lābho bhāvī" | tatheti pratipadya gautamastatrāgatyetyalapat-“he bhadra ! tava samādhirvartate ? tvaṃ kasmādanekadvīndriyādijīvānāṃ (virādhanāmayena) kṛṣikarmaṇā mudhā pāpāni karoṣi ? kathaṃ pāpakuṭumbārthamātmā'narthe nipātyate ? | yataḥ
saṃsāramāvanna parassa aṭṭhā, sāhāraṇaṃ jaṃ ca karei kammaṃ |
kammassa te tassa u veyakāle, na baṃdhavābaṃdhavayaṃ uviṃti ||1|| tasmāttapasyāpotamādāya bhavodadheḥ samuttara iti sadvākyapīyūṣaiḥ siktastaṃ jagau-“svāmin ! ahaṃ vipraḥ saptakanyakādīnāṃ duṣpūrodayapūrtyarthamanekapāpakarmāṇi karomi | ataḥ paraṃ tvameva mama bhrātā mātā cāsi | yadādiśasi tatkurve, nānyathā pūjyavacaḥ kariṣye" | tataḥ so'pi tadarpitaṃ sādhuveṣaṃ svīcakre | gṛhītavrataṃ taṃ sahādāya gautamo jinābhimukhaṃ cacāla | tadā so'vadat-'pūjya ! kutra gamyate ? gautamo'vak-'yatra me guravaḥ santi tatra' | so'vādīt-'surāsurapūjyānāṃ bhavatāmapi pūjyāste tu kīdṛśā bhaviṣyanti ?' | tadā tasyāgre'rhadguṇāḥ proktāḥ | tacchrutvā tena samyaktvamarjitaṃ, punarjinasamṛddhyavalokanādviśeṣeṇa prāptam | tato krameṇa saparicchadaṃ śrīvīraṃ yāvadadrākṣīttāvattasya manasi dveṣaḥ samabhūt | gautamaḥ prāha-'bho mune ! śrīvīraṃ vandasva' | so'pi gaṇadharaṃ pratyāha"bhavadīyo'yaṃ guruścettadā mama pravrajyāyāḥ prayojanaṃ nāsti, tava śiṣyatvabhavanenālaṃ, imaṃ veṣaṃ gṛhāṇa, ahaṃ svamandire yāsyāmi" | ityuktvā yadā tyaktaveṣo'sau muṣṭiṃ baddhvā praṇaṣṭavān, tadā tāddak tasya ceṣṭitaṃ vīkṣya sarve'pīndrādyā hasantaḥ prāhu:-'aho ! indrabhūtinā varīyān śiṣya upārjitaḥ | etadadbhutaṃ vīkṣya manāg lajjitamanā gautamo vīraṃ tadvairakāraṇaṃ papraccha | svāmī taṃ pratyāha-“he vatsa ! anenārhadguṇacintanairgranthibhedaḥ kṛtaḥ | tena tasya tava ca mahān lābho'bhūt | yanmāṃ vīkṣyāsya dveṣo jātastatsvarūpaṃ śṛṇu___purā potanapure prajāpatinṛpāṅgajastripṛṣṭhākhyo vāsudevo'hamabhavam, tadā trikhaṇḍādhipaḥ pratyardhacakrī aśvagrīvāhvo'bhūt | anyadā svasaṃsadi tena svanimīlanaṃ nimittajñaṃ prati pṛṣṭam | so'pi tripṛṣṭhahastāt tvanmṛtyurbhāvītyabravīt | tato'nvahaṃ sa tripṛṣṭhasyopari dveṣaṃ vahan māraṇopāyāṃścakre | te tu viphalā jajñire | anyadā tatpuro vane śālikṣetramanuṣyeṣūpadravaṃ kurvantaṃ siṃhaṃ hantuṃ na kenāpi samarthyate, tadā tadupadravanivāraṇāyārdhacakrinṛpājñayā svasvavārake nṛpāḥ sthitiṃ kurvanti | anyeyuḥ

prajāpatinṛpasya vārako'bhavat | tadā pitaraṃ niṣidhya tatra tripṛṣṭhastadupadravaṃ rakṣituṃ sārathiyuto rathamāruhya yayau | tenālāpitamātro'sau siṃhastaṃ pratyadhāvat | tadā tripṛṣṭhaḥ śuktisaṃpuṭavattasyauṣṭhayāmalaṃ dvidhā kṛtvā siṃhamardhamṛtaṃ cakre | tataḥ siṃhaḥ svaṃ nininda-'hahā ! nṛmātreṇāhaṃ hataḥ' | tadā madhuravācā sārathistamasāntvayat-“he siṃha ! vāsudevo'yaṃ bhavitā | raṃkamātramimaṃ mā jānīhi | puruṣendrasya hastena mṛtaścet kiṃ viṣīdasi ? | martyaloke'yameva siṃho'sti, bhavān punastiryagyonau” | iti vākyena hṛṣṭaḥ siṃhaḥ samādhinā nimīlanaṃ prāptaḥ | tataste trayo'pi bhavāmbhodhau bhrāmaṃ bhrāmaṃ kramādatrābhavan | yastripṛṣṭhajīvaḥ so'haṃ jātaḥ, siṃhajīvastu kṛṣīvalo jātaḥ, sārathe - vastu bhavānindrabhūtirjātaḥ | purā madhurayā vācā tvayāsau prīṇito mayā ca hataḥ | tadatra bhavanāṭake snehavairayoḥ kāraṇaṃ jñeyam | paraṃ tvayaṃ śuklapākṣiko jātaḥ | 38. vairadhyāne śrīupadeśaprāsāde sudarśanaprabandhaḥ |
campāyāmṛṣabhadāsaḥ śreṣṭhī | tasyārhadāsī strī suśīlābhavat | anyedhurmāghamāse subhagākhyastanmahiṣīpāla: śreṣṭhīmahiṣīścārayitvā gṛhamāgacchan sāyamapāvṛtaṃ pratimāsthaṃ śītārtaṃ muniṃ pathi dṛṣṭvā tasya ślāghāṃ vidhāya gṛhamāgatya niśāmativāhya savelamutthāya mahiṣīḥ puraskṛtya gacchan tathāsthaṃ muni vīkṣya tatpārśve niṣaṇaḥ | ita udite ravau sa cāraṇamuniḥ 'namo arihaṃtāṇaṃ' ityuktvā divamutpatitaḥ | tatastena tatpadaṃ vyomagāmividyāmaṃtramiva matvā citte nyastaṃ | tadeva so'rhatsannidhau anyedyuḥ paṭhati sma | taddhyānaparaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā śreṣṭhī papraccha-'kuta idaṃ tvayā prāptaṃ ?' tenoktaṃ-'muneḥ' ityukte sarvavṛttānte ca tuṣṭaḥ śreṣṭhī taṃ saṃpūrṇaṃ namaskāramapāṭhayat |
itastadguṇanaṃ kurvatastasya kramādvarṣākāla: sametaḥ | tato meghenaikārṇave mahīmaṃḍale kṛte sa mahiṣīrlātvā vanaṃ gataḥ | antarāle nadīpūramāgatā | atha tena vyomavidyābuddhyā tadeva smaratā nadyāṃ jhaṃpā dattā | antarāle sa kīlakaviddho mṛtvā tasyaiva śreṣṭhinaḥ sudarśanākhyaḥ suto jātaḥ | kramāt pitṛbhyāṃ manoramāmibhyasutāṃ vivāhitaḥ | itastasya kapilena nṛpapurodhasā samaṃ niviḍā prītirabhūt | anyadā patyuktadguṇaśravaṇānuraktā tatpatnī kapilā kāmāturā sudarśanasaudhe sametya 'tavādya suhṛdo vapurapāṭavamastyatastvayā sukhapṛcchārthaṃ śīghraṃ madgṛhe sametavyaṃ' ityuktvā taṃ guptagṛhāntītvā dvāraṃ datvā trapāṃ tyaktvā ratārthaṃ prārthayāmāsa | tataḥ parastrīratau ṣaṃḍhaḥ sa śreṣṭhī śīlarakṣārthaṃ 'paṃḍho'hamasmi mugdhe, kiṃ vṛthā māṃ prārthayasi ?' ityuktvā nirgatya gṛhamāgataḥ || __ anyadā nṛpaḥ sapurodhaḥsudarśanaḥ krīḍitumudyānaṃ gataḥ | yānārūḍhābhayā rājyapi kapilayā samaṃ vanamagāt | itaḥ kapilā sudarśanapriyāṃ sutaṣaṭkayutāṃ pathi dṛṣṭvā ‘keyaṃ strī ?' ityabhayāmapṛcchat |

tayoktaṃ-'iyaṃ śreṣṭhistrī, ete tatsutāḥ' | tataḥ kapilayā tadvṛttānto mūlataḥ proktaḥ | atha sā devyā proce-tvaṃ mugdhā'nena daṃbhena vañcitā' | tatastayoktaṃ-'devi ! tavāpi vaidagdhyaṃ tadā vedmi, cedanena samaṃ ramase' | iti tadvacaḥ śrutvā devyāṅgīkṛtam ||
ekadā rājādau vane raṃtuṃ gate śūnyagṛhe kāyotsargasthaṃ kāmayakṣamūrtidaṃbhena yānārūḍhaṃ kārayitvā paṃḍitākhyayā svadhātryānāyya svabhuvanāntaḥ pracchannaṃ kṣiptvā sā kāmavibhramādidarśanapūrvamatyarthaṃ prārthitavatī | tathāpi tanmano na manāgapi calitam | tatastayā stanopapīḍaṃ tasya sarvāgīṇamāliṃganaṃ dattam | tathāpi na kṣubdhaṃ cetaḥ | tataḥ kupitayā tayā pūñcakre | tacchrutvārakṣakaiḥ sa dhṛtvā rājño'ne nītaḥ | pṛṣṭenāpi tena tasyāṃ kṛpayā maunamāśritam | tato doṣaṃ saṃbhāvya ruṣṭena rājñā 'eṣa viḍambya pure bhrāmayitvā māryatāṃ' iti rakṣakā ādiṣṭāḥ | taistathā kṛtvā nīyamānastatpriyayā dṛṣṭaḥ | tadā sā jināgre kāyotsarga cakāra kalaṃkottaraṇaṃ yāvat | itaste taṃ śūlikāyāṃ nyadhuḥ | sā ca svarṇasiṃhāsanamabhūt | atha taistadvadhārthaṃ khaḍgaprahārā muktāste ca kaṃṭhe hāratvaṃ, maulau maulitāṃ, karṇayoḥ kuṃḍalatvaṃ, karacaraṇe ca kaṭakatvaṃ bhejire | ārakṣakaistañcitraṃ rājñe jñāpitam | tato rājñā tatrāgatya śreṣṭhī satkṛtya svahastinamāropya samahotsavaṃ svagṛhe nītaḥ | tajjñātaṃ jñātvā tayā kāyotsargo muktaḥ | atha rājā tanmukhādrājñīvṛttaṃ jñātvā śreṣṭhivacanādabhayāyā abhayaṃ datvā śreṣṭhinaṃ hastiskandhamāropya svagṛhe preṣīt | tadvṛttaṃ vijñāyābhayā svamudvadhya mṛtā | paṃḍitā pāṭalīpure veśyāntike'gamat |
atha sudarśano bhavavirakto gṛhītavrato vicaran pāṭalīpure gataḥ | tatra paṃḍitayā pratilābhamiṣeṇa svagṛhe nītvā dvāraṃ pidhāya kadarthito'pi nācalat | tataḥ sāyaṃ vimukto nirgatya vane gatvā smaśānāntaḥ pratimayā sthitaḥ | tatrāpi vyantarībhūtayā tayābhayārājyā prāgvairādaneke upasargāḥ kṛtāḥ | tathāpi tanmano na calitam | sa muniḥ śubhadhyānāt kevalajñānamāsādya deśanāṃ dadau | tadā'bhayāpi samyaktvaṃ prāpa | paṃḍitāpi pratibodhitā | evaṃ kevalaparyāyaṃ ciraṃ prapālya śivaṃ gataḥ || 39. vitarkadhyāne śrīupadeśaprāsāde cāṇakyasya jñātam | __ golladeśe jainaścaṇakanāmā vipro'bhūt | tasya bhāryā caṇeśvarī | tayoḥ sutaścāṇākyaḥ garbhāddaśanaiḥ saha jātaḥ | anyadā tadgṛhe jñānī munirāhārārthamāgāt | dampatī muniṃ natvā papracchatuḥ-“he bhagavan ! asau sadanto jāto'sti tasya ko hetuḥ ?" | muniḥ smāha-“bālo'yaṃ nṛpo bhavitā" | tacchrutvā caṇako viṣaṇo dadhyau-“matsuto rājyārambhairadhogatiṃ mā yāsīt” iti dhvātvā dantān ghṛṣṭvā sādhubhyaḥ so'vak| sādhuḥ smāha-"tarhi saciveśo bhāvī |" tataścāṇākyaḥ krameṇa sakalakalāsamudro'bhūt | yauvane viprakanyāṃ paryaṇaiṣīt | nirdhano'pi hi saṃtoṣato dravyārthaṃ mahāprayatnaṃ nākarot | anyadā tatpriyā

bhrāturvivāhe pitṛgṛhe'gāt, paraṃ nirdhanatvena bhrātṛjāyādayo'syāḥ pratipattiṃ na cakrire, bhojanādāvapi paṅiktabhedaṃ vyadhuḥ, tato'tidauḥsthyālajjitā patyurgṛhe'gāt | tataḥ śokabharāṃ tāṃ vīkṣya bharnā sanirbandhaṃ pṛṣṭā muktāvyūhopamamaśruvrajaṃ muñcantī smāha taṃ parābhavam | tacchrutvā cāṇakyo dadhyau
kalāvān kulavān dātā, yaśasvī rūpavānapi | vinā śriyaṃ bhavenmayoṃ, nistejāḥ kṣīṇacandravat ||1|| nandabhūpo viprāṇāṃ dhanaṃ bahulaṃ dadāti iti vimṛśya sa pāṭalipuraṃ drāg jagāma | tatra rājasabhāyāṃ gatvā nṛpāsanamaśiśriyat | atha nandanṛpa ekena siddhaputreṇa nimittajñena saṃyuta upāgamat | tatrasthaṃ cāṇākyaṃ vīkṣya siddhaputro'bravīt-“asau vipro nandavaṃśasya cchāyāmākramya tiṣṭhati" | tataścāṇākyamurvīśadāsyuvāca-"bhagavan ! idaṃ dvitīyaṃ siṃhaviṣṭaramadhyāssva" | so'vak-"asminnāsane matkamaṇḍaluḥ sthāsyati" | ityutvā tatra taṃ nyasyādyamāsanaṃ na tyaktavān | tatastṛtīyaṃ daṇḍena caturthaṃ cākṣamālayā pañcamaṃ brahmasūtreṇa so'ruṇat ! tato dāsī jagau-"aho asya dhāṣṭyaṃ jaḍatvaṃ ca” | sa ruṣṭo'hiṇā tāmāhatya sarvasamakṣamityuvāca
kośaiśca bhṛtyaiśca nibaddhamūlaṃ, putraiśca mitraiśca vivṛddhaśākham |
utpāṭya nandaṃ parivartayāmi, mahādrumaṃ vāyurivogravegaḥ ||1|| anena bhikṣuṇā kiṃ syāditi rājñā'pyupekṣitaḥ sa niragāt, bhramaṃśca nandabhūpatermayūrapoṣakagrāme'gāt | tatra parivrājakaveṣeṇābhramat | tatra grāmaṇīputryāścandrapānadohado'jani, taṃ pūrayituṃ ko'pi nāśakat | tataḥ pitrādibhiścāṇākyaḥ proktaḥ, so'vak-“tatsutaṃ mama datta cettadāhaṃ pūrayāmi" | "apūrṇadohadāgarbhānvitā mā mriyatām" iti vimṛśya taistasya vacanaṃ svīkṛtam | atha sa sacchidraṃ paṭamaṇḍapamacīkarata, tasyopari pracchannaṃ śanaiḥ śanaiśchidrapidhāyakaṃ naramamuñcat, tasya cchidrasyādhastāt sthālaṃ payobhṛtaṃ nyadhāt, niśīthe kārtikīcandrastatra pratibimbitaḥ, cāndraṃ pratibimbamantarvantyāḥ pradī sa pibetyūce, tatastuṣṭā sā tatpātuṃ pracakrame, candrapānadhiyā sthālapayaḥ sā yathā yathā'pāt tathā tathoparistho naro maṇmapacchidraṃ pidadhe | tato dohade pūrṇe sā samaye sutaṃ suṣuve | cāṇākyastu dravyārjanaṃ prārebhe | tasya putrasya candragupta iti nāma kṛtam | tataḥ prāptaprauḍhavayāḥ sa bāla: śiśubhiḥ saha bhūpalīlayā krīḍati, deśapuragokulahayagajāmātyādīn sthāpayati | tatra bhraman cāṇakya etya prāha-"he bāla! mamāpi kiñciddehi" | candro'vadat-"imāḥ surabhīrgṛhāṇa" | sa smāha-"etā gṛhṇan bibhemi" | candro'vak-"mā bhaiṣīḥ, vīrabhogyā bhūriyaṃ" | tato hṛṣṭaḥ sa tasya kulādikamanyebhyo jñātvā svakīyaṃ bālaṃ nirdhāryatyūce-“he vatsa ! ehi rājyaṃ dadāmi" | tacchrutvā drutamāyātaṃ taṃ hatvā drāk palāyiṣṭa| tataḥ senāṃ kṛtvā pāṭalipuraṃ ruddham | nandena tatsainyaṃ kṣaṇena jitam | candraguptaścāṇākyena samaṃ
